```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_target_cannot_be_replaced_mid_delivery() {
    let (r, rid) = keys_rig("claude-idle-detection.txt", "claude-idle-prompt.txt").await;
    let gate = r.fake.block_next("agent.read");
    let pending = tokio::spawn({
        let core = r.core.clone();
        let rid = rid.clone();
        async move {
            core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
                id: "a1".into(), request_id: rid, revision: None,
                option_id: Some("allow".into()), text: None,
            }).await
        }
    });
    gate.arrived().await;
    let old = r.adapter.keys_targets().get(&rid).unwrap();
    let replaced = KeysTarget::from_screen(
        rid.clone(), old.agent_id().to_string(), old.key().clone(), &redrawn_dialog(), allow_deny(),
    ).unwrap();
    assert!(r.adapter.keys_targets().set(replaced).is_err());
    gate.release();
    let res = pending.await.unwrap();
    assert!(delivered(&res), "{res:?}");
    assert_eq!(r.fake.keys_sent().len(), 1, "the original target was used");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_held_agent_lock_delays_the_key() {
    let (r, rid) = keys_rig("claude-idle-detection.txt", "claude-idle-prompt.txt").await;
    let agent_id = r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().agent_id;
    let guard = r.adapter.agent_locks().lock(&agent_id).await;
    let pending = tokio::spawn({
        let core = r.core.clone();
        let rid = rid.clone();
        async move {
            core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
                id: "a1".into(), request_id: rid, revision: None,
                option_id: Some("allow".into()), text: None,
            }).await
        }
    });
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert!(r.fake.keys_sent().is_empty(), "no key while another path holds the agent");
    // The other path opens a different request meanwhile: the claim loses.
    let mut other = permission_obs(&agent_id, "k2");
    other.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
    r.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(other));
    drop(guard);
    let res = pending.await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(res.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestGone), "{res:?}");
    assert!(r.fake.keys_sent().is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_dialog_change_while_waiting_for_the_lock_sends_nothing() {
    // Sol B5: the guard read must happen after the lock, not before it.
    let (r, rid) = keys_rig("claude-idle-detection.txt", "claude-idle-prompt.txt").await;
    let agent_id = r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().agent_id;
    let guard = r.adapter.agent_locks().lock(&agent_id).await;
    let pending = tokio::spawn({
        let core = r.core.clone();
        let rid = rid.clone();
        async move {
            core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
                id: "a1".into(), request_id: rid, revision: None,
                option_id: Some("allow".into()), text: None,
            }).await
        }
    });
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    // The dialog changes while the engine waits for the lock.
    r.fake.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &redrawn_dialog());
    drop(guard);
    let res = pending.await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(res.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::ScreenMismatch), "{res:?}");
    assert!(r.fake.keys_sent().is_empty(), "zero keys");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn stray_keystroke_carries_its_reason() {
    let (r, rid) = keys_rig("claude-idle-detection.txt", "claude-idle-prompt-stray-1.txt").await;
    r.adapter.set_evidence(Arc::new(Fixed(Verdict::Consistent)));
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await;
    loop {
        if let Ok(ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, reason, .. }) = rx.recv().await
            && request_id == rid
        {
            assert_eq!((by, reason), (ResolvedBy::Uncertain, Some(ResolveReason::StrayKeystroke)));
            break;
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement set-once targets**

Replace the `KeysTargets` storage:

```rust
/// Retired request ids remembered so a removed target is never re-set.
const RETIRED_CAP: usize = 4096;

#[derive(Default)]
struct TargetsInner {
    live: HashMap<String, KeysTarget>,
    retired: std::collections::VecDeque<String>,
    retired_set: std::collections::HashSet<String>,
}

/// Registered targets, by request id. A target is immutable for its
/// request id (D8): the content the user reviewed is the content the
/// guard checks.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct KeysTargets(Mutex<TargetsInner>);

impl TargetsInner {
    fn retire(&mut self, request_id: &str) {
        if self.retired_set.insert(request_id.to_owned()) {
            self.retired.push_back(request_id.to_owned());
            if self.retired.len() > RETIRED_CAP
                && let Some(old) = self.retired.pop_front()
            {
                self.retired_set.remove(&old);
            }
        }
    }
}

impl KeysTarget {
    fn same_as(&self, o: &KeysTarget) -> bool {
        self.agent_id == o.agent_id
            && self.key == o.key
            && self.digest == o.digest
            && self.identity == o.identity
            && self.option_keys == o.option_keys
    }
}
```

and rewrite the methods over `TargetsInner` (`lock()` returns
`MutexGuard<'_, TargetsInner>`):

```rust
    pub fn set(&self, t: KeysTarget) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        check_keys(&t.option_keys)?;
        let mut inner = self.lock();
        if inner.retired_set.contains(&t.request_id) {
            return Err("this request's target was already retired");
        }
        match inner.live.get(&t.request_id) {
            Some(old) if old.same_as(&t) => Ok(()),
            Some(_) => Err("a different target is already registered for this request"),
            None => {
                inner.live.insert(t.request_id.clone(), t);
                Ok(())
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn get(&self, request_id: &str) -> Option<KeysTarget> {
        self.lock().live.get(request_id).cloned()
    }

    pub fn remove(&self, request_id: &str) -> Option<KeysTarget> {
        let mut inner = self.lock();
        inner.retire(request_id);
        inner.live.remove(request_id)
    }

    pub fn for_agent(&self, agent_id: &str) -> Vec<KeysTarget> {
        self.lock().live.values().filter(|t| t.agent_id == agent_id).cloned().collect()
    }

    pub(crate) fn all(&self) -> Vec<KeysTarget> {
        self.lock().live.values().cloned().collect()
    }

    pub(crate) fn remove_agent(&self, agent_id: &str) {
        let mut inner = self.lock();
        let ids: Vec<String> = inner.live.values().filter(|t| t.agent_id == agent_id).map(|t| t.request_id.clone()).collect();
        for id in ids {
            inner.retire(&id);
            inner.live.remove(&id);
        }
    }
```

Update the doc comment of `set` ("Registers or replaces…") to describe
the set-once rule.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the agent locks**

```rust
/// One async lock per agent (D7). The engine holds it from before its
/// final guard read until the key write completes, so waiting for the
/// lock never ages a screen check (Sol B5). P3 holds it around every
/// revalidation, observation and target registration on a herdr agent,
/// so a lease check can never pass on a state P3 is about to change.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct AgentLocks(Mutex<HashMap<String, Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<()>>>>);

impl AgentLocks {
    pub async fn lock(&self, agent_id: &str) -> tokio::sync::OwnedMutexGuard<()> {
        let m = {
            let mut map = self.0.lock().expect("agent locks");
            // An entry nobody holds or waits for is only a clone in the map.
            map.retain(|_, m| Arc::strong_count(m) > 1);
            map.entry(agent_id.to_owned()).or_default().clone()
        };
        m.lock_owned().await
    }

    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.0.lock().expect("agent locks").len()
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.len() == 0
    }
}
```

`Engine` gains `locks: Arc<AgentLocks>` (created in `Engine::new`) and
`pub(crate) fn locks(&self) -> Arc<AgentLocks>`. In `Engine::deliver`,
acquire the lock right after the pane lookup and **before** the guard
read, so the order is: lock, guard read, connect, lease, write, unlock:

```rust
        // Before the guard read: a wait for the lock must never sit
        // between the screen check and the key (Sol B5).
        let lock = match timeout_at(deadline, self.locks.lock(&request.agent_id)).await {
            Ok(g) => g,
            Err(_) => return Err(not_delivered(ErrorCode::SourceUnavailable)),
        };

        // Guard: the screen must show exactly the dialog the user saw.
        let read = pane.herdr.read_screen(&pane.pane_id, "detection", None, deadline).await
            .map_err(|_| not_delivered(ErrorCode::SourceUnavailable))?;
        if !target.guard_passes(&read) {
            return Err(not_delivered(ErrorCode::ScreenMismatch));
        }
```

(this replaces the existing guard-read block; the early returns drop
`lock`), and between `connect_until` and the write:

```rust

            drop(lock);
            drop(connected); // unused: nothing was sent
            return Err(not_delivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        }
```

```rust
        let (by, reason) = if seen.stray {
```

```rust
async fn a_pane_closed_and_redetected_is_a_new_agent() {
    let r = rig(&[("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")]).await;
    let id = agent(&r).id;
    r.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(permission_obs(&id, "k1")));
    let rid = r.core.request_id_for(&id, &CorrelationKey("k1".into())).unwrap();
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let mut info = fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "idle");
    info["terminal_id"] = "term_new".into();
    r.fake.set_agent(info);
    r.fake.push_event(serde_json::json!({"event": "pane_closed", "data": {"pane_id": "w1:p1"}}));
    r.fake.push_event(serde_json::json!({"event": "pane_agent_detected", "data": {"pane_id": "w1:p1"}}));
    let mut removed = false;
    loop {
        match rx.recv().await.unwrap() {
            ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } if request_id == rid => assert_eq!(by, ResolvedBy::Lost),
            ServerMessage::AgentRemoved { agent_id, .. } if agent_id == id => removed = true,
            ServerMessage::AgentUpdated { agent, .. } if agent.id == id && removed => {
                assert!(agent.pending.is_none());
                break;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_changed_session_is_a_new_agent_but_a_first_session_is_not() {
    let r = rig(&[("w1:p1", "claude", "idle")]).await;
    let id = agent(&r).id;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let mut info = fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "idle");
    info["agent_session"] = serde_json::json!({"value": "s-1"});
    r.fake.set_agent(info.clone());
    r.fake.push_event(serde_json::json!({"event": "pane_updated", "data": {"pane_id": "w1:p1"}}));
    until("session known", || r.adapter.panes().read().unwrap().get(&id).and_then(|p| p.session.clone())).await;
    info["agent_session"] = serde_json::json!({"value": "s-2"});
    r.fake.set_agent(info);
    r.fake.push_event(serde_json::json!({"event": "pane_updated", "data": {"pane_id": "w1:p1"}}));
    let mut removals = 0;
    while let Ok(Ok(m)) = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), rx.recv()).await {
        if matches!(m, ServerMessage::AgentRemoved { ref agent_id, .. } if *agent_id == id) {
            removals += 1;
        }
        if r.adapter.panes().read().unwrap().get(&id).and_then(|p| p.session.clone()).as_deref() == Some("s-2") { break; }
    }
    assert_eq!(removals, 1, "null -> s-1 is not a new agent; s-1 -> s-2 is");
}

    #[tokio::test]
```

```rust
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct PaneRef {
    pub herdr: Herdr,
    pub pane_id: String,
    pub kind: AgentKind,
    pub hints: mpsc::Sender<Hint>,
    /// The herdr socket this pane lives on.
    pub socket: std::path::PathBuf,

    /// Which agent incarnation this is (G1). A replacement under the same
    /// agent id gets a new value; targets and read tickets compare it.
    pub incarnation: u64,
}

/// Process-wide, so incarnations never repeat across sockets.
```

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct PaneIdentity {
    terminal_id: Option<String>,
    session: Option<String>,
    kind: AgentKind,
}

impl PaneIdentity {
    /// A different terminal, a different reported session, or a different
    /// agent kind means a new agent under the same pane id. A session that
    /// appears for the first time does not.
    fn replaced_by(&self, next: &PaneIdentity) -> bool {
        self.kind != next.kind
            || matches!((&self.terminal_id, &next.terminal_id), (Some(a), Some(b)) if a != b)
            || matches!((&self.session, &next.session), (Some(a), Some(b)) if a != b)
    }
}
```

Rewrite `apply`, factoring the old `None` branch into `forget_pane`:

```rust
    fn apply(&mut self, pane_id: &str, info: Option<AgentInfo>) {
        let agent_id = map::agent_id(&self.hash, pane_id);
        let Some((info, obs)) = info.and_then(|i| map::agent_obs(&self.hash, &i).map(|o| (i, o))) else {
            self.closed.remove(pane_id);
            self.identity.remove(pane_id);
            self.forget_pane(pane_id);
            return;
        };
        let next = PaneIdentity {
            terminal_id: info.terminal_id.clone(),
            session: info.agent_session.as_ref().and_then(|s| s.value.clone()),
            kind: obs.kind,
        };
        let closed = self.closed.remove(pane_id);
        let replaced = self.identity.get(pane_id).is_some_and(|old| old.replaced_by(&next));
        if self.known.contains_key(pane_id) && (closed || replaced) {
            self.forget_pane(pane_id);
        }
        self.identity.insert(pane_id.to_owned(), next.clone());
        let incarnation = match self.incarnations.get(pane_id) {
            Some(n) if self.known.contains_key(pane_id) => *n,
            _ => {
                let n = NEXT_INCARNATION.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                self.incarnations.insert(pane_id.to_owned(), n);
                n
            }
        };
        if self.known.insert(pane_id.to_owned(), obs.kind) != Some(obs.kind) {
            self.subs_changed = true;
        }
        if obs.state == AgentState::NeedsYou {
            self.blocked.insert(pane_id.to_owned());
        } else {
            self.blocked.remove(pane_id);
        }
        self.panes.write().expect("pane map").insert(
            agent_id,
            PaneRef {
                herdr: self.herdr.clone(),
                pane_id: pane_id.to_owned(),
                kind: obs.kind,
                hints: self.hints_tx.clone(),
                socket: self.herdr.path().to_path_buf(),
                session: next.session,
                cwd: info.foreground_cwd.clone().or_else(|| info.cwd.clone()),
                terminal_id: next.terminal_id,
                incarnation,
            },
        );
        self.fallback.on_state(pane_id, obs.state == AgentState::NeedsYou, Instant::now().into_std());
        self.core.observe(Observation::AgentUpsert(obs));
    }

    /// The pane's agent is gone: drop everything known about it.
    fn forget_pane(&mut self, pane_id: &str) {
        let agent_id = map::agent_id(&self.hash, pane_id);
        self.incarnations.remove(pane_id);
        self.fallback.forget(pane_id);
        self.fb_open.remove(pane_id);
        self.keys.remove_agent(&agent_id);
        if self.known.remove(pane_id).is_some() {
            self.subs_changed = true;
            self.blocked.remove(pane_id);
            self.panes.write().expect("pane map").remove(&agent_id);
            self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id });
        }
    }
```

A `closed` entry for a pane that is never re-fetched is cleared by the
next `apply` of that pane or by `lost()` (add `self.closed.clear()` and
keep `identity` there, since panes survive a reconnect).

- [ ] **Step 5: Watch ordering**

Replace the tail of `watch_targets` (from `self.watch_absent.remove`)
with a call to a free function, so the ordering is unit-testable:

```rust
            if retire_target(&self.core, &self.keys, &t.request_id, status) {
                self.watch_absent.remove(&t.request_id);
                self.mark_dirty(pane_id.to_owned());
            } else {
                // A claim landed after the status read. Its guard will see
                // the dialog gone and release the request; the next absent
                // read ends it.
                self.watch_absent.insert(t.request_id.clone(), ABSENT_READS - 1);
            }
```

```rust
/// The dialog of `request_id`'s target is confirmed gone. Ends an open
/// request `terminal` first, then removes the target, unless a claim got
/// in between (P1 follow-up: removing first left a claimed request with
/// no target and no watcher). Returns whether the target was removed.
pub fn retire_target(
    core: &Core,
    keys: &KeysTargets,
    request_id: &str,
    status_at_read: Option<RequestStatus>,
) -> bool {
    if status_at_read == Some(RequestStatus::Open) {
        core.observe(Observation::RequestEndedIfOpen {
            request_id: request_id.to_owned(),
            by: ResolvedBy::Terminal,
        });
    }
    if core.request(request_id).map(|r| r.status) == Some(RequestStatus::Answering) {
        return false;
    }
    keys.remove(request_id);
    core.observe(Observation::DialogGoneById { request_id: request_id.to_owned() });
    true
}
```

### Task 6: Keys targets bound to a pane incarnation

A target registered for one agent incarnation must never send a key to a
replacement pane that reuses the agent id (Read ticket contract G1;
Sol B2 and B5).

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/keys.rs`
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/herdr_adapter.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 (lock order), Task 5 (`PaneRef::incarnation`).
- Produces:
  - `KeysTarget::for_incarnation(self, incarnation: u64) -> KeysTarget`
    and `KeysTarget::incarnation(&self) -> Option<u64>`. P3 always binds
    its targets (Global Constraints); P2's own tests may leave it unset.
  - `Engine::deliver` refuses a bound target whose pane has another
    incarnation (`NotDelivered(RequestGone)`, zero keys), both after the
    pane lookup and in the lease right before the write.

```rust
async fn bound_keys_rig(incarnation_offset: u64) -> (Rig, String) {
    let (r, rid) = keys_rig("claude-idle-detection.txt", "claude-idle-prompt.txt").await;
    r.adapter.keys_targets().remove(&rid);
    // A fresh request (it supersedes the first): set-once forbids

    let id = agent(&r).id;
    let mut obs = permission_obs(&id, "k2");
    obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
    r.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
    let key = CorrelationKey("k2".into());
    let rid = r.core.request_id_for(&id, &key).unwrap();
    let incarnation = r.adapter.panes().read().unwrap()[&id].incarnation + incarnation_offset;
    let dialog = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt");
    r.adapter.keys_targets().set(
        KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.clone(), id, key, &dialog, allow_deny()).unwrap().for_incarnation(incarnation),
    ).unwrap();
    (r, rid)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `keys.rs`, `KeysTarget` gains `pub(crate) incarnation: Option<u64>`
(set to `None` in `from_screen`), compared in `same_as`, and:

```rust
    /// Binds the target to one pane incarnation (G1). A key is only ever
    /// sent while the pane map shows this incarnation for the agent.
    pub fn for_incarnation(mut self, incarnation: u64) -> KeysTarget {
        self.incarnation = Some(incarnation);
        self
    }

    pub fn incarnation(&self) -> Option<u64> {
        self.incarnation
    }

    fn incarnation_ok(&self, pane: &PaneRef) -> bool {
        self.incarnation.is_none_or(|n| n == pane.incarnation)
    }
```

In `Engine::deliver`, right after the pane lookup:

```rust
        if !target.incarnation_ok(&pane) {
            return Err(not_delivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        }
```

and extend `lease_held` to take the pane map and recheck it last, right
before the write:

```rust
    fn lease_held(&self, core: &Weak<Core>, panes: &PaneMap, target: &KeysTarget, option_id: &str) -> bool {
        let Some(core) = core.upgrade() else { return false };
        let claimed = core.request(&target.request_id).is_some_and(|r| {
            r.status == RequestStatus::Answering && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys
        });
        let same_pane = panes
            .read()
            .expect("pane map")
            .get(&target.agent_id)
            .is_some_and(|p| target.incarnation_ok(p));
        claimed
            && same_pane
            && self.targets.get(&target.request_id).is_some_and(|t| t.unchanged_since(target, option_id))
    }
```

and pass `panes` at its call site. Import `super::reconciler::PaneRef`.

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (later tasks use these exact names):
  - `wire::HookAgent { Claude, Codex }`, `HookAgent::parse(&str) -> Option<HookAgent>`.
  - `wire::HookName { SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, PermissionRequest, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, Stop, SessionEnd, Other }`,
    `HookName::parse(&str) -> HookName`.
  - `wire::Payload { session_id: String, prompt_id: Option<String>, turn_id: Option<String>, cwd: Option<String>, tool_name: Option<String>, tool_input: Value, tool_response: Value, permission_suggestions: Vec<Value>, last_assistant_message: Option<String>, agent_id: Option<String> }`,
    `Payload::from_value(&Value) -> Option<Payload>`, `Payload::is_subagent(&self) -> bool`.
  - `wire::Hints { herdr_pane: Option<String>, herdr_socket: Option<String>, entrypoint: Option<String> }`,
    `Hints::from_headers(&http::HeaderMap) -> Hints`, `wire::hint(Option<&str>) -> Option<String>`.
  - `wire::HookEvent { agent: HookAgent, name: HookName, payload: Payload, hints: Hints, at: tokio::time::Instant }`.
  - `wire::valid_id(&str) -> bool`, `wire::canonical_json(&Value) -> String`,
    `wire::tool_digest(&str, &Value) -> String`, `wire::command_of(&Value) -> Option<&str>`,
    `wire::claude_key(&Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey>`, `wire::codex_key(&Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey>`.
  - `wire::AlwaysRule { tool_name: String, rule_content: String }`,
    `wire::always_rule(p: &Payload) -> Option<AlwaysRule>`: the one rule
    construction used for both display and delivery (ruling Q2, Sol I7):
    a `Bash` request with a string `command` gives
    `{toolName: "Bash", ruleContent: <command>}`; otherwise the first
    `addRules` suggestion's first rule.
  - `mapping::rule_text(&AlwaysRule) -> String` (`Tool(content)`) and
    `mapping::rule_displayable(&str) -> bool` (no control, bidi or
    separator characters, at most 4000 chars): `allow_always` is offered
    only when the whole rule is displayable, never truncated.
  - `mapping::QuestionSpec { header: String, question: String, multi_select: bool, options: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> }`
    (raw strings) and `mapping::question_specs(input: &Value) -> Result<Vec<QuestionSpec>, &'static str>`:
    full structural validation (Sol question 2, D25). Tasks 13, 16 use
    the same specs for screen matching and evidence labels.
    `wire::reply_allow() -> String`, `reply_deny() -> String`, `reply_allow_always(&AlwaysRule) -> String`.
  - `mapping::detail_of(input: &Value) -> String` and `mapping::DETAIL_CHARS = 4000`.
  - `mapping::claude_permission(agent_id: &str, key: CorrelationKey, upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>, p: &Payload) -> RequestObs`.
  - `mapping::claude_keys_options() -> Vec<RequestOption>` and
    `mapping::claude_permission_keys(dialog: &herdr::screen::Dialog) -> HashMap<String, String>`.
  - `mapping::claude_question(agent_id: &str, key: CorrelationKey, upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>, p: &Payload, bound: bool) -> Option<RequestObs>`
    (`None` only when `tool_input` has no `questions` key; malformed
    input gives a read-only request),
    `mapping::question_keys(n: usize) -> HashMap<String, String>`.
  - `mapping::codex_permission(agent_id: &str, key: CorrelationKey, upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>, p: &Payload, collapsed: bool) -> RequestObs`,
    `mapping::CODEX_KEYS: [(&str, &str); 3]`.
  - `mapping::payload_for_log(raw: &[u8]) -> String` and
    `mapping::log_payload(agent: HookAgent, name: &HookName, raw: &[u8])`.

```rust
        assert_eq!((p.prompt_id, p.permission_suggestions.len()), (None, 0), "wrong types are absent, not fatal");

        assert_eq!(a.prompt_id, b.prompt_id, "prompt_id is per turn (D2)");

        assert_eq!(claude_key(&a), claude_key(&c), "key order does not matter");

        let req = payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        let post = payload("codex/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json");
        assert_ne!(tool_digest("Bash", &req.tool_input), tool_digest("Bash", &post.tool_input), "description is dropped (D3)");
        assert_eq!(command_of(&req.tool_input), command_of(&post.tool_input));
        assert!(codex_key(&req).unwrap().0.starts_with("codex:01a116d3-"));
        assert!(codex_key(&post).is_some());

        assert_eq!(v(reply_allow_always(&rule)), fx("claude/response-allow-always-localSettings.json"));
    }

        let p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");

        edit.permission_suggestions = vec![serde_json::json!({"type": "addRules",
            "rules": [{"toolName": "Edit", "ruleContent": "/x"}], "behavior": "allow", "destination": "session"})];
        assert_eq!(always_rule(&edit), Some(AlwaysRule { tool_name: "Edit".into(), rule_content: "/x".into() }));
    }

        assert_eq!(hint(Some("")), None, "an unlisted env var interpolates to \"\" (gate 2a)");
        assert_eq!(hint(Some(&"x".repeat(1025))), None);
        assert_eq!(hint(Some("w1:p2\t")), None);
        assert_eq!(hint(Some("w1:p2")).as_deref(), Some("w1:p2"));
    }

    #[test]

        assert_eq!(HookName::parse("PermissionRequest"), HookName::PermissionRequest);
        assert_eq!(HookName::parse("Notification"), HookName::Other);
        assert_eq!(HookAgent::parse("codex"), Some(HookAgent::Codex));
        assert_eq!(HookAgent::parse("aider"), None);
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::hooks::wire::Payload;

    fn payload(rel: &str) -> Payload {
        let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
        Payload::from_value(&serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()).unwrap()
    }
    fn screen(name: &str) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
    }
    fn k() -> CorrelationKey { CorrelationKey("k".into()) }

    #[test]
    fn claude_permission_is_a_held_hook_request() {
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"));
        assert_eq!((o.kind, o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (RequestKind::Permission, AnswerPath::Hook, true, true));
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Bash");
        assert_eq!(o.detail, "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt");
        let ids: Vec<_> = o.options.iter().map(|x| (x.id.as_str(), x.effect)).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, vec![("allow", Effect::Allow), ("allow_always", Effect::AllowAlways), ("deny", Effect::Deny)]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn allow_always_shows_the_exact_rule_it_will_write() {
        let p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        let always = o.options.iter().find(|x| x.effect == Effect::AllowAlways).unwrap();
        let d = always.description.as_deref().unwrap();
        let rule = crate::hooks::wire::always_rule(&p).unwrap();
        assert!(d.contains(&rule_text(&rule)), "{d}");
        assert_eq!(rule_text(&rule), "Bash(touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt)");
        assert!(d.contains(".claude/settings.local.json") && d.contains("outside"), "{d}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_rule_that_cannot_be_shown_whole_is_not_offered() {
        for cmd in ["x".repeat(5000), "echo \u{1b}[31m".to_string(), "a\nb".to_string(), "rm \u{202E}x".to_string()] {
            let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
            p.tool_input["command"] = cmd.clone().into();
            let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
            assert!(o.options.iter().all(|x| x.effect != Effect::AllowAlways), "{cmd:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn claude_keys_after_the_hold_drop_allow_always_and_use_the_no_option() {
        let dialog = crate::herdr::screen::find_dialog(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).unwrap();
        let keys = claude_permission_keys(&dialog);
        assert_eq!(keys.get("allow").map(String::as_str), Some("1"));
        assert_eq!(keys.get("deny").map(String::as_str), Some("4"), "option 4 is \"No\" in the fixture");
        assert_eq!(keys.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(claude_keys_options().len(), 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_question_is_answerable_only_when_bound() {
        let p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
        let bound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((bound.kind, bound.answer_path, bound.ready), (RequestKind::Question, AnswerPath::Keys, false));
        assert_eq!(bound.options.iter().map(|o| o.label.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["Red", "Blue"]);
        assert_eq!(bound.options[0].id, "o1");
        assert_eq!(bound.questions.as_ref().unwrap()[0].text, "Pick a color");
        assert!(!bound.free_text, "Type something and Chat about this are never options");
        let unbound = claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, false).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(unbound.answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
        assert_eq!(question_keys(2), [("o1".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("o2".to_string(), "2".to_string())].into());

    #[test]
    fn malformed_questions_are_read_only() {
        for broken in [

            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": 5}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "multiSelect": "no", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}]),
            serde_json::json!([{"question": "Q", "header": "H", "options": [{"label": "A"}]}, 7]),

            assert_eq!(o.answer_path, AnswerPath::None, "{broken}");
            assert!(!o.options.iter().any(|_| true), "no options from a malformed input");

    #[test]
    fn multi_select_or_many_questions_stay_read_only() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");

        let q = p.tool_input["questions"][0].clone();
        p.tool_input["questions"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push(q);
        assert_eq!(claude_question("a", k(), None, &p, true).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn codex_permission_uses_keys_and_shows_the_command_then_the_reason() {
        let o = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), false);
        assert_eq!((o.answer_path, o.ready, o.reusable_key), (AnswerPath::Keys, false, true));
        assert!(o.detail.starts_with("touch ~/acp0_test_1.txt\n"));
        assert!(o.detail.contains("outside the writable workspace"));
        assert_eq!(o.options.iter().map(|x| x.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["allow", "allow_always", "deny"]);
        let collapsed = codex_permission("a", k(), None, &payload("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), true);
        assert!(collapsed.detail.ends_with("Enlarge the pane to answer here."));
    }

    #[test]
    fn hostile_text_is_cleaned_and_capped() {
        let mut p = payload("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        p.tool_name = Some("Ba\u{1b}[31msh\u{202E}".into());
        p.tool_input["command"] = format!("rm -rf /\u{7}{}", "x".repeat(5000)).into();
        let o = claude_permission("a", k(), None, &p);
        assert_eq!(o.title, "Ba[31msh");
        assert!(o.detail.chars().count() <= DETAIL_CHARS && !o.detail.contains('\u{7}'));
    }

    #[test]
    fn logged_payloads_are_redacted() {
        let s = payload_for_log(br#"{"tool_input":{"command":"GITHUB_TOKEN=ghp_secret make"}}"#);
        assert!(!s.contains("ghp_secret"), "{s}");
    }
}
```

```rust
//! Hook payloads, names and replies (spec §6.1 to §6.3). Payloads are read
//! field by field: a field of the wrong type is absent, never fatal, so a
//! future Claude or Codex version fails open instead of breaking acd.
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

pub const MAX_ID_BYTES: usize = 128;
pub const MAX_HINT_BYTES: usize = 1024;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum HookAgent {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

impl HookAgent {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<HookAgent> {
        match s {
            "claude" => Some(HookAgent::Claude),
            "codex" => Some(HookAgent::Codex),
            _ => None,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum HookName {
    SessionStart,
    UserPromptSubmit,
    PermissionRequest,
    PreToolUse,
    PostToolUse,
    Stop,
    SessionEnd,
    /// Notification and anything newer: logged and ignored.
    Other,
}

impl HookName {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> HookName {
        match s {
            "SessionStart" => HookName::SessionStart,
            "UserPromptSubmit" => HookName::UserPromptSubmit,
            "PermissionRequest" => HookName::PermissionRequest,
            "PreToolUse" => HookName::PreToolUse,
            "PostToolUse" => HookName::PostToolUse,
            "Stop" => HookName::Stop,
            "SessionEnd" => HookName::SessionEnd,
            _ => HookName::Other,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Payload {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub prompt_id: Option<String>,
    pub turn_id: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub tool_name: Option<String>,
    pub tool_input: Value,
    pub tool_response: Value,
    pub permission_suggestions: Vec<Value>,
    pub last_assistant_message: Option<String>,
    /// Present on subagent payloads, which are always dropped (spec §6.2).
    pub agent_id: Option<String>,
}

/// 1 to 128 bytes, no control characters.
pub fn valid_id(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_ID_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

impl Payload {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<Payload> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let id = |k: &str| s(k).filter(|x| valid_id(x));
        Some(Payload {
            session_id: id("session_id")?,
            prompt_id: id("prompt_id"),
            turn_id: id("turn_id"),
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            tool_name: s("tool_name"),
            tool_input: o.get("tool_input").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            tool_response: o.get("tool_response").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
            permission_suggestions: o
                .get("permission_suggestions")
                .and_then(Value::as_array)
                .cloned()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            last_assistant_message: s("last_assistant_message"),
            agent_id: s("agent_id"),
        })
    }

    pub fn is_subagent(&self) -> bool {
        self.agent_id.as_deref().is_some_and(|a| !a.is_empty())
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Hints {
    pub herdr_pane: Option<String>,
    pub herdr_socket: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
}

/// A header hint, or `None` when empty, too long or holding control chars.
pub fn hint(v: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    v.filter(|s| !s.is_empty() && s.len() <= MAX_HINT_BYTES && !s.chars().any(char::is_control))
        .map(str::to_owned)
}

impl Hints {
    pub fn from_headers(h: &axum::http::HeaderMap) -> Hints {
        let get = |name: &str| hint(h.get(name).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()));
        Hints {
            herdr_pane: get("x-herdr-pane"),
            herdr_socket: get("x-herdr-socket"),
            entrypoint: get("x-claude-entrypoint"),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HookEvent {
    pub agent: HookAgent,
    pub name: HookName,
    pub payload: Payload,
    pub hints: Hints,
    pub at: tokio::time::Instant,
}

/// Compact JSON with object keys sorted at every level.
pub fn canonical_json(v: &Value) -> String {
    fn write(v: &Value, out: &mut String) {
        match v {
            Value::Object(m) => {
                let mut keys: Vec<&String> = m.keys().collect();
                keys.sort();
                out.push('{');
                for (i, k) in keys.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    out.push_str(&Value::String((*k).clone()).to_string());
                    out.push(':');
                    write(&m[*k], out);
                }
                out.push('}');
            }
            Value::Array(a) => {
                out.push('[');
                for (i, x) in a.iter().enumerate() {
                    if i > 0 {
                        out.push(',');
                    }
                    write(x, out);
                }
                out.push(']');
            }
            other => out.push_str(&other.to_string()),
        }
    }
    let mut s = String::new();
    write(v, &mut s);
    s
}

/// Lowercase hex `sha256(tool_name ‖ 0x00 ‖ canonical_json(tool_input))`.
pub fn tool_digest(tool_name: &str, input: &Value) -> String {
    let mut h = Sha256::new();
    h.update(tool_name.as_bytes());
    h.update([0u8]);
    h.update(canonical_json(input).as_bytes());
    h.finalize().iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

pub fn command_of(input: &Value) -> Option<&str> {
    input.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str)
}

/// `claude:<session>:<prompt_id>:<digest>` (D2).
pub fn claude_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "claude:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.prompt_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

/// `codex:<session>:<turn_id>:<digest>` (spec §5.1).
pub fn codex_key(p: &Payload) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
    Some(CorrelationKey(format!(
        "codex:{}:{}:{}",
        p.session_id,
        p.turn_id.as_deref()?,
        tool_digest(p.tool_name.as_deref()?, &p.tool_input)
    )))
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct AlwaysRule {
    pub tool_name: String,
    pub rule_content: String,
}

/// The single rule construction used for display and delivery (ruling Q2):
/// a Bash request's exact command, else the first `addRules` suggestion.
pub fn always_rule(p: &Payload) -> Option<AlwaysRule> {
    if p.tool_name.as_deref() == Some("Bash")
        && let Some(c) = command_of(&p.tool_input)
    {
        return Some(AlwaysRule { tool_name: "Bash".into(), rule_content: c.to_owned() });
    }
    p.permission_suggestions.iter().find_map(|s| {
        if s.get("type")?.as_str()? != "addRules" {
            return None;
        }
        let r = s.get("rules")?.as_array()?.first()?;
        Some(AlwaysRule {
            tool_name: r.get("toolName")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
            rule_content: r.get("ruleContent")?.as_str()?.to_owned(),
        })
    })
}

fn envelope(decision: Value) -> String {
    json!({"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": "PermissionRequest", "decision": decision}}).to_string()
}

pub fn reply_allow() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "allow"}))
}

pub fn reply_deny() -> String {
    envelope(json!({"behavior": "deny", "message": "Denied from agent inbox"}))
}

/// Allow, plus the suggestion's rule persisted to `localSettings`, the only
/// destination P0 and gate 2e saw persist.
pub fn reply_allow_always(r: &AlwaysRule) -> String {
    envelope(json!({
        "behavior": "allow",
        "updatedPermissions": [{
            "type": "addRules",
            "rules": [{"toolName": r.tool_name, "ruleContent": r.rule_content}],
            "behavior": "allow",
            "destination": "localSettings"
        }]
    }))
}
```

```rust
//! Hook payloads to registry observations (spec §6.2, §6.3).
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_core::{CorrelationKey, RequestObs};
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, Effect, Question, RequestKind, RequestOption};
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{AlwaysRule, HookAgent, HookName, Payload, always_rule, canonical_json, command_of};
use crate::herdr::screen::Dialog;
use crate::text::{TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display, clean_screen, redact_log};

pub const DETAIL_CHARS: usize = 4000;
const DESCRIPTION_CHARS: usize = 400;
pub const CODEX_KEYS: [(&str, &str); 3] = [("allow", "1"), ("allow_always", "2"), ("deny", "esc")];

fn opt(id: &str, label: &str, effect: Effect, description: Option<String>) -> RequestOption {
    RequestOption { id: id.into(), label: label.into(), description, effect }
}

fn detail(raw: &str) -> String {
    cap_chars(clean_screen(raw).trim(), DETAIL_CHARS)
}

/// The command, the file path, or compact JSON (spec §6.2 step 1).
pub fn detail_of(input: &Value) -> String {
    if let Some(c) = command_of(input) {
        return detail(c);
    }
    if let Some(f) = input.get("file_path").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        return detail(f);
    }
    detail(&canonical_json(input))
}

pub fn claude_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut options = vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)];
    // The same `AlwaysRule` is stored with the hold and written by
    // `deliver` (Task 14), so what the client shows is what persists.
    if let Some(rule) = always_rule(p)
        && rule_displayable(&rule_text(&rule))
    {
        options.insert(
            1,
            opt(
                "allow_always",
                "Yes, always",
                Effect::AllowAlways,
                Some(format!(
                    "Adds the rule {} to .claude/settings.local.json in this project. Paths outside this directory may still prompt.",
                    rule_text(&rule)
                )),
            ),
        );
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Hook,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail: detail_of(&p.tool_input),
        options,
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: true,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

/// Options of a Claude permission request on the keys path (D9).
pub fn claude_keys_options() -> Vec<RequestOption> {
    vec![opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None), opt("deny", "No", Effect::Deny, None)]
}

/// `allow` is option 1; `deny` is the option labelled `No`, else `esc`.
pub fn claude_permission_keys(dialog: &Dialog) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    let no = dialog
        .options
        .iter()
        .find(|o| o.label.trim() == "No")
        .map(|o| o.number.to_string())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "esc".into());
    [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), no)].into()
}

pub fn rule_text(r: &AlwaysRule) -> String {
    format!("{}({})", r.tool_name, r.rule_content)
}

/// Shown whole or not offered at all: no control, bidi or separator
/// characters (they would be stripped or reorder the text), at most 4000
/// chars (the detail cap).
pub fn rule_displayable(text: &str) -> bool {
    text.chars().count() <= DETAIL_CHARS && clean_display(text, usize::MAX) == text
}

/// One question exactly as Claude sent it (raw strings, not cleaned).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct QuestionSpec {
    pub header: String,
    pub question: String,
    pub multi_select: bool,
    /// `(label, description)` in order.
    pub options: Vec<(String, Option<String>)>,
}

/// Full structural validation of `tool_input.questions` (D25): a
/// non-empty array of objects, each with a non-empty string `question`,
/// a string `header`, an optional boolean `multiSelect`, and a non-empty
/// `options` array of objects with a non-empty string `label` and an
/// optional string `description`. Any violation rejects the whole input.
pub fn question_specs(input: &Value) -> Result<Vec<QuestionSpec>, &'static str> {
    let qs = input.get("questions").and_then(Value::as_array).ok_or("questions is not an array")?;
    if qs.is_empty() {
        return Err("no questions");
    }
    qs.iter()
        .map(|q| {
            let q = q.as_object().ok_or("a question is not an object")?;
            let question = q.get("question").and_then(Value::as_str).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).ok_or("question text")?;
            let header = q.get("header").and_then(Value::as_str).ok_or("header")?;
            let multi_select = match q.get("multiSelect") {
                None => false,
                Some(v) => v.as_bool().ok_or("multiSelect")?,
            };
            let opts = q.get("options").and_then(Value::as_array).filter(|a| !a.is_empty()).ok_or("options")?;
            let options = opts
                .iter()
                .map(|o| {
                    let o = o.as_object().ok_or("an option is not an object")?;
                    let label = o.get("label").and_then(Value::as_str).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()).ok_or("label")?;
                    let description = match o.get("description") {
                        None => None,
                        Some(d) => Some(d.as_str().ok_or("description")?.to_owned()),
                    };
                    Ok((label.to_owned(), description))
                })
                .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, &'static str>>()?;
            Ok(QuestionSpec { header: header.to_owned(), question: question.to_owned(), multi_select, options })
        })
        .collect()
}

pub fn question_keys(n: usize) -> HashMap<String, String> {
    (1..=n.min(9)).map(|i| (format!("o{i}"), i.to_string())).collect()
}

/// `bound`: the session is bound to a herdr pane. Only a single,
/// single-select question with 1 to 9 options on a bound session is
/// answerable (spec §6.2 Questions); its path is `keys`, not ready until
/// the dialog is captured.
pub fn claude_question(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    bound: bool,
) -> Option<RequestObs> {
    p.tool_input.get("questions")?;
    let Ok(specs) = question_specs(&p.tool_input) else {
        // D25: shown, never answerable, no options derived from bad input.
        return Some(RequestObs {
            agent_id: agent_id.into(),
            key,
            upgrade_from,
            kind: RequestKind::Question,
            answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
            title: "Question".into(),
            detail: "The question could not be read. Answer it in the terminal.".into(),
            options: vec![],
            questions: None,
            free_text: false,
            raw_screen: None,
            ready: false,
            reusable_key: true,
        });
    };
    let questions: Vec<Question> = specs
        .iter()
        .enumerate()
        .map(|(qi, q)| Question {
            id: format!("q{}", qi + 1),
            header: Some(clean_display(&q.header, TITLE_CHARS)),
            text: detail(&q.question),
            multi_select: q.multi_select,
            options: q
                .options
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(i, (label, d))| {
                    opt(&format!("o{}", i + 1), &clean_display(label, TITLE_CHARS), Effect::Choose,
                        d.as_deref().map(|d| clean_display(d, DESCRIPTION_CHARS)))
                })
                .collect(),
        })
        .collect();
    let first = questions.first()?.clone();
    let answerable = bound && questions.len() == 1 && !first.multi_select && (1..=9).contains(&first.options.len());
    Some(RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Question,
        answer_path: if answerable { AnswerPath::Keys } else { AnswerPath::None },
        title: first.header.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "Question".into()),
        detail: first.text.clone(),
        options: first.options.clone(),
        questions: Some(questions),
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    })
}

/// spec §6.3 `PermissionRequest`. `collapsed`: the pane is too short and
/// the dialog hides the command, so it can never be matched.
pub fn codex_permission(
    agent_id: &str,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    upgrade_from: Option<CorrelationKey>,
    p: &Payload,
    collapsed: bool,
) -> RequestObs {
    let mut text = command_of(&p.tool_input).unwrap_or_default().to_owned();
    if let Some(d) = p.tool_input.get("description").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        text.push('\n');
        text.push_str(d);
    }
    let mut detail = detail(&text);
    if collapsed {
        detail.push_str("\n\nEnlarge the pane to answer here.");
    }
    RequestObs {
        agent_id: agent_id.into(),
        key,
        upgrade_from,
        kind: RequestKind::Permission,
        answer_path: AnswerPath::Keys,
        title: clean_display(p.tool_name.as_deref().unwrap_or("Permission"), TITLE_CHARS),
        detail,
        options: vec![
            opt("allow", "Yes", Effect::Allow, None),
            opt("allow_always", "Yes, don't ask again", Effect::AllowAlways, None),
            opt("deny", "No, and tell Codex what to do differently", Effect::Deny, None),
        ],
        questions: None,
        free_text: false,
        raw_screen: None,
        ready: false,
        reusable_key: true,
    }
}

pub fn payload_for_log(raw: &[u8]) -> String {
    redact_log(&String::from_utf8_lossy(raw))
}

/// Payloads only at debug, redacted and truncated (spec §7.1 Logs).
pub fn log_payload(agent: HookAgent, name: &HookName, raw: &[u8]) {
    if tracing::enabled!(tracing::Level::DEBUG) {
        tracing::debug!(?agent, ?name, payload = %payload_for_log(raw), "hook payload");
    }
}
```

### Task 8: Hook endpoint: auth, generations, bounds, holds, close detection

GATE 2: an empty header value counts as absent, because an unlisted
variable in a Claude header interpolates to `""` (2a). The
close-detection test relies on Claude closing the held connection on a
terminal deny or Esc (2b); the endpoint only reports the close, Task 15
decides what it means.

Sol I8 and N2 shaped the bounds: the lifecycle queue is bounded in
events and bytes and fails open when full; a held request is admitted
only with one of `max_holds` permits, and the permit travels inside
`Inbound::Held` until the adapter is done with the hold (A1), so the
control channel is a bounded channel of `2 × max_holds` and is never
dropped from (A2); the listener caps open connections; the rejection
table is capped and logs only an opaque id. Each accepted request
carries the credential generation it matched (Hook token store T2).

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/endpoint.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/testing.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_endpoint.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`HookAgent`, `HookName`, `Payload`, `Hints`,
  `HookEvent`, `log_payload`).
- Produces:
  - `endpoint::Credential { token: String, generation: u64 }`.
  - `endpoint::HookAuth::new(current: Credential) -> HookAuth`,
    `set(&self, current: Credential, previous: Option<Credential>)`,
    `check(&self, presented: &str) -> Option<u64>` (the matched
    generation), `note_rejected(&self, hint: Option<&str>)`,
    `rejected_since(&self, since: Instant) -> usize`,
    `rejected_entries(&self) -> usize`.
  - `endpoint::remote_id(hint: Option<&str>) -> String` (8 hex digits of
    `sha256`, or `"none"`).
  - `endpoint::HoldId = u64`.
  - `endpoint::HoldPermit` (opaque; dropping it frees a hold slot) and
    `endpoint::HoldIn = (HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>, HoldPermit)`.
  - `endpoint::Inbound { Event { event, generation, bytes }, Held { event, hold, reply: oneshot::Sender<String>, generation, permit: HoldPermit }, HoldClosed { hold }, HoldExpired { hold } }`.
  - `endpoint::EndpointOptions { permission_hold, max_holds, body_timeout, max_body, max_events, max_event_bytes, max_connections }`
    with `Default` (3600 s, 64, 5 s, 1 MiB, 512, 16 MiB, 128).
  - `endpoint::mailbox(opts: &EndpointOptions) -> (MailboxTx, Mailbox)`;
    `Mailbox { pub events: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound>, pub control: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound> }`
    (control capacity `2 × max_holds`)
    with `Mailbox::release(&self, bytes: usize)` (the reconciler calls it
    for every `Event` it takes) and `Mailbox::dropped(&self) -> u64`.
  - `endpoint::router(auth: Arc<HookAuth>, tx: MailboxTx, opts: EndpointOptions) -> axum::Router`.
  - `endpoint::capped(listener: tokio::net::TcpListener, max: usize) -> CappedListener`
    (an `axum::serve::Listener`); Task 30 serves the router through it.
  - `testing::post`, `testing::send`, `testing::read_response` (feature
    `testing`).

```rust
//! A raw HTTP/1.1 client for hook tests: no client library, and it can
//! close a connection mid-hold the way Claude does.
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

pub async fn send(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> TcpStream {
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).await.unwrap();
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    if !token.is_empty() {
        req.push_str(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n"));
    }
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    req.push_str(body);
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    s
}

pub async fn read_response(mut s: TcpStream) -> (u16, String) {
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    // A reset after the reply (unread request bytes) keeps what was read.
    let _ = s.read_to_end(&mut buf).await;
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf).into_owned();
    let status = text.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|c| c.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    let body = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n").map(|(_, b)| b.to_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    (status, body)
}

pub async fn post(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String) {
    read_response(send(port, path, token, headers, body).await).await
}
```

```rust
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::*;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, read_response, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::wire::HookName;
use tokio::time::{Instant, timeout};

const TOKEN: &str = "tok-current-0123456789";

fn cred(token: &str, generation: u64) -> Credential {
    Credential { token: token.into(), generation }
}

async fn serve(opts: EndpointOptions) -> (u16, Arc<HookAuth>, Mailbox) {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, mb) = mailbox(&opts);
    let auth = Arc::new(HookAuth::new(cred(TOKEN, 1)));
    let app = router(auth.clone(), tx, opts.clone());
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(capped(listener, opts.max_connections), app).await.unwrap() });
    (port, auth, mb)
}

fn fx(rel: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn next_event(mb: &mut Mailbox) -> Inbound {
    let m = timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), mb.events.recv()).await.expect("event in time").unwrap();
    if let Inbound::Event { bytes, .. } = &m {
        mb.release(*bytes);
    }
    m
}

async fn next_control(mb: &mut Mailbox) -> Inbound {
    timeout(Duration::from_secs(2), mb.control.recv()).await.expect("control in time").unwrap()
}

const PR: &str = "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest";

/// Drains the control channel without waiting; returns how many messages
/// were queued (each is dropped, which frees its permit).
fn drain_control(mb: &mut Mailbox) -> usize {
    let mut n = 0;
    while mb.control.try_recv().is_ok() {
        n += 1;
    }
    n
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_disconnected_hold_keeps_its_permit_until_its_messages_are_consumed() {
    // Sol N2: closing the connection does not free the slot; consuming the
    // queued `Held` (and its `HoldClosed`) does.
    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_holds: 2, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let body = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    for _ in 0..2 {
        drop(send(port, PR, TOKEN, &[], &body).await);
    }
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    let t = Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(post(port, PR, TOKEN, &[], &body).await, (200, String::new()), "no slot: fails open");
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(500), "at once, not after a hold");
    assert!(matches!(next_event(&mut mb).await, Inbound::Event { .. }));
    assert_eq!(drain_control(&mut mb), 4, "two Held and two HoldClosed");
    let _s = send(port, PR, TOKEN, &[], &body).await;
    assert!(matches!(next_control(&mut mb).await, Inbound::Held { .. }), "a slot is free again");
}

    let (port, _auth, mut mb) = serve(EndpointOptions { max_holds: 4, ..Default::default() }).await;
    let body = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    for _ in 0..200 {
        drop(send(port, PR, TOKEN, &[], &body).await);
    }
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    let queued = drain_control(&mut mb);
    assert!(queued <= 2 * 4, "{queued} control messages retained for 4 permits");
}
```

`hooks/endpoint.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook endpoint (spec §6.1): `POST /hook/{agent}/{event}` on
//! 127.0.0.1. Lifecycle hooks get their reply before any processing. A
//! Claude tool `PermissionRequest` is parked as a hold until the adapter
//! replies, releases it, or `permission_hold` passes.
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex, RwLock};
use std::task::{Context, Poll};
use std::pin::Pin;
use std::time::Duration;

use axum::Router;
use axum::extract::{Path, Request, State};
use axum::http::{Method, StatusCode, header};
use axum::response::{IntoResponse, Response};
use axum::routing::any;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;
use tokio::io::{AsyncRead, AsyncWrite, ReadBuf};
use tokio::net::{TcpListener, TcpStream};
use tokio::sync::{OwnedSemaphorePermit, Semaphore, mpsc, oneshot};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::mapping::log_payload;
use super::wire::{HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName, Hints, Payload};

const MAX_REJECTED: usize = 256;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Credential {
    pub token: String,
    pub generation: u64,
}

struct Creds {
    current: Credential,
    previous: Option<Credential>,
}

pub struct HookAuth {
    creds: RwLock<Creds>,
    /// Opaque remote id -> last rejection. At most `MAX_REJECTED` entries.
    rejected: Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>,
}

/// 8 hex digits of `sha256(hint)`: logs and tables never hold a header value.
pub fn remote_id(hint: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match hint {
        None => "none".into(),
        Some(h) => Sha256::digest(h.as_bytes()).iter().take(4).map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect(),
    }
}

impl HookAuth {
    pub fn new(current: Credential) -> HookAuth {
        HookAuth { creds: RwLock::new(Creds { current, previous: None }), rejected: Mutex::default() }
    }

    pub fn set(&self, current: Credential, previous: Option<Credential>) {
        *self.creds.write().expect("hook auth") = Creds { current, previous };
    }

    /// Constant-time comparison; returns the matched generation (T2).
    pub fn check(&self, presented: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        let c = self.creds.read().expect("hook auth");
        let eq = |stored: &Credential| {
            !stored.token.is_empty() && bool::from(stored.token.as_bytes().ct_eq(presented.as_bytes()))
        };
        if eq(&c.current) {
            Some(c.current.generation)
        } else {
            c.previous.as_ref().filter(|p| eq(p)).map(|p| p.generation)
        }
    }

    /// Logs at most once a minute per remote, by opaque id only.
    pub fn note_rejected(&self, hint: Option<&str>) {
        let id = remote_id(hint);
        let now = Instant::now();
        let mut r = self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth");
        let quiet = r.get(&id).is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(*t) < Duration::from_secs(60));
        r.insert(id.clone(), now);
        while r.len() > MAX_REJECTED {
            let oldest = r.iter().min_by_key(|(_, t)| **t).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).expect("non-empty");
            r.remove(&oldest);
        }
        drop(r);
        if !quiet {
            tracing::warn!(remote = %id, "hook request with a missing or retired token (restart that Claude session or run /clear in it)");
        }
    }

    pub fn rejected_since(&self, since: Instant) -> usize {
        self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth").values().filter(|t| **t >= since).count()
    }

    pub fn rejected_entries(&self) -> usize {
        self.rejected.lock().expect("hook auth").len()
    }
}

pub type HoldId = u64;

/// One of `max_holds` hold slots (A1). Dropping it frees the slot; the
/// adapter keeps it until it is done with the hold.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct HoldPermit(#[allow(dead_code)] OwnedSemaphorePermit);

pub type HoldIn = (HoldId, oneshot::Sender<String>, HoldPermit);

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Inbound {
    /// A lifecycle event (or an unheld permission). `bytes` returns to the
    /// budget through `Mailbox::release`.
    Event { event: HookEvent, generation: u64, bytes: usize },
    Held { event: HookEvent, hold: HoldId, reply: oneshot::Sender<String>, generation: u64, permit: HoldPermit },
    /// The client closed the connection before acd replied.
    HoldClosed { hold: HoldId },
    /// `permission_hold` passed; the endpoint already replied empty.
    HoldExpired { hold: HoldId },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct EndpointOptions {
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
    pub body_timeout: Duration,
    pub max_body: usize,
    pub max_events: usize,
    pub max_event_bytes: usize,
    pub max_connections: usize,
}

impl Default for EndpointOptions {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EndpointOptions {
            permission_hold: Duration::from_secs(3600),
            max_holds: 64,
            body_timeout: Duration::from_secs(5),
            max_body: 1 << 20,
            max_events: 512,
            max_event_bytes: 16 << 20,
            max_connections: 128,
        }
    }
}

struct Budget {
    bytes: AtomicUsize,
    max: usize,
    dropped: AtomicU64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct MailboxTx {
    events: mpsc::Sender<Inbound>,
    /// Holds, closes and expiries (A2): capacity `2 × max_holds`. Every
    /// message belongs to a hold whose permit is still taken, so a send
    /// never finds the channel full.
    control: mpsc::Sender<Inbound>,
    budget: Arc<Budget>,
}

pub struct Mailbox {
    pub events: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound>,
    pub control: mpsc::Receiver<Inbound>,
    budget: Arc<Budget>,
}

impl Mailbox {
    pub fn release(&self, bytes: usize) {
        self.budget.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
    pub fn dropped(&self) -> u64 {
        self.budget.dropped.load(Ordering::SeqCst)
    }
}

pub fn mailbox(opts: &EndpointOptions) -> (MailboxTx, Mailbox) {
    let (etx, erx) = mpsc::channel(opts.max_events);
    let (ctx, crx) = mpsc::channel(2 * opts.max_holds);
    let budget = Arc::new(Budget { bytes: AtomicUsize::new(0), max: opts.max_event_bytes, dropped: AtomicU64::new(0) });
    (
        MailboxTx { events: etx, control: ctx, budget: budget.clone() },
        Mailbox { events: erx, control: crx, budget },
    )
}

impl MailboxTx {
    /// Queues a lifecycle event, or drops and counts it when the queue or
    /// the byte budget is full (fail open: the hook already got its reply).
    fn event(&self, event: HookEvent, generation: u64, bytes: usize) {
        let b = &self.budget;
        if b.bytes.fetch_add(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst) + bytes > b.max {
            b.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
            b.dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
            return;
        }
        if self.events.try_send(Inbound::Event { event, generation, bytes }).is_err() {
            b.bytes.fetch_sub(bytes, Ordering::SeqCst);
            b.dropped.fetch_add(1, Ordering::SeqCst);
        }
    }

    /// A2: cannot fail while the sender holds a permit; if it ever does,
    /// the message is logged and dropped (the hold still replies).
    fn control(&self, m: Inbound) -> bool {
        match self.control.try_send(m) {
            Ok(()) => true,
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::error!("hook control channel full or closed: {e}");
                false
            }
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Ctx {
    auth: Arc<HookAuth>,
    tx: MailboxTx,
    opts: EndpointOptions,
    holds: Arc<Semaphore>,
    next: Arc<AtomicU64>,
}

pub fn router(auth: Arc<HookAuth>, tx: MailboxTx, opts: EndpointOptions) -> Router {
    let holds = Arc::new(Semaphore::new(opts.max_holds));
    let ctx = Ctx { auth, tx, opts, holds, next: Arc::default() };
    Router::new()
        .route("/hook/{agent}/{event}", any(handle))
        .fallback(|| async { StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED })
        .with_state(ctx)
}

fn empty() -> Response {
    StatusCode::OK.into_response()
}

async fn handle(State(ctx): State<Ctx>, Path((agent, event)): Path<(String, String)>, req: Request) -> Response {
    if req.method() != Method::POST {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    }
    let Some(agent) = HookAgent::parse(&agent) else {
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let hints = Hints::from_headers(req.headers());
    let presented = req.headers().get("x-acd-token").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let Some(generation) = ctx.auth.check(presented) else {
        ctx.auth.note_rejected(hints.herdr_pane.as_deref());
        return StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED.into_response();
    };
    let body = match tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.body_timeout, axum::body::to_bytes(req.into_body(), ctx.opts.max_body)).await {
        Ok(Ok(b)) => b,
        Ok(Err(_)) => return StatusCode::PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE.into_response(),
        Err(_) => return StatusCode::REQUEST_TIMEOUT.into_response(),
    };
    let name = HookName::parse(&event);
    log_payload(agent, &name, &body);
    let Some(payload) = serde_json::from_slice(&body).ok().and_then(|v| Payload::from_value(&v)) else {
        return empty();
    };
    let holdable = agent == HookAgent::Claude
        && name == HookName::PermissionRequest
        && payload.tool_name.as_deref() != Some("AskUserQuestion");
    let event = HookEvent { agent, name, payload, hints, at: Instant::now() };
    if !holdable {
        ctx.tx.event(event, generation, body.len());
        return empty();
    }
    let Ok(permit) = ctx.holds.clone().try_acquire_owned() else {
        tracing::warn!("too many held permission prompts; this one is answered in the terminal");
        ctx.tx.event(event, generation, body.len());
        return empty();
    };
    hold(ctx, event, generation, HoldPermit(permit)).await
}

/// Reports a close when dropped before a reply was taken. hyper drops the
/// handler future when the client closes the connection, so a guard drop
/// without `done` is the "answered in the terminal or interrupted" signal.
struct HoldGuard {
    tx: MailboxTx,
    hold: HoldId,
    done: bool,
}

impl Drop for HoldGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if !self.done {
            self.tx.control(Inbound::HoldClosed { hold: self.hold });
        }
    }
}

async fn hold(ctx: Ctx, event: HookEvent, generation: u64, permit: HoldPermit) -> Response {
    let id = ctx.next.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
    let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = oneshot::channel::<String>();
    let mut guard = HoldGuard { tx: ctx.tx.clone(), hold: id, done: false };
    // The permit now belongs to the queued `Held` (A1).
    if !ctx.tx.control(Inbound::Held { event, hold: id, reply: reply_tx, generation, permit }) {
        guard.done = true;
        return empty();
    }
    let out = tokio::time::timeout(ctx.opts.permission_hold, reply_rx).await;
    guard.done = true;
    match out {
        Ok(Ok(body)) if !body.is_empty() => ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "application/json")], body).into_response(),
        Ok(_) => empty(),
        Err(_) => {
            ctx.tx.control(Inbound::HoldExpired { hold: id });
            empty()
        }
    }
}

/// A listener that keeps at most `max` connections open; `accept` waits
/// for a slot. The permit lives in the connection's IO and is returned
/// when hyper drops it.
pub struct CappedListener {
    inner: TcpListener,
    slots: Arc<Semaphore>,
}

pub fn capped(listener: TcpListener, max: usize) -> CappedListener {
    CappedListener { inner: listener, slots: Arc::new(Semaphore::new(max)) }
}

pub struct CappedIo {
    io: TcpStream,
    _slot: OwnedSemaphorePermit,
}

impl AsyncRead for CappedIo {
    fn poll_read(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>, buf: &mut ReadBuf<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_read(cx, buf)
    }
}

impl AsyncWrite for CappedIo {
    fn poll_write(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>, buf: &[u8]) -> Poll<std::io::Result<usize>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_write(cx, buf)
    }
    fn poll_flush(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_flush(cx)
    }
    fn poll_shutdown(mut self: Pin<&mut Self>, cx: &mut Context<'_>) -> Poll<std::io::Result<()>> {
        Pin::new(&mut self.io).poll_shutdown(cx)
    }
}

impl axum::serve::Listener for CappedListener {
    type Io = CappedIo;
    type Addr = std::net::SocketAddr;

    async fn accept(&mut self) -> (CappedIo, std::net::SocketAddr) {
        loop {
            let slot = self.slots.clone().acquire_owned().await.expect("never closed");
            match self.inner.accept().await {
                Ok((io, addr)) => return (CappedIo { io, _slot: slot }, addr),
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::debug!(error = %e, "hook listener accept failed");
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn local_addr(&self) -> std::io::Result<std::net::SocketAddr> {
        self.inner.local_addr()
    }
}
```

(`axum::serve::Listener::accept` is declared as returning `impl Future`;
an `async fn` in the impl satisfies it on Rust 1.90. If the trait in the
locked axum version differs, follow its signature; the behaviour stays
the same.)

**If `a_client_close_is_seen_as_hold_closed` fails** (hyper does not
drop the handler future on a client EOF in this version), do not weaken
the test. Make `CappedIo` also watch for EOF: keep a `Notify` per
connection, and serve each connection with
`hyper::server::conn::http1::Builder::serve_connection` in an accept
loop that aborts the connection task when a duplicated socket
(`TcpStream::into_std` + `try_clone`) reads zero bytes on `peek`. Expose
it as `pub async fn serve(listener: CappedListener, router: Router)` from
`endpoint.rs` so Task 30 calls the same function. Report the change.

status values change only `map_status`.

- Produces:
