```rust
//! `~/.claude/sessions/<pid>.json` discovery (spec §6.2, gate 5). Read
//! only `<digits>.json`; never open the `.key` files. Best effort: an
//! entry that cannot be read or verified is skipped and logged once.
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

use acd_protocol::AgentState;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::valid_id;

const MAX_ENTRY_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024;
/// Owner-only writes: a group- or world-writable entry is not trusted.
const MAX_ENTRY_MODE: u32 = 0o755;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RegistryEntry {
    pub pid: i32,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    pub started_at_ms: Option<i64>,
    pub proc_start: Option<String>,
    pub entrypoint: Option<String>,
    pub name: Option<String>,
    pub status: Option<String>,
    pub waiting_for: Option<String>,
}

impl RegistryEntry {
    pub fn from_value(v: &Value) -> Option<RegistryEntry> {
        let o = v.as_object()?;
        let s = |k: &str| o.get(k).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_owned);
        let pid = o.get("pid")?.as_i64().filter(|p| *p > 0 && *p <= i32::MAX as i64)? as i32;
        Some(RegistryEntry {
            pid,
            session_id: s("sessionId").filter(|x| valid_id(x))?,
            cwd: s("cwd"),
            started_at_ms: o.get("startedAt").and_then(Value::as_i64),
            proc_start: s("procStart"),
            entrypoint: s("entrypoint"),
            name: s("name"),
            status: s("status"),
            waiting_for: s("waitingFor"),
        })
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ProcVerdict {
    Live,
    Gone,
    Unverifiable,
}

pub trait ProcCheck: Send + Sync {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict;
}

/// `ps -o lstart=` in UTC (gate 5): `Fri Oct  9 07:29:36 2026`.
pub fn parse_lstart_utc(s: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    let t: Vec<&str> = s.split_whitespace().collect();
    let [_, mon, day, hms, year] = t.as_slice() else { return None };
    const MONTHS: [&str; 12] = ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May", "Jun", "Jul", "Aug", "Sep", "Oct", "Nov", "Dec"];
    let month = MONTHS.iter().position(|m| m == mon)? as u32 + 1;
    let mut hms = hms.split(':').map(|x| x.parse::<u32>().ok());
    let (h, m, sec) = (hms.next()??, hms.next()??, hms.next()??);
    let date = chrono::NaiveDate::from_ymd_opt(year.parse().ok()?, month, day.parse().ok()?)?;
    Some(date.and_hms_opt(h, m, sec)?.and_utc().timestamp())
}

pub struct SystemProc;

impl ProcCheck for SystemProc {
    fn check(&self, pid: i32, proc_start: &str) -> ProcVerdict {
        let Some(claimed) = parse_lstart_utc(proc_start) else {
            return ProcVerdict::Unverifiable;
        };
        match process_start_secs(pid) {
            Ok(Some(actual)) if (actual - claimed).abs() <= 1 => ProcVerdict::Live,
            Ok(_) => ProcVerdict::Gone,
            Err(()) => ProcVerdict::Unverifiable,
        }
    }
}

fn pid_exists(pid: i32) -> Option<bool> {
    // SAFETY: signal 0 only checks for existence and permission.
    if unsafe { libc::kill(pid, 0) } == 0 {
        return Some(true);
    }
    match std::io::Error::last_os_error().raw_os_error() {
        Some(libc::ESRCH) => Some(false),
        Some(libc::EPERM) => Some(true),
        _ => None,
    }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    match pid_exists(pid) {
        Some(false) => return Ok(None),
        None => return Err(()),
        Some(true) => {}
    }
    let mut info: libc::proc_bsdinfo = unsafe { std::mem::zeroed() };
    let size = std::mem::size_of::<libc::proc_bsdinfo>() as libc::c_int;
    // SAFETY: `info` is a properly sized, writable proc_bsdinfo.
    let n = unsafe {
        libc::proc_pidinfo(pid, libc::PROC_PIDTBSDINFO, 0, &mut info as *mut _ as *mut libc::c_void, size)
    };
    if n == size { Ok(Some(info.pbi_start_tvsec as i64)) } else { Err(()) }
}

#[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
fn process_start_secs(pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    let stat = match std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/stat")) {
        Ok(s) => s,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(None),
        Err(_) => return Err(()),
    };
    // Field 22 (starttime, clock ticks after boot); fields after the ")"
    // that ends the command name start at field 3.
    let after = stat.rsplit_once(')').ok_or(())?.1;
    let ticks: i64 = after.split_whitespace().nth(19).ok_or(())?.parse().map_err(|_| ())?;
    let btime: i64 = std::fs::read_to_string("/proc/stat")
        .map_err(|_| ())?
        .lines()
        .find_map(|l| l.strip_prefix("btime "))
        .ok_or(())?
        .trim()
        .parse()
        .map_err(|_| ())?;
    // SAFETY: sysconf has no preconditions.
    let hz = unsafe { libc::sysconf(libc::_SC_CLK_TCK) };
    if hz <= 0 {
        return Err(());
    }
    Ok(Some(btime + ticks / hz as i64))
}

#[cfg(not(any(target_os = "macos", target_os = "linux")))]
fn process_start_secs(_pid: i32) -> Result<Option<i64>, ()> {
    Err(())
}

pub struct Scan {
    pub live: Vec<RegistryEntry>,
    /// False when any root or any entry could not be read or verified. An
    /// incomplete scan never removes an agent (spec §6.2) and never
    /// retires a hook token (T4).
    pub complete: bool,
    pub scanned_at: tokio::time::Instant,
    /// Wall-clock milliseconds when the scan started and finished (T4).
    pub started_at_ms: i64,
    pub finished_at_ms: i64,
}

pub struct Scanner {
    dirs: Vec<PathBuf>,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    logged: HashSet<String>,
}

impl Scanner {
    pub fn new(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>, proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>) -> Scanner {
        Scanner { dirs, proc, logged: HashSet::new() }
    }

    pub fn set_dirs(&mut self, dirs: Vec<PathBuf>) {
        self.dirs = dirs;
    }

    pub fn scan(&mut self) -> Scan {
        let started_at_ms = wall_ms();
        let mut live: Vec<RegistryEntry> = Vec::new();
        let mut complete = true;
        for dir in self.dirs.clone() {
            let (found, ok) = self.scan_dir(&dir);
            complete &= ok;
            for e in found {
                if !live.iter().any(|l| l.session_id == e.session_id) {
                    live.push(e);
                }
            }
        }
        Scan { live, complete, scanned_at: tokio::time::Instant::now(), started_at_ms, finished_at_ms: wall_ms() }
    }

    fn log_once(&mut self, what: &str, why: &str) {
        if self.logged.insert(format!("{what}:{why}")) {
            tracing::info!(entry = %what, %why, "skipping a Claude session registry entry");
        }
    }

    fn scan_dir(&mut self, dir: &Path) -> (Vec<RegistryEntry>, bool) {
        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {
            Ok(e) => e,
            Err(_) => {
                self.log_once(&dir.display().to_string(), "unreadable");
                return (vec![], false);
            }
        };
        let mut live = Vec::new();
        let mut complete = true;
        for e in entries.flatten() {
            let name = e.file_name();
            let Some(name) = name.to_str() else { continue };
            let Some(stem) = name.strip_suffix(".json") else { continue };
            if stem.is_empty() || !stem.bytes().all(|b| b.is_ascii_digit()) {
                continue;
            }
            let entry = acd_securefs::read_private(&dir.join(name), MAX_ENTRY_MODE, MAX_ENTRY_BYTES)
                .ok()
                .and_then(|b| serde_json::from_slice::<Value>(&b).ok())
                .and_then(|v| RegistryEntry::from_value(&v))
                .filter(|e| e.pid.to_string() == stem);
            let Some(entry) = entry else {
                complete = false;
                self.log_once(name, "unreadable");
                continue;
            };
            match entry.proc_start.as_deref().map(|p| self.proc.check(entry.pid, p)) {
                Some(ProcVerdict::Live) => live.push(entry),
                Some(ProcVerdict::Gone) => {}
                Some(ProcVerdict::Unverifiable) | None => {
                    complete = false;
                    self.log_once(name, "process metadata unverifiable");
                }
            }
        }
        (live, complete)
    }
}

pub fn map_status(status: Option<&str>) -> AgentState {
    match status {
        Some("idle") => AgentState::Idle,
        Some("busy") => AgentState::Working,
        Some("waiting") => AgentState::NeedsYou,
        _ => AgentState::Unknown,
    }
}

/// A complete scan as the token retirement needs it (T4).
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CompleteScan {
    pub started_at_ms: i64,
    pub finished_at_ms: i64,
    pub live: Vec<RegistryEntry>,
}

pub fn wall_ms() -> i64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).map_or(0, |d| d.as_millis() as i64)
}

pub fn sessions_dir(home: &Path, claude_config_dir: Option<&Path>) -> PathBuf {
    claude_config_dir.map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")).join("sessions")
}
```

### Task 10: Ownership and binding

GATE 4 (round-2 ruling B4): `codex_bind` never uses the hook env. It
binds by the **one** Codex pane reporting the session (two or more is
ambiguous), else by the cwd only when exactly one Codex pane is in that
cwd across all panes, whatever sessions they report (gate 4: reported
sessions come from the same stale env, so they cannot rule a pane out).
Fixtures: `agent-info-codex-with-agent-session.json`,
`agent-info-codex-shared-daemon-no-agent-session.json`,
`agent-list-codex-fresh-app-server-misbound.json`. Task 17 then requires
the bound pane's own screen to match (M2) and no other Codex pane to show
the same command. GATE 2: Claude header binding relies on header
interpolation (2a).

- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/owner.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

```rust
//! Who owns a session (spec §6.2 Ownership) and which herdr pane it is
//! bound to (§6.2 Binding; §6.3 Binding as amended by gate 4).
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use acd_protocol::AgentKind;

use super::wire::{Hints, valid_id};
use crate::herdr::map::socket_hash;
use crate::herdr::reconciler::PaneMap;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PaneFact {
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub socket: PathBuf,
    pub pane_id: String,
    pub kind: AgentKind,
    pub session: Option<String>,
    pub cwd: Option<String>,
    /// G1: tickets and targets compare it.
    pub incarnation: u64,
}

pub fn pane_facts(panes: &PaneMap) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
    panes
        .read()
        .expect("pane map")
        .iter()
        .map(|(id, p)| PaneFact {
            agent_id: id.clone(),
            socket: p.socket.clone(),
            pane_id: p.pane_id.clone(),
            kind: p.kind,
            session: p.session.clone(),
            cwd: p.cwd.clone(),
            incarnation: p.incarnation,
        })
        .collect()
}

pub trait SessionOwners: Send + Sync {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String>;
}

impl SessionOwners for SessionMap {
    fn owner(&self, session_id: &str) -> Option<String> {
        SessionMap::owner(self, session_id)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Owner {
    T3(String),
    Herdr(String),
    Hooks,
    Unknown,
}

/// The pane named by the Claude headers. The socket header is only
/// compared through `socket_hash` with sockets herdr discovery already
/// knows; acd never opens a path taken from a header. A pane that reports
/// a different session is not this session's pane.
pub fn header_pane<'a>(hints: &Hints, session_id: &str, panes: &'a [PaneFact]) -> Option<&'a PaneFact> {
    let pane = hints.herdr_pane.as_deref().filter(|p| valid_id(p))?;
    let hash = socket_hash(Path::new(hints.herdr_socket.as_deref()?));
    panes.iter().find(|f| {
        f.pane_id == pane
            && f.kind == AgentKind::Claude
            && socket_hash(&f.socket) == hash
            && f.session.as_deref().is_none_or(|s| s == session_id)
    })
}

/// Spec §6.2 Ownership, rules 1 to 4, in order.
pub fn claude_owner(
    session_id: &str,
    hints: &Hints,
    entrypoint: Option<&str>,
    t3: Option<&dyn SessionOwners>,
    panes: &[PaneFact],
    ignored: &[String],
) -> Owner {
    if let Some(agent) = t3.and_then(|m| m.owner(session_id)) {
        return Owner::T3(agent);
    }
    if let Some(p) = header_pane(hints, session_id, panes) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    if let Some(p) = panes.iter().find(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Claude && p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)) {
        return Owner::Herdr(p.agent_id.clone());
    }
    match entrypoint {
        Some(e) if ignored.iter().any(|i| i == e) => Owner::Unknown,
        _ => Owner::Hooks,
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CodexBind {
    Session(PaneFact),
    OnlyInCwd(PaneFact),
    Ambiguous,
    Unbound,
}

/// Gate 4 and ruling B4: the hook env is never used. Exactly one Codex
/// pane reporting this session binds; two or more are ambiguous.
/// Otherwise the cwd binds only when exactly one Codex pane is in it
/// across all panes, whatever sessions they report. The caller still
/// requires that pane's own screen to match (M2) and no competing pane
/// to show the same command (Task 17).
pub fn codex_bind(session_id: &str, cwd: Option<&str>, panes: &[PaneFact]) -> CodexBind {
    let codex: Vec<&PaneFact> = panes.iter().filter(|p| p.kind == AgentKind::Codex).collect();
    let by_session: Vec<&&PaneFact> = codex.iter().filter(|p| p.session.as_deref() == Some(session_id)).collect();
    match by_session.as_slice() {
        [one] => return CodexBind::Session((**one).clone()),
        [_, _, ..] => return CodexBind::Ambiguous,
        [] => {}
    }
    let Some(cwd) = cwd else { return CodexBind::Unbound };
    let same: Vec<&&PaneFact> = codex
        .iter()
        .filter(|p| p.cwd.as_deref().is_some_and(|c| same_dir(c, cwd)))
        .collect();
    match same.as_slice() {
        [] => CodexBind::Unbound,
        [one] => CodexBind::OnlyInCwd((**one).clone()),
        _ => CodexBind::Ambiguous,
    }
}

/// herdr reports `/tmp/...`, Codex `/private/tmp/...` (D5).
pub fn same_dir(a: &str, b: &str) -> bool {
    let canon = |s: &str| std::fs::canonicalize(s).unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from(s.trim_end_matches('/')));
    canon(a) == canon(b)
}
```

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (`HooksAdapter`, `HooksConfig`)
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/state.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/common/mod.rs` (the shared hooks test rig)
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_claude.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 2, 7, 8, 9, 10; `HerdrAdapter::panes()` and
  `agent_locks()`; `SessionMap`.
- Produces:
  - `hooks::HooksConfig` (fields below) and
    `HooksConfig::new(permission_hold: Duration, ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>, sessions_dirs: SessionRoots) -> HooksConfig`.
  - `hooks::SessionRoots`: `SessionRoots::fixed(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>)`,
    `SessionRoots::dynamic(f: impl Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync + 'static)`,
    `SessionRoots::none()`. The scan asks it for the roots before every
    scan, so the daemon (Task 30) can follow the install records (D24).
  - `hooks::HooksAdapter::new(cfg: HooksConfig, herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>, t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>, proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>) -> Arc<HooksAdapter>`.
  - `HooksAdapter::router(&self, auth: Arc<HookAuth>) -> axum::Router`.
  - `HooksAdapter::start(self: &Arc<Self>, core: Arc<Core>)`.
  - `HooksAdapter::last_complete_scan(&self) -> Option<CompleteScan>`
    (the last **complete** scan: wall-clock start and end and its live
    sessions; T4 checks both times).
  - `HooksAdapter::sessions_on_generation(&self, generation: u64) -> HashSet<String>`
    (session ids seen authenticating with that credential generation;
    the last four generations are kept).
  - `Adapter` impl: name `"hooks"`, `answer_paths() == [AnswerPath::Hook]`,
    `sources() == [Source::Hooks]`. `send` returns
    `NotDelivered(NotSupported)`. `deliver` is added in Task 14 (this
    task returns `NotDelivered(RequestGone)`).
  - `state::Shared { held: Mutex<HashMap<String, HeldReq>>, last_scan: Mutex<Option<CompleteScan>>, by_generation: Mutex<BTreeMap<u64, HashSet<String>>> }` (crate-private).
  - Every session carries `bind_gen` (G2), bumped by `apply_owner` on
    every owner change. The reconciler loop serves control, internal
    results, the tick, T3 changes and then events, in that priority
    (Task 19 replaces it with A6's batched turns).
    Task 18 adds request revocation to the transitions that need it.

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::{HerdrAdapter, HerdrConfig, fake::FakeHerdr, keys::KeysTiming, reconciler::Timing};
use acd_adapters::hooks::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, ProcVerdict};
use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::{Credential, HookAuth};
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::{post, send};
use acd_adapters::hooks::{HooksAdapter, HooksConfig};
use acd_adapters::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use acd_core::{Core, testing::test_machine};
use acd_protocol::Agent;
use serde_json::Value;

pub const TOKEN: &str = "tok-hooks-test-0123456789";

pub struct Live;
impl ProcCheck for Live {
    fn check(&self, _: i32, _: &str) -> ProcVerdict { ProcVerdict::Live }
}

pub struct Rig {
    pub core: Arc<Core>,
    pub hooks: Arc<HooksAdapter>,
    pub herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    pub fake: Option<FakeHerdr>,
    pub t3: Arc<SessionMap>,
    pub port: u16,
    pub home: tempfile::TempDir,
}

pub fn fast(cfg: &mut HooksConfig) {
    cfg.tick = Duration::from_millis(20);
    cfg.scan_interval = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.evidence_window = Duration::from_millis(400);
    cfg.capture_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.capture_poll = Duration::from_millis(50);
    cfg.codex_window = Duration::from_millis(600);
    cfg.held_dialog_poll = Duration::from_millis(100);
    cfg.end_grace = Duration::from_millis(200);
}

/// `panes`: herdr agents to fake; empty means no herdr at all.
pub async fn rig(panes: &[Value], tweak: impl FnOnce(&mut HooksConfig)) -> Rig {
    let home = tempfile::Builder::new().prefix("acd").tempdir_in("/tmp").unwrap();
    let (herdr, fake) = if panes.is_empty() {
        (None, None)
    } else {
        let sock = home.path().join(".config/herdr/herdr.sock");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(sock.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        let fake = FakeHerdr::start_at(&sock).await;
        for p in panes { fake.set_agent(p.clone()); }
        let cfg = HerdrConfig {
            enabled: true, home: home.path().into(), env_socket: None, extra_sockets: vec![],
            scan_interval: Duration::from_millis(100),
            timing: Timing { list_idle: Duration::from_millis(300), list_blocked: Duration::from_millis(100),
                             backoff_max: Duration::from_millis(200), fallback_after: Duration::from_millis(200),
                             poll: Duration::from_millis(50) },
            keys: KeysTiming { evidence_timeout: Duration::from_millis(800), ..KeysTiming::default() },
        };
        (Some(HerdrAdapter::new(cfg)), Some(fake))
    };
    let sessions = home.path().join(".claude/sessions");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&sessions).unwrap();
    let mut cfg = HooksConfig::new(Duration::from_secs(3600), vec!["sdk-ts".into()],
        acd_adapters::hooks::SessionRoots::fixed(vec![sessions]));
    fast(&mut cfg);
    tweak(&mut cfg);
    let t3 = Arc::new(SessionMap::default());
    let hooks = HooksAdapter::new(cfg, herdr.clone(), Some(t3.clone()), Arc::new(Live));
    let mut adapters: Vec<Arc<dyn acd_core::Adapter>> = vec![hooks.clone()];
    if let Some(h) = &herdr { adapters.push(h.clone()); }
    let core = Core::new(test_machine(), adapters);
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        // h.set_evidence(hooks.evidence()); // Task 16 adds HooksAdapter::evidence and restores this line
        h.start(core.clone());
    }
    hooks.start(core.clone());
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.unwrap();
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let auth = Arc::new(HookAuth::new(Credential { token: TOKEN.into(), generation: 1 }));
    let app = hooks.router(auth);
    tokio::spawn(async move { axum::serve(acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::capped(listener, 128), app).await.unwrap() });
    if let Some(h) = &herdr {
        let n = panes.len();
        until("herdr panes", || (h.panes().read().unwrap().len() == n).then_some(())).await;
    }
    Rig { core, hooks, herdr, fake, t3, port, home }
}

pub async fn until<T>(what: &str, mut f: impl FnMut() -> Option<T>) -> T {
    for _ in 0..250 {
        if let Some(v) = f() { return v; }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    panic!("timed out waiting for {what}");
}

pub fn fx(rel: &str) -> Value {
    let p = format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/{rel}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"));
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

pub const SID: &str = "4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0";

impl Rig {
    pub fn sock(&self) -> String { self.fake.as_ref().unwrap().path().to_string_lossy().into_owned() }
    pub async fn hook(&self, agent: &str, event: &str, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (u16, String) {
        post(self.port, &format!("/hook/{agent}/{event}"), TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub async fn hold(&self, body: &Value, headers: &[(&str, &str)]) -> tokio::net::TcpStream {
        send(self.port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, headers, &body.to_string()).await
    }
    pub fn agent(&self, id: &str) -> Option<Agent> { self.core.agent(id) }
}

pub const CLI: [(&str, &str); 1] = [("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
```

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;

use acd_protocol::*;

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_hooks_only_session_appears_on_its_first_hook() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    let a = until("agent", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
    assert_eq!((a.source, a.kind, a.state, a.can_send), (Source::Hooks, AgentKind::Claude, AgentState::Working, false));

}

#[tokio::test]

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_t3_owned_session_is_dropped_and_comes_back_when_t3_lets_go() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("agent", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}"))).await;
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", Some(SID));
    until("dropped", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;

    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", None);
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;

    until("pane session", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().values().find_map(|p| p.session.clone())).await;
    r.hook("claude", "UserPromptSubmit", &fx("claude/payload-UserPromptSubmit.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("migrated", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).is_none().then_some(())).await;
}
```

```rust
//! Claude and Codex hooks (spec §6.1 to §6.3).
pub mod claude_sessions;
pub mod endpoint;
pub mod mapping;
pub mod owner;
mod state;
#[cfg(any(test, feature = "testing"))]
pub mod testing;
pub mod wire;

use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_core::{Adapter, Answer, Core, DeliverError};
use acd_protocol::{Agent, AnswerPath, ErrorCode, Request, Source};
use async_trait::async_trait;

use self::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry};
use self::endpoint::{EndpointOptions, HookAuth, Mailbox, MailboxTx};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct HooksConfig {
    /// `claude.permission_hold` (spec §9); holds end here with an empty reply.
    pub permission_hold: Duration,
    pub ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>,
    /// Registry roots (`<claude config dir>/sessions`), asked before every
    /// scan; empty disables the scan.
    pub sessions_dirs: SessionRoots,
    pub scan_interval: Duration,
    pub tick: Duration,
    /// Correlated outcome evidence window (spec §5.1, §6.2: 30 s).
    pub evidence_window: Duration,
    /// Fingerprint capture after a request opens (spec §6.2 step 6: 2 s).
    pub capture_window: Duration,
    pub capture_poll: Duration,
    /// Codex dialog wait (spec §6.3: 1 s).
    pub codex_window: Duration,
    /// Presence checks of a held request's captured dialog.
    pub held_dialog_poll: Duration,
    /// Removal after `SessionEnd` or the registry entry disappearing (10 min).
    pub end_grace: Duration,
    /// Removal after no event at all (24 h).
    pub idle_expiry: Duration,
    pub max_holds: usize,
    /// A3: screen reads running at once.
    pub max_reads: usize,
    /// A5: open tracks.
    pub max_tracks: usize,
    /// A7: the longest the reconciler waits for a delivery lock to apply a read result.
    pub lock_wait: Duration,
    /// A6: items per queue per loop turn.
    pub batch: usize,
    /// O7: how long an unconsumed identity can shadow a new occurrence.
    pub unconsumed_ttl: Duration,
    /// A8: Claude sessions, Codex sessions and sessions with unconsumed
    /// identities, each.
    pub max_sessions: usize,
    /// A8: session ids per credential generation.
    pub max_generation_ids: usize,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct SessionRoots(Arc<dyn Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync>);

impl SessionRoots {
    pub fn fixed(dirs: Vec<PathBuf>) -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots(Arc::new(move || dirs.clone()))
    }
    pub fn dynamic(f: impl Fn() -> Vec<PathBuf> + Send + Sync + 'static) -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots(Arc::new(f))
    }
    pub fn none() -> SessionRoots {
        SessionRoots::fixed(vec![])
    }
    pub fn get(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
        (self.0)()
    }
}

impl std::fmt::Debug for SessionRoots {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str("SessionRoots")
    }
}

impl HooksConfig {
    pub fn new(permission_hold: Duration, ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>, sessions_dirs: SessionRoots) -> HooksConfig {
        HooksConfig {
            permission_hold,
            ignored_entrypoints,
            sessions_dirs,
            scan_interval: Duration::from_secs(30),
            tick: Duration::from_millis(250),
            evidence_window: Duration::from_secs(30),
            capture_window: Duration::from_secs(2),
            capture_poll: Duration::from_millis(200),
            codex_window: Duration::from_secs(1),
            held_dialog_poll: Duration::from_secs(1),
            end_grace: Duration::from_secs(600),
            idle_expiry: Duration::from_secs(24 * 3600),
            max_holds: 64,
            max_reads: 8,
            max_tracks: 256,
            lock_wait: Duration::from_secs(2),
            batch: 32,
            unconsumed_ttl: Duration::from_secs(600),
            max_sessions: 4096,
            max_generation_ids: 4096,
        }
    }
}

pub struct HooksAdapter {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    shared: Arc<state::Shared>,
    opts: EndpointOptions,
    tx: MailboxTx,
    rx: Mutex<Option<Mailbox>>,
}

impl HooksAdapter {
    pub fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
    ) -> Arc<HooksAdapter> {
        let opts = EndpointOptions { permission_hold: cfg.permission_hold, max_holds: cfg.max_holds, ..Default::default() };
        let (tx, rx) = endpoint::mailbox(&opts);
        Arc::new(HooksAdapter { cfg, herdr, t3, proc, shared: Arc::default(), opts, tx, rx: Mutex::new(Some(rx)) })
    }

    pub fn router(&self, auth: Arc<HookAuth>) -> axum::Router {
        endpoint::router(auth, self.tx.clone(), self.opts.clone())
    }

    /// Spawns the reconciler. Call once.
    pub fn start(self: &Arc<Self>, core: Arc<Core>) {
        let rx = self.rx.lock().expect("rx").take().expect("HooksAdapter::start called twice");
        let r = state::Reconciler::new(
            self.cfg.clone(), core, self.herdr.clone(), self.t3.clone(), self.proc.clone(), self.shared.clone(), rx,
        );
        tokio::spawn(r.run());
    }

    pub fn last_complete_scan(&self) -> Option<claude_sessions::CompleteScan> {
        self.shared.last_scan.lock().expect("shared").clone()
    }

    pub fn sessions_on_generation(&self, generation: u64) -> HashSet<String> {
        self.shared.by_generation.lock().expect("shared").get(&generation).cloned().unwrap_or_default()
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Adapter for HooksAdapter {
    fn name(&self) -> &'static str {
        "hooks"
    }
    fn answer_paths(&self) -> &[AnswerPath] {
        &[AnswerPath::Hook]
    }
    fn sources(&self) -> &[Source] {
        &[Source::Hooks]
    }
    async fn deliver(&self, _request: &Request, _answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone)) // Task 14
    }
    /// Hooks-only agents have `can_send: false`; the core refuses first.
    async fn send(&self, _agent: &Agent, _text: &str) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported))
    }
}
```

`hooks/state.rs`:

```rust
//! The hooks reconciler: the only code that changes hook-derived state.
//! It drains the endpoint's mailbox in arrival order and its own internal
//! channel (results of spawned screen reads and scans). It never awaits a
//! herdr call itself.
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap, HashSet};
use std::path::Path;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};

use acd_core::{AgentObs, Core, Observation};
use acd_protocol::{AgentKind, AgentState, Effect, Source};
use tokio::sync::{mpsc, oneshot, watch};
use tokio::time::Instant;

use super::HooksConfig;
use super::claude_sessions::{ProcCheck, RegistryEntry, Scan, Scanner, map_status};
use super::endpoint::{HoldId, HoldIn, HoldPermit, Inbound, Mailbox};
use super::owner::{Owner, PaneFact, claude_owner, pane_facts};
use super::wire::{AlwaysRule, HookAgent, HookEvent, HookName};
use crate::herdr::HerdrAdapter;
use crate::t3::sessions::SessionMap;
use crate::text::{LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS, TITLE_CHARS, cap_chars, clean_display};

/// State `deliver` and the reconciler share. `deliver` only takes a reply
/// sender and stamps `delivered`; no lock is held across an await.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(crate) struct Shared {
    pub held: Mutex<HashMap<String, HeldReq>>,
    /// The last complete scan (T4).
    pub last_scan: Mutex<Option<super::claude_sessions::CompleteScan>>,
    /// Credential generation -> sessions seen using it (T4).
    pub by_generation: Mutex<BTreeMap<u64, HashSet<String>>>,
}

pub(crate) struct HeldReq {
    pub hold: HoldId,
    pub reply: Option<oneshot::Sender<String>>,
    pub rule: Option<AlwaysRule>,
    pub delivered: Option<(Effect, Instant)>,
    /// A1: the hold slot. Dropped when the reply is written, when the
    /// hold's close or expiry is processed, or when the request is forgotten.
    pub permit: Option<HoldPermit>,
}

pub(crate) enum Internal {
    Scanned(Scan),
}

struct Session {
    owner: Owner,
    /// G2: bumped on every owner or binding change and every revocation.
    bind_gen: u64,
    /// The registry agent this session shows as, if any.
    agent_id: Option<String>,
    hooks_only: bool,
    cwd: Option<String>,
    name: Option<String>,
    entrypoint: Option<String>,
    state: AgentState,
    last_message: Option<String>,
    last_hook: Option<Instant>,
    last_event: Instant,
    in_registry: bool,
    ended: Option<Instant>,
}

pub(crate) struct Reconciler {
    cfg: HooksConfig,
    core: Arc<Core>,
    herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
    t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
    shared: Arc<Shared>,
    mailbox: Mailbox,
    internal_tx: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Internal>,
    internal_rx: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Internal>,
    scanner: Option<Arc<Mutex<Scanner>>>,
    sessions: HashMap<String, Session>,
}

impl Reconciler {
    pub(crate) fn new(
        cfg: HooksConfig,
        core: Arc<Core>,
        herdr: Option<Arc<HerdrAdapter>>,
        t3: Option<Arc<SessionMap>>,
        proc: Arc<dyn ProcCheck>,
        shared: Arc<Shared>,
        mailbox: Mailbox,
    ) -> Reconciler {
        let (internal_tx, internal_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let scanner = Some(Arc::new(Mutex::new(Scanner::new(cfg.sessions_dirs.get(), proc))));
        Reconciler { cfg, core, herdr, t3, shared, mailbox, internal_tx, internal_rx, scanner, sessions: HashMap::new() }
    }

    /// Priority (Global Constraints, Sol I8): control (holds, closes,
    /// expiries), internal read results, the tick, T3 ownership changes,
    /// then lifecycle events. A flood of events cannot starve closes,
    /// timers or takeovers; events wait in the bounded mailbox.
    pub(crate) async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut tick = tokio::time::interval(self.cfg.tick);
        tick.set_missed_tick_behavior(tokio::time::MissedTickBehavior::Delay);
        let mut t3_changes = self.t3.as_ref().map(|m| m.changes());
        let mut next_scan = Instant::now();
        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                biased;
                Some(m) = self.mailbox.control.recv() => self.on_inbound(m).await,
                Some(m) = self.internal_rx.recv() => self.on_internal(m).await,
                _ = tick.tick() => {
                    let now = Instant::now();
                    if now >= next_scan {
                        next_scan = now + self.cfg.scan_interval;
                        self.spawn_scan();
                    }
                    self.on_tick().await;
                }
                _ = changed(&mut t3_changes) => self.reevaluate_all().await,
                m = self.mailbox.events.recv() => match m {
                    Some(m) => {
                        if let Inbound::Event { bytes, .. } = &m {
                            self.mailbox.release(*bytes);
                        }
                        self.on_inbound(m).await
                    }
                    None => return,
                },
            }
        }
    }

    fn spawn_scan(&self) {
        let Some(scanner) = self.scanner.clone() else { return };
        let dirs = self.cfg.sessions_dirs.get();
        if dirs.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
            let mut s = scanner.lock().expect("scanner");
            s.set_dirs(dirs);
            let scan = s.scan();
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::Scanned(scan));
        });
    }

    fn panes(&self) -> Vec<PaneFact> {
        self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| pane_facts(&h.panes())).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    fn owner_of(&self, session_id: &str, hints: &super::wire::Hints, entrypoint: Option<&str>) -> Owner {
        let t3 = self.t3.as_deref().map(|m| m as &dyn super::owner::SessionOwners);
        claude_owner(session_id, hints, entrypoint, t3, &self.panes(), &self.cfg.ignored_entrypoints)
    }

    async fn on_inbound(&mut self, m: Inbound) {
        match m {
            Inbound::Event { event, generation, .. } => self.on_event(event, generation, None).await,
            Inbound::Held { event, hold, reply, generation, permit } => self.on_event(event, generation, Some((hold, reply, permit))).await,
            Inbound::HoldClosed { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, false).await,
            Inbound::HoldExpired { hold } => self.on_hold_ended(hold, true).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_event(&mut self, event: HookEvent, generation: u64, hold: Option<HoldIn>) {
        {
            // T4: remember which credential generation each session used.
            let mut g = self.shared.by_generation.lock().expect("shared");
            g.entry(generation).or_default().insert(event.payload.session_id.clone());
            while g.len() > 4 {
                let oldest = *g.keys().next().expect("non-empty");
                g.remove(&oldest);
            }
        }
        if event.payload.is_subagent() {
            return; // `hold` drops here: the endpoint replies empty
        }
        match event.agent {
            HookAgent::Claude => self.on_claude(event, hold).await,
            HookAgent::Codex => self.on_codex(event).await,
        }
    }

    async fn on_claude(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<HoldIn>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let entrypoint = event.hints.entrypoint.clone();
        let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &event.hints, entrypoint.as_deref());
        let now = Instant::now();
        let s = self.sessions.entry(sid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
            owner: Owner::Unknown, bind_gen: 0, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
            state: AgentState::Idle, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: false, ended: None,
        });
        s.last_event = now;
        s.last_hook = Some(now);
        if event.payload.cwd.is_some() {
            s.cwd = event.payload.cwd.clone();
        }
        if entrypoint.is_some() {
            s.entrypoint = entrypoint;
        }
        match event.name {
            HookName::UserPromptSubmit => s.state = AgentState::Working,
            HookName::Stop => {
                s.state = AgentState::Done;
                s.last_message = event
                    .payload
                    .last_assistant_message
                    .as_deref()
                    .map(|m| cap_chars(&clean_display(m, usize::MAX), LAST_MESSAGE_CHARS));
            }
            HookName::SessionEnd => s.ended = Some(now),
            _ => {}
        }
        self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
        let _ = hold; // permission requests: Task 14
    }

    async fn on_codex(&mut self, _event: HookEvent) {} // Task 17

    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, _hold: HoldId, _expired: bool) {} // Task 15

    async fn on_internal(&mut self, m: Internal) {
        match m {
            Internal::Scanned(scan) => self.on_scan(scan).await,
        }
    }

    /// Moves a session to `owner`, publishing whatever that changes.
    async fn apply_owner(&mut self, sid: &str, owner: Owner) {
        let hooks_id = format!("claude:{sid}");
        let Some(s) = self.sessions.get_mut(sid) else { return };
        let was_hooks_only = s.hooks_only;
        if s.owner != owner {
            s.bind_gen += 1; // G2: outstanding tickets for this session fail
        }
        s.owner = owner.clone();
        match owner {
            Owner::T3(_) | Owner::Unknown => {
                s.agent_id = None;
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only {
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: hooks_id });
                }
            }
            Owner::Herdr(agent) => {
                s.agent_id = Some(agent.clone());
                s.hooks_only = false;
                if was_hooks_only && self.core.agent(&agent).is_some() {
                    let lock = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
                    let _g = match &lock {
                        Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                        None => None,
                    };
                    self.core.observe(Observation::AgentMigrated { from: hooks_id, to: agent });
                }
            }
            Owner::Hooks => {
                s.agent_id = Some(hooks_id.clone());
                s.hooks_only = true;
                let obs = hooks_only_obs(&hooks_id, s);
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentUpsert(obs));
            }
        }
    }

    async fn reevaluate_all(&mut self) {
        let ids: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> =
            self.sessions.iter().map(|(k, s)| (k.clone(), s.entrypoint.clone())).collect();
        for (sid, entrypoint) in ids {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&sid, &Default::default(), entrypoint.as_deref());
            let changed = self.sessions.get(&sid).is_some_and(|s| s.owner != owner);
            if changed {
                self.apply_owner(&sid, owner).await;
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_scan(&mut self, scan: Scan) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let live: HashSet<String> = scan.live.iter().map(|e| e.session_id.clone()).collect();
        for e in &scan.live {
            let s = self.sessions.entry(e.session_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Session {
                owner: Owner::Unknown, bind_gen: 0, agent_id: None, hooks_only: false, cwd: None, name: None, entrypoint: None,
                state: AgentState::Unknown, last_message: None, last_hook: None, last_event: now, in_registry: true, ended: None,
            });
            s.in_registry = true;
            s.last_event = now;
            s.cwd = s.cwd.clone().or_else(|| e.cwd.clone());
            s.name = e.name.clone().or(s.name.take());
            if s.entrypoint.is_none() {
                s.entrypoint = e.entrypoint.clone();
            }
            // D11: hooks are fresher than the scan, and done stays done.
            let fresh_hook = s.last_hook.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) < self.cfg.scan_interval);
            let scanned = map_status(e.status.as_deref());
            if !fresh_hook && !(s.state == AgentState::Done && scanned == AgentState::Idle) {
                s.state = scanned;
            }
        }
        if scan.complete {
            for (sid, s) in self.sessions.iter_mut() {
                if s.in_registry && !live.contains(sid) && s.ended.is_none() {
                    s.ended = Some(now);
                }
            }
            *self.shared.last_scan.lock().expect("shared") = Some(super::claude_sessions::CompleteScan {
                started_at_ms: scan.started_at_ms,
                finished_at_ms: scan.finished_at_ms,
                live: scan.live.clone(),
            });
        }
        for e in &scan.live {
            let owner = self.owner_of(&e.session_id, &Default::default(), e.entrypoint.as_deref());
            self.apply_owner(&e.session_id, owner).await;
        }
    }

    async fn on_tick(&mut self) {
        let now = Instant::now();
        let gone: Vec<String> = self
            .sessions
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, s)| {
                s.ended.is_some_and(|t| now.duration_since(t) >= self.cfg.end_grace)
                    || now.duration_since(s.last_event) >= self.cfg.idle_expiry
            })
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for sid in gone {
            if let Some(s) = self.sessions.remove(&sid)
                && s.hooks_only
            {
                self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: format!("claude:{sid}") });
            }
        }
    }
}

fn hooks_only_obs(id: &str, s: &Session) -> AgentObs {
    let project = s
        .cwd
        .as_deref()
        .and_then(|c| Path::new(c).file_name())
        .map(|n| clean_display(&n.to_string_lossy(), TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|p| !p.is_empty());
    let title = s
        .name
        .as_deref()
        .map(|n| clean_display(n, TITLE_CHARS))
        .filter(|t| !t.is_empty())
        .or_else(|| project.clone())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "Claude".into());
    AgentObs {
        id: id.into(),
        source: Source::Hooks,
        kind: AgentKind::Claude,
        title,
        cwd: s.cwd.as_deref().map(|c| clean_display(c, usize::MAX)),
        project,
        state: s.state,
        failed: false,
        last_message: s.last_message.clone(),
        can_send: false,
    }
}

async fn changed(rx: &mut Option<watch::Receiver<u64>>) {
    match rx {
        Some(r) if r.changed().await.is_ok() => {}
        _ => std::future::pending().await,
    }
}
```

Note: `apply_owner` re-upserts a hooks-only agent on every event, which
is how state changes (`working`, `done`) reach the registry; the
registry drops identical upserts.

### Task 12: Outcome rules

Pure rules used by Tasks 14 to 17: what a later hook event says about a
request (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3 Completion; Occurrence
contract O5). No state, so this task runs in parallel with Task 11.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/evidence.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`HookEvent`, `HookName`, `tool_digest`, `command_of`),
  herdr `keys::Verdict`.
- Produces:
  - `evidence::Expect { ClaudeTool { session_id, tool_name, digest }, ClaudeQuestion { session_id, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> }, CodexTool { session_id, tool_name, command } }`.
  - `evidence::Seen { ToolRan, TurnEnded, Answered(String) }`,
    `evidence::Sent { Allow, Deny, Choose(String) }`,
    `evidence::observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen>`,
    `evidence::verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict` (herdr's `keys::Verdict`),
    `evidence::sent_for_effect(Effect) -> Option<Sent>`,
    `evidence::is_identity(&Seen) -> bool`,
    `evidence::same_identity(&Expect, &Expect) -> bool` (O7, labels excluded) and
    `evidence::observe_shadowed(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent, shadowed: bool) -> Option<Seen>` (O7).

```rust
mod tests {

        assert_eq!(observe(&e, &stop), Some(Seen::TurnEnded));

        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Allow, &Seen::TurnEnded), Verdict::Contradicts);

        assert_eq!(verdict(&Sent::Deny, &Seen::ToolRan), Verdict::Contradicts);

        let e = bash(post.payload.tool_input["command"].as_str().unwrap());

        assert_eq!(observe_shadowed(&e, &post, true), None);
        let stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        assert_eq!(observe_shadowed(&e, &stop, true), Some(Seen::TurnEnded));
        assert!(is_identity(&Seen::ToolRan) && is_identity(&Seen::Answered("x".into())) && !is_identity(&Seen::TurnEnded));

        let a = question("Pick a color", &[("o1", "Red"), ("o2", "Blue")]);
        let b = question("Pick a color", &[("o1", "Blue"), ("o2", "Green")]);

        assert!(!same_identity(&a, &bash("x")));

        assert_eq!(observe(&question("A different question?", &[("o1", "Red")]), &post), None);

        bad.payload.tool_input = serde_json::json!({});
        assert_eq!(observe(&question("Pick a color", &[("o1", "Red")]), &bad), None, "not a string answer");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `evidence.rs`**

```rust
//! Correlated outcome evidence (spec §5.1 Attribution, §6.2 step 3, §6.3
//! Completion). Pure rules here; `EvidenceHub` (Task 16) feeds the keys
//! engine with them.
use std::collections::HashMap;

use acd_protocol::Effect;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::wire::{HookEvent, HookName, command_of, tool_digest};
pub use crate::herdr::keys::Verdict;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Expect {
    ClaudeTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, digest: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { session_id: String, question: String, labels: HashMap<String, String> },
    CodexTool { session_id: String, tool_name: String, command: String },
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Seen {
    ToolRan,
    TurnEnded,
    Answered(String),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Sent {
    Allow,
    Deny,
    Choose(String),
}

pub fn sent_for_effect(e: Effect) -> Option<Sent> {
    match e {
        Effect::Allow | Effect::AllowAlways => Some(Sent::Allow),
        Effect::Deny => Some(Sent::Deny),
        Effect::Choose | Effect::Unknown => None,
    }
}

fn session_of(e: &Expect) -> &str {
    match e {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::ClaudeQuestion { session_id, .. }
        | Expect::CodexTool { session_id, .. } => session_id,
    }
}

/// What `ev` says about the expected request, if anything.
pub fn observe(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent) -> Option<Seen> {
    if ev.payload.session_id != session_of(expect) {
        return None;
    }
    let turn_end = matches!(ev.name, HookName::Stop | HookName::UserPromptSubmit);
    let tool = ev.payload.tool_name.as_deref();
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeTool { tool_name, digest, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && tool_digest(tool_name, &ev.payload.tool_input) == *digest
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { question, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse && tool == Some("AskUserQuestion") {
                // Sol R3-2: identity evidence only when the answers map has
                // this question with a string answer; another question's
                // outcome is no evidence at all.
                ev.payload.tool_response.get("answers").or_else(|| ev.payload.tool_input.get("answers"))
                    .and_then(|a| a.get(question.as_str()))
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    .map(|answer| Seen::Answered(answer.to_owned()))
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
        Expect::CodexTool { tool_name, command, .. } => {
            if ev.name == HookName::PostToolUse
                && tool == Some(tool_name.as_str())
                && command_of(&ev.payload.tool_input) == Some(command.as_str())
            {
                Some(Seen::ToolRan)
            } else {
                turn_end.then_some(Seen::TurnEnded)
            }
        }
    }
}

/// O7: the evidence identity of an expectation (Sol R3-2). Question
/// identity is the question text; option labels are not part of it,
/// because answer evidence names only the question and the chosen label.
pub fn same_identity(a: &Expect, b: &Expect) -> bool {
    match (a, b) {
        (
            Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id: s1, tool_name: t1, digest: d1 },
            Expect::ClaudeTool { session_id: s2, tool_name: t2, digest: d2 },
        ) => s1 == s2 && t1 == t2 && d1 == d2,
        (
            Expect::ClaudeQuestion { session_id: s1, question: q1, .. },
            Expect::ClaudeQuestion { session_id: s2, question: q2, .. },
        ) => s1 == s2 && q1 == q2,
        (
            Expect::CodexTool { session_id: s1, tool_name: t1, command: c1 },
            Expect::CodexTool { session_id: s2, tool_name: t2, command: c2 },
        ) => s1 == s2 && t1 == t2 && c1 == c2,
        _ => false,
    }
}

/// O7: evidence that names an occurrence's identity, as opposed to a
/// turn end.
pub fn is_identity(seen: &Seen) -> bool {
    matches!(seen, Seen::ToolRan | Seen::Answered(_))
}

/// O7: a shadowed occurrence ignores identity evidence; turn ends apply.
pub fn observe_shadowed(expect: &Expect, ev: &HookEvent, shadowed: bool) -> Option<Seen> {
    observe(expect, ev).filter(|seen| !(shadowed && is_identity(seen)))
}

pub fn verdict(sent: &Sent, seen: &Seen) -> Verdict {
    match (sent, seen) {
        (Sent::Allow, Seen::ToolRan) | (Sent::Deny, Seen::TurnEnded) => Verdict::Consistent,
        (Sent::Choose(want), Seen::Answered(got)) if want == got => Verdict::Consistent,
        _ => Verdict::Contradicts,
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `pub mod evidence;`.

### Task 13: Exact dialog matching

The Dialog match contract (M1 to M5), and its adversarial tests (Sol
B1). This replaces the whitespace-stripping `contains` match of
revision 1, which let a hook for `touch /tmp/x` match a dialog for
`touch /tmp/x; rm -rf ~`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/screen.rs` (`Dialog::region`, `Dialog::option_rows`)
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/dialogs.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs` (module list)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 7 (`QuestionSpec`), herdr `screen::find_dialog`.
- Produces:
  - `screen::Dialog` gains `pub region: Vec<String>` (the raw rows of
    the located region, first row to footer, as read) and
    `pub option_rows: Vec<usize>` (indexes into `region`). For P3 hook
    matching only; the guard never reads them.
  - `dialogs::DialogMatch { ClaudeBash { command: String }, ClaudeQuestion { spec: QuestionSpec }, CodexCommand { command: String } }`,
    `DialogMatch::matches(&self, d: &Dialog) -> bool`,
    `DialogMatch::find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog>`,
    `DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool`,
    `dialogs::single_row(s: &str) -> bool` (M4).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn screen(name: &str) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
    }
    fn bash(c: &str) -> DialogMatch {
        DialogMatch::ClaudeBash { command: c.into() }
    }
    fn codex(c: &str) -> DialogMatch {
        DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command: c.into() }
    }
    fn question(header: &str, q: &str, labels: &[&str]) -> DialogMatch {
        DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion {
            spec: QuestionSpec {
                header: header.into(),
                question: q.into(),
                multi_select: false,
                options: labels.iter().map(|l| (l.to_string(), None)).collect(),
            },
        }
    }
    const CMD: &str = "touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt";
    const CODEX_CMD: &str = "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt";

    #[test]
    fn the_recorded_dialogs_match() {
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_some());
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt")).is_some());
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt")).is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn prefix_and_suffix_commands_do_not_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1").find(&s).is_none(), "hook is a prefix of the screen");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt; rm -rf ~").find(&s).is_none(), "hook is longer");
        let longer = s.replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", " touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt; rm -rf ~");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&longer).is_none(), "screen is longer than the hook");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt");
        assert!(codex("touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1").find(&c).is_none());
        let c2 = c.replace("  $ touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt", "  $ touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt && curl x | sh");
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c2).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn quoted_whitespace_is_compared_exactly() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", r#" echo "a  b""#);
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a  b""#).find(&s).is_some());
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a b""#).find(&s).is_none(), "two spaces are not one");
        assert!(bash(r#"echo "a	b""#).find(&s).is_none(), "a tab never matches (M4)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_forged_description_makes_the_match_ambiguous() {
        let rule = "╌".repeat(40);
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")
            .replace(" Create empty file", &format!(" Create empty file\n{rule}\n {CMD}\n{rule}"));
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none(), "four dashed rules: not exactly two (M5)");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt").replace(
            "  Reason: Allow",
            &format!("  Reason: see below\n\n  $ {CODEX_CMD}\n\n  Allow"),
        );
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c).is_none(), "two `$ ` rows (M5)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn other_claude_tools_never_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" Bash command", " Write file");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn same_labels_different_question_does_not_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a colour", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&s).is_none());
        assert!(question("Colour", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&s).is_none());
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red"]).find(&s).is_none(), "the full option list");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Blue", "Red"]).find(&s).is_none(), "in order");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"])
            .find(&screen("claude-askuserquestion-multi.txt")).is_none(), "a tab strip never matches");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrapped_rows_never_match() {
        let s = screen("claude-permission-bash.txt").replace(" touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt", " touch /tmp/ac\n p0/t1.txt");
        assert!(bash(CMD).find(&s).is_none(), "two rows (M4)");
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/a\ntouch /tmp/b").find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_none());
        assert!(bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t1.txt ").find(&screen("claude-permission-bash.txt")).is_none(), "trailing space");
        let c = screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt").replace(
            &format!("  $ {CODEX_CMD}\n"),
            "  $ touch ~/acp0_\n    test_t5_1.txt\n",
        );
        assert!(codex(CODEX_CMD).find(&c).is_none());
        let q = screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt").replace("Pick a color", "Pick a\ncolor");
        assert!(question("Color", "Pick a color", &["Red", "Blue"]).find(&q).is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn single_row_rules() {
        assert!(single_row("ls -la"));
        for bad in ["", "a\nb", "a\rb", "a\tb", "trailing ", "x\u{1b}[0m"] {
            assert!(!single_row(bad), "{bad:?}");
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_dialog_exposes_its_raw_region_and_option_rows() {
        let d = find_dialog(&fx("claude-permission-bash.txt")).unwrap();
        assert!(d.region.first().unwrap().trim().chars().all(|c| c == '─'));
        assert!(d.region.last().unwrap().contains("Esc to cancel"));
        let labels: Vec<_> = d.option_rows.iter().map(|&i| d.region[i].trim().to_string()).collect();
        assert!(labels[0].ends_with("1. Yes"), "{labels:?}");
        assert_eq!(d.option_rows.len(), d.options.len());
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `dialogs.rs`**

```rust
//! Binding a hook to the dialog on a pane (Dialog match contract M1 to
//! M5). Exact and fail-closed: anything not provably the hook's own
//! command or question does not match. Only `Dialog::region` and
//! `Dialog::options` are read; `Dialog::text` and `Dialog::identity` are
//! never used to decide.
use crate::herdr::screen::{Dialog, DialogKind, find_dialog};
use super::mapping::QuestionSpec;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum DialogMatch {
    ClaudeBash { command: String },
    ClaudeQuestion { spec: QuestionSpec },
    CodexCommand { command: String },
}

/// M4: a value that can only render on one row and compares exactly.
pub fn single_row(s: &str) -> bool {
    !s.is_empty() && !s.chars().any(char::is_control) && s.trim_end() == s
}

fn blank(row: &str) -> bool {
    row.trim().is_empty()
}

fn rule(row: &str) -> bool {
    let t = row.trim();
    t.chars().count() >= 10 && t.chars().all(|c| c == '─' || c == '╌')
}

fn dashed_rule(row: &str) -> bool {
    let t = row.trim();
    t.chars().count() >= 10 && t.chars().all(|c| c == '╌')
}

/// The rows above the first option.
fn head(d: &Dialog) -> Option<&[String]> {
    let first = *d.option_rows.first()?;
    Some(&d.region[..first])
}

/// M1.
fn claude_bash(d: &Dialog, command: &str) -> bool {
    if d.kind != DialogKind::ClaudePermission || !single_row(command) {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let Some(title) = rows.iter().skip(1).find(|r| !blank(r)) else { return false };
    if title.trim() != "Bash command" {
        return false;
    }
    let dashed: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| dashed_rule(r)).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [a, b] = dashed[..] else { return false };
    if b != a + 2 {
        return false; // zero rows or a wrapped (multi-row) command
    }
    rows[a + 1].strip_prefix(' ').is_some_and(|r| r.trim_end() == command)
}

/// M2.
fn codex_command(d: &Dialog, command: &str) -> bool {
    if d.kind != DialogKind::CodexPermission || !single_row(command) {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let cmd_rows: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| r.starts_with("  $ ")).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [i] = cmd_rows[..] else { return false };
    let single = rows.get(i + 1).is_none_or(|r| blank(r));
    single && rows[i]["  $ ".len()..].trim_end() == command
}

/// M3.
fn claude_question(d: &Dialog, spec: &QuestionSpec) -> bool {
    if d.kind != (DialogKind::ClaudeQuestion { tabs: false })
        || spec.multi_select
        || !single_row(&spec.question)
        || !single_row(&spec.header)
        || !spec.options.iter().all(|(l, _)| single_row(l))
    {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(rows) = head(d) else { return false };
    let headers: Vec<usize> = rows.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, r)| r.contains('☐')).map(|(i, _)| i).collect();
    let [h] = headers[..] else { return false };
    if rows[h].trim() != format!("☐ {}", spec.header) {
        return false;
    }
    let between: Vec<&String> = rows[h + 1..].iter().filter(|r| !blank(r) && !rule(r)).collect();
    if between.len() != 1 || between[0].trim() != spec.question {
        return false;
    }
    let n = spec.options.len();
    d.options.len() == n + 2
        && d.options.iter().enumerate().all(|(i, o)| usize::from(o.number) == i + 1)
        && spec.options.iter().zip(&d.options).all(|((label, _), o)| o.label == *label)
        && d.options[n].label == "Type something."
        && d.options[n + 1].label == "Chat about this"
}

impl DialogMatch {
    pub fn matches(&self, d: &Dialog) -> bool {
        match self {
            DialogMatch::ClaudeBash { command } => claude_bash(d, command),
            DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { spec } => claude_question(d, spec),
            DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command } => codex_command(d, command),
        }
    }

    pub fn find(&self, screen: &str) -> Option<Dialog> {
        find_dialog(screen).filter(|d| self.matches(d))
    }

    /// A collapsed Codex dialog: reported, never matched (M2).
    pub fn collapsed_codex(screen: &str) -> bool {
        find_dialog(screen).is_some_and(|d| d.kind == DialogKind::CodexCollapsed)
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `pub mod dialogs;`.

```rust
async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

async fn resolution(rx: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<ServerMessage>, rid: &str) -> ResolvedBy {
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == rid
        {
            return by;
        }
    }
}

fn pending_id(r: &Rig, agent: &str) -> String {
    r.agent(agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).expect("pending request")
}

const HOOKS_ONLY: &str = "claude:4e3885ee-7454-410d-a2e2-86f4dc2533c0";

/// The gate-2 fixture has a placeholder session id.
fn stop_after_deny() -> Value {
    let mut v = fx("claude/payload-Stop-after-deny.json");
    v["session_id"] = SID.into();
    v
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn allow_from_the_inbox_replies_allow_and_post_tool_use_makes_it_inbox() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &CLI).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.is_ok());
    let (status, body) = read_response(held).await;
    assert_eq!((status, body), (200, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow()));
    let mut post = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json");
    post["tool_input"] = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json")["tool_input"].clone();
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post, &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Inbox);
}

async fn an_identical_command_before_evidence_opens_a_new_occurrence() {
    // Sol I3: the first reply was delivered and awaits evidence; the same
    // command in the same turn is a new dialog (O1), with its own hold.
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let p = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let held1 = r.hold(&p, &CLI).await;
    let rid1 = until("first", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid1, "allow").await;
    read_response(held1).await;
    let held2 = r.hold(&p, &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid1).await, ResolvedBy::Uncertain, "O1: delivered, superseded before evidence");
    let rid2 = until("second", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|i| *i != rid1)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a2", &rid2, "deny").await.is_ok(), "the new occurrence has its own hold");
    assert_eq!(read_response(held2).await.1, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_deny());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_delayed_outcome_of_an_older_identical_request_does_not_resolve_the_newer_one() {
    // Sol N4, O7: R1 delivered, identical R2 opens, then R1's PostToolUse
    // arrives. It cannot be told apart from R2's, so R2 stays open.
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let p = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let held1 = r.hold(&p, &CLI).await;
    let rid1 = until("R1", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid1, "allow").await.is_ok());
    read_response(held1).await;
    let held2 = r.hold(&p, &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid1).await, ResolvedBy::Uncertain);
    let rid2 = until("R2", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|i| *i != rid1)).await;
    let mut post = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json");
    post["tool_input"] = p["tool_input"].clone();
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post, &CLI).await; // R1's delayed outcome
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid2).map(|q| q.status), Some(RequestStatus::Open), "R2 unresolved");
    // R2 still answers through its own hold.
    assert!(answer(&r, "a2", &rid2, "deny").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(read_response(held2).await.1, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_deny());
    // Identity evidence cannot confirm R2 either; its turn end decides.
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
    assert!(r.core.request(&rid2).is_some(), "a second identical PostToolUse is still ambiguous");
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &fx("claude/payload-Stop.json"), &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid2).await, ResolvedBy::Uncertain, "a turn end cannot confirm a shadowed answer");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_new_turn_clears_the_shadow() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;

    let _ = resolution(&mut rx, &rid1).await;
    let _held2 = r.hold(&p, &CLI).await;
    let rid2 = until("R2", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|i| *i != rid1)).await;
    let mut post = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json");
    post["tool_input"] = p["tool_input"].clone();
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post, &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid2).await, ResolvedBy::Terminal, "not shadowed: a terminal allow");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `deliver`**

In `hooks/mod.rs`, replace the Task 11 stub:

```rust
    /// Writes the reply into the request's hold. Nothing reached Claude if
    /// the hold is gone (closed, expired or released): `NotDelivered`, and
    /// the core puts the request back to `open`.
    async fn deliver(&self, request: &Request, answer: &Answer) -> Result<(), DeliverError> {
        use acd_protocol::Effect;
        let Answer::Option(option_id) = answer else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::NotSupported));
        };
        let Some(effect) = request.options.iter().find(|o| &o.id == option_id).map(|o| o.effect) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest));
        };
        let mut held = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held");
        let Some(h) = held.get_mut(&request.id) else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        let body = match (effect, &h.rule) {
            (Effect::Allow, _) => wire::reply_allow(),
            (Effect::Deny, _) => wire::reply_deny(),
            (Effect::AllowAlways, Some(rule)) => wire::reply_allow_always(rule),
            _ => return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::BadRequest)),
        };
        let Some(reply) = h.reply.take() else {
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        };
        h.delivered = Some((effect, tokio::time::Instant::now()));
        if reply.send(body).is_err() {
            h.delivered = None;
            return Err(DeliverError::NotDelivered(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
        }
        h.permit = None; // A1: the hold is done once its reply is written
        Ok(())
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Open held requests and attribute them**

Add to `state.rs`:

```rust
use acd_core::CorrelationKey;
use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, RequestKind, ResolvedBy};
use super::dialogs::DialogMatch;
use super::evidence::{self, Expect, Verdict};
use super::mapping;
use super::dialogs::single_row;
use super::wire::{Payload, always_rule, claude_key, command_of, tool_digest};

#[allow(dead_code)] // `HoldEnded` and `Keys` are constructed from Task 15
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum Phase {
    /// Hook path, hold open, nothing written.
    Held,
    /// `deliver` wrote the reply; awaiting evidence (O6). Derived by
    /// `phase()` from the shared `delivered` stamp, never stored.
    Delivered,
    /// Hook path, hold closed with no delivery; waiting for evidence.
    HoldEnded { since: Instant },
    /// Answer path `keys`: the herdr engine and target watch own it.
    Keys,
    /// Not answerable; ended by evidence or the dialog leaving the screen.
    ReadOnly,
}

#[allow(dead_code)] // `matcher`, `pane`, `capture`, `absent`, `next_check`, `read_seq` are read from Task 15
struct Track {
    agent_id: String,
    session_id: String,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    /// G3: unique per occurrence; tickets carry it.
    op: u64,
    /// G3: bumped for every spawned read; only the latest result applies.
    read_seq: u64,
    payload: Payload,
    expect: Expect,
    /// The exact screen match for this hook (M1), if it can have one.
    matcher: Option<DialogMatch>,
    pane: Option<PaneFact>,
    phase: Phase,
    opened_at: Instant,
    /// The detection snapshot that matched this request's dialog.
    capture: Option<String>,
    absent: u8,
    next_check: Instant,
    /// O7: an older identical occurrence of the session may still produce
    /// evidence; identity evidence never resolves this one.
    shadowed: bool,
    /// O7: this occurrence consumed identity evidence of its own.
    identity_seen: bool,
}

/// O7: identities of a session's occurrences that ended without their
/// identity evidence.
#[derive(Default)]
struct Unconsumed {
    entries: std::collections::VecDeque<(Expect, Instant)>,
    /// Set when an entry was dropped at the cap; until the next turn end
    /// or `unconsumed_ttl`, every new occurrence of the session is shadowed.
    shadow_all: Option<Instant>,
}

const MAX_UNCONSUMED: usize = 8;
```

`holds: HashMap<HoldId, String>`, `next_op: u64` and
`unconsumed: HashMap<String, Unconsumed>`, empty or zero in `new`.
