The new methods:

```rust
    fn fallback_key(&self, agent_id: &str) -> Option<CorrelationKey> {
        self.core
            .pending_key(agent_id)
            .filter(|(_, _, kind)| *kind == RequestKind::BlockedUnparsed)
            .map(|(_, key, _)| key)
    }

    /// Observes under the per-agent delivery lock for herdr agents (D7).
    async fn observe_locked(&self, agent_id: &str, herdr_agent: bool, obs: Observation) {
        let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().filter(|_| herdr_agent).map(|h| h.agent_locks());
        let _g = match &locks {
            Some(l) => Some(l.lock(agent_id).await),
            None => None,
        };
        self.core.observe(obs);
    }

    fn bound_pane(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<PaneFact> {
        let Owner::Herdr(agent) = &self.sessions.get(sid)?.owner else { return None };
        self.panes().into_iter().find(|p| p.agent_id == *agent)
    }

    async fn open_claude_permission(&mut self, event: HookEvent, hold: Option<HoldIn>) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        // O1: a new dialog means the session's previous one ended.
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await;
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload);
        if hold.is_none() {
            // Over the hold cap: no hook path. A bound pane can still be
            // answered with keys once its dialog is captured.
            if pane.is_none() {
                return;
            }
            obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
            obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
            obs.ready = false;
        }
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        let tool_name = event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default();
        // M1: only a Bash command that renders on one row can ever match.
        let matcher = command_of(&event.payload.tool_input)
            .filter(|c| tool_name == "Bash" && single_row(c))
            .map(|c| DialogMatch::ClaudeBash { command: c.to_owned() });
        // The rule the client was shown (Task 7), never rebuilt here.
        let rule = always_rule(&event.payload).filter(|r| mapping::rule_displayable(&mapping::rule_text(r)));
        let phase = match hold {
            Some((hold_id, reply, permit)) => {
                self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").insert(rid.clone(), HeldReq {
                    hold: hold_id,
                    reply: Some(reply),
                    rule,
                    delivered: None,
                    permit: Some(permit),
                });
                self.holds.insert(hold_id, rid.clone());
                Phase::Held
            }
            None => Phase::ReadOnly, // becomes Keys once captured
        };
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.next_op += 1;
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            op: self.next_op,
            read_seq: 0,
            expect: Expect::ClaudeTool {
                session_id: sid,
                digest: tool_digest(&tool_name, &event.payload.tool_input),
                tool_name,
            },
            matcher,
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
            shadowed: false,
            identity_seen: false,
        });
        self.mark_shadow(&rid);
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// O7: called by every opening path right after inserting the track,
    /// after O1/O3 ended the older occurrences.
    fn mark_shadow(&mut self, rid: &str) {
        let shadowed = self.tracks.get(rid).is_some_and(|t| {
            self.unconsumed.get(&t.session_id).is_some_and(|u| {
                u.shadow_all.is_some() || u.entries.iter().any(|(e, _)| evidence::same_identity(e, &t.expect))
            })
        });
        if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) {
            t.shadowed = shadowed;
        }
    }

    /// O7: remembers an occurrence that ended without identity evidence.
    fn note_unconsumed(&mut self, t: &Track) {
        if t.identity_seen {
            return;
        }
        let u = self.unconsumed.entry(t.session_id.clone()).or_default();
        u.entries.push_back((t.expect.clone(), Instant::now()));
        if u.entries.len() > MAX_UNCONSUMED {
            u.entries.pop_front();
            u.shadow_all = Some(Instant::now());
        }
    }


    fn delivered(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<(Effect, Instant)> {
        self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get(rid).and_then(|h| h.delivered)
    }

    /// O6: `Held` becomes `Delivered` once `deliver` stamped the reply.
    fn phase(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<Phase> {
        let t = self.tracks.get(rid)?;
        Some(match t.phase {
            Phase::Held if self.delivered(rid).is_some() => Phase::Delivered,
            p => p,
        })
    }

    /// O1: before a new occurrence of `sid` opens, its open one ends.
    /// Held (nothing written) and every screen-bound phase resolve
    /// `terminal`; Delivered resolves `uncertain` (D10). Holds are released
    /// empty and targets retired (inside the delivery lock).
    async fn end_session_occurrences(&mut self, sid: &str) {
        let ids: Vec<String> = self.tracks.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let by = match self.phase(&rid) {
                Some(Phase::Delivered) => ResolvedBy::Uncertain,
                _ => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
            };
            let agent = self.tracks.get(&rid).map(|t| t.agent_id.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
            let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
            let _g = match &locks {
                Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                None => None,
            };
            if let Some(h) = &self.herdr {
                h.keys_targets().remove(&rid);
            }
            if by == ResolvedBy::Uncertain {
                tracing::debug!(request_id = %rid, "superseded before its evidence arrived (D10)");
            }
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
    }

    /// Ends a request and forgets it; dropping its reply sender releases a
    /// still-open hold with an empty reply.
    fn end(&mut self, rid: &str, by: ResolvedBy) {
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestEndedById { request_id: rid.to_owned(), by });
        self.forget(rid);
    }

    /// Forgets a request. Its occurrence counts as confirmed gone whatever
    /// the outcome (spec §5.1: the hook source reported the end), so a
    /// repeat of the same reusable key opens a new request (D2).
    fn forget(&mut self, rid: &str) {
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").remove(rid) {
            self.holds.remove(&h.hold);
        }
        self.core.observe(Observation::DialogGoneById { request_id: rid.to_owned() });
        if let Some(t) = self.tracks.remove(rid) {
            self.note_unconsumed(&t); // O7
        }
    }

    /// Drops tracks whose request the registry already resolved
    /// (superseded, agent gone, migrated away with a conflict).
    fn sweep(&mut self) {
        let dead: Vec<String> = self.tracks.keys().filter(|rid| self.core.request(rid).is_none()).cloned().collect();
        for rid in dead {
            if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
                tracing::debug!(request_id = %rid, "request ended before its evidence arrived (D10)");
            }
            self.forget(&rid);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §5.1 and §6.2 step 3 for requests the hook path owns, with O7.
    fn apply_evidence(&mut self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        let sid = ev.payload.session_id.clone();
        let ids: Vec<String> = self
            .tracks
            .iter()
            .filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid && t.phase != Phase::Keys && ev.at >= t.opened_at)
            .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
            .collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let Some(seen) = self.tracks.get(&rid).and_then(|t| evidence::observe_shadowed(&t.expect, ev, t.shadowed)) else {
                continue;
            };
            if evidence::is_identity(&seen)
                && let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid)
            {
                t.identity_seen = true;
            }
            let by = match self.delivered(&rid) {
                Some((_, at)) if ev.at < at => continue,
                // O7: with identity evidence ignored, a turn end cannot
                // confirm or contradict what acd sent.
                Some(_) if self.tracks.get(&rid).is_some_and(|t| t.shadowed) => ResolvedBy::Uncertain,
                Some((effect, _)) => match evidence::sent_for_effect(effect).map(|s| evidence::verdict(&s, &seen)) {
                    Some(Verdict::Consistent) => ResolvedBy::Inbox,
                    _ => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
                },
                // The dialog ended without any acd delivery.
                None => ResolvedBy::Terminal,
            };
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
        // O7: a turn end clears the session's unconsumed identities; an
        // identity event consumes the oldest matching one.
        if matches!(ev.name, HookName::Stop | HookName::UserPromptSubmit) {
            self.unconsumed.remove(&sid);
        } else if let Some(u) = self.unconsumed.get_mut(&sid)
            && let Some(i) = u.entries.iter().position(|(e, _)| evidence::observe(e, ev).is_some_and(|s| evidence::is_identity(&s)))
        {
            u.entries.remove(i);
        }
    }

```

Extend `on_tick` (before the session sweep):

```rust
        self.sweep();
        let window = self.cfg.evidence_window;
        let mut ends = Vec::new();
        for (rid, t) in self.tracks.iter() {
            let delivered = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get(rid).and_then(|h| h.delivered);
            match (t.phase, delivered) {
                (_, Some((_, at))) if now.duration_since(at) >= window && t.phase != Phase::Keys => {
                    ends.push((rid.clone(), ResolvedBy::Uncertain));
                }
                (Phase::HoldEnded { since }, None) if now.duration_since(since) >= window => {
                    ends.push((rid.clone(), ResolvedBy::Uncertain));
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        for (rid, by) in ends {
            self.end(&rid, by);
        }
        // O7: unconsumed identities expire.
        let ttl = self.cfg.unconsumed_ttl;
        self.unconsumed.retain(|_, u| {
            u.entries.retain(|(_, at)| now.duration_since(*at) < ttl);
            if u.shadow_all.is_some_and(|at| now.duration_since(at) >= ttl) {
                u.shadow_all = None;
            }
            !u.entries.is_empty() || u.shadow_all.is_some()
        });
```

T3 takeover and agent removal reach holds through `sweep` (the request
is gone from the registry, so its reply sender drops). Call
`self.sweep()` at the end of `apply_owner` too, so a hold is released in
the same turn as the takeover.

### Task 15: Read tickets; hold close and expiry, capture, and the switch to keys

Spec §6.2 steps 4 to 6, §12 rows 2 and 3, the Read ticket contract (G1
to G6; Sol B2), read admission and cancellation (A3, A4; Sol N2, N3) and
Sol minor 1. GATE 2 (2b): the held connection closes
about 15 ms after a terminal deny or Esc and stays open on a terminal
allow, so a close means deny, Esc, interruption or process death; the
terminal allow is handled by Task 14's `PostToolUse` evidence.

- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/tickets.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 6, 13, 14.

  `Purpose`, `ReadResult`, `Internal::Screen { ticket, purpose, result, permit }`,
  `ticket_for`, `bind_gen_of`, `spawn_read(rid, purpose)`, `now_for`,
  `on_screen` (revalidates inside the delivery lock),
  `switch_to_keys_locked(rid, screen, incarnation)`.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_hooks_only_close_is_terminal_on_evidence_and_uncertain_without() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &CLI).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    drop(held); // terminal deny or Esc closes the hold
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestGone),
        "a closed hold can never take an inbox answer");
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &stop_after_deny(), &CLI).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Terminal);

    let mut second = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    second["tool_input"]["command"] = "touch /tmp/acp0/t9.txt".into();
    let held = r.hold(&second, &CLI).await;
    let rid2 = until("second", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|i| *i != rid)).await;
    drop(held);

fn bound_headers(r: &Rig) -> Vec<(&'static str, String)> {
    vec![("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p1".into()), ("X-Herdr-Socket", r.sock()), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli".into())]
}

async fn bound_rig() -> (Rig, String) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &dialog);
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "visible", &dialog);
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    (r, agent)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_herdr_bound_close_with_the_dialog_on_screen_switches_to_keys() {
    let (r, agent) = bound_rig().await;
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("request on the herdr agent", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::Hook);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await; // capture
    assert!(r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid).is_none(), "no target while the hold is open");
    drop(held); // terminal Esc closed the hold, but the dialog is still up
    let req = until("keys path", || r.core.request(&rid).filter(|q| q.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys)).await;
    assert_eq!(req.options.iter().map(|o| o.id.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), vec!["allow", "deny"], "D9");
    assert!(req.revision >= 2);
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    let res = answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await;
    assert!(res.is_ok(), "{res:?}");
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]

    r.hook("claude", "Notification", &fx("claude/payload-Notification.json"), &CLI).await; // any hook re-binds

/// Unheld permissions (hold cap 0) go straight to the keys path once
/// captured, so a stale capture would register a target: the sharpest
/// stale-read probe. The pane is `working`, so herdr's own fallback and
/// watch never read it and the gate catches P3's capture read.
async fn stale_rig() -> (Rig, String, Vec<(&'static str, String)>) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "working")], |c| c.max_holds = 0).await;
    let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &dialog);
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    (r, agent, h)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_capture_after_stop_is_dropped() {
    let (r, agent, h) = stale_rig().await;
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    let gate = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().block_next("agent.read");
    r.hook("claude", "PermissionRequest", &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    gate.arrived().await;
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &fx("claude/payload-Stop.json"), &h).await; // the occurrence ends
    until("ended", || r.core.request(&rid).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    gate.release();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert!(r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid).is_none(), "the stale capture did nothing");
    assert!(r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).is_none());
}
```

The admission test (Sol N3, A3). One read slot; another request's read
holds it, so this request's hold-end read is not admitted at first. It
must run once the slot frees, and the switch to keys must happen:

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_read_that_waits_for_a_slot_still_switches_to_keys() {
    let r = rig(
        &[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked"), fake_agent("w1:p2", "claude", "blocked")],
        |c| {
            c.max_reads = 1;
            c.held_dialog_poll = Duration::from_secs(30); // no presence reads in this test
        },
    )
    .await;
    let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    for p in ["w1:p1", "w1:p2"] {
        f.set_screen(p, "detection", &dialog);
        f.set_screen(p, "visible", &dialog);
    }
    let sock = r.sock();
    let on = |pane: &'static str| vec![("X-Herdr-Pane", pane), ("X-Herdr-Socket", sock.as_str()), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
    let agent_of = |pane: &str| {
        r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().iter().find(|(_, p)| p.pane_id == pane).unwrap().0.clone()
    };
    let (a1, a2) = (agent_of("w1:p1"), agent_of("w1:p2"));
    // Request A on p1: held and captured.
    let held_a = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &on("w1:p1")).await;
    let rid_a = until("A", || r.agent(&a1).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(700)).await; // A's capture finished
    // Request B (another session) on p2: its capture takes the only slot and blocks.
    let gate = f.block_next("agent.read");
    let mut b = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    b["session_id"] = "5f4996ff-0000-4000-8000-000000000002".into();
    let _held_b = r.hold(&b, &on("w1:p2")).await;
    until("B", || r.agent(&a2).and_then(|a| a.pending)).await;
    gate.arrived().await;
    // A's hold closes: its hold-end read finds no slot and is only wanted.
    drop(held_a);
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert!(r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid_a).is_none(), "not yet: no slot");
    gate.release();
    until("A switched to keys", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid_a)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid_a).map(|q| q.answer_path), Some(AnswerPath::Keys));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Read tickets (`tickets.rs`)**

```rust
//! Read tickets (Read ticket contract G1 to G6, Sol B2). A spawned screen
//! read carries what must still hold when its result comes back; the
//! reconciler gathers `Now` inside the delivery lock and applies the
//! result only if `still_valid`.
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use acd_protocol::{AnswerPath, RequestStatus};
use tokio::task::AbortHandle;

/// G3: what a read is for. Each track has at most one outstanding read
/// per kind, with its own sequence.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub(crate) enum ReadKind {
    Capture,
    Presence,
    HoldEnded,
    /// Codex bind reads (C2), used instead of `Capture` for Codex tracks.
    CodexBind,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct Slot {
    seq: u64,
    task: Option<AbortHandle>,
    wanted: bool,
}

/// Per-kind read bookkeeping of one track (G3, A3, A4).
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub(crate) struct ReadSlots(BTreeMap<ReadKind, Slot>);

impl ReadSlots {
    /// A3: `admitted` says whether a read permit was obtained. Admitted:
    /// aborts the older read of this kind (A4), bumps and returns the
    /// sequence. Not admitted: marks the kind wanted and changes nothing
    /// else (Sol N3).
    pub fn admit(&mut self, kind: ReadKind, admitted: bool) -> Option<u64> {
        let slot = self.0.entry(kind).or_default();
        if !admitted {
            slot.wanted = true;
            return None;
        }
        if let Some(t) = slot.task.take() {
            t.abort();
        }
        slot.wanted = false;
        slot.seq += 1;
        Some(slot.seq)
    }
    pub fn started(&mut self, kind: ReadKind, task: AbortHandle) {
        self.0.entry(kind).or_default().task = Some(task);
    }
    /// The read of `kind` with `seq` finished (its result was taken).
    pub fn finished(&mut self, kind: ReadKind, seq: u64) {
        if let Some(slot) = self.0.get_mut(&kind)
            && slot.seq == seq
        {
            slot.task = None;
        }
    }
    pub fn latest(&self, kind: ReadKind) -> u64 {
        self.0.get(&kind).map_or(0, |s| s.seq)
    }
    /// A read of `kind` is running.
    pub fn busy(&self, kind: ReadKind) -> bool {
        self.0.get(&kind).is_some_and(|s| s.task.is_some())
    }
    pub fn wanted(&self) -> Vec<ReadKind> {
        self.0.iter().filter(|(_, s)| s.wanted).map(|(k, _)| *k).collect()
    }
    pub fn unwant(&mut self, kind: ReadKind) {
        if let Some(s) = self.0.get_mut(&kind) {
            s.wanted = false;
        }
    }
    /// A4: the track ended or was revoked.
    pub fn abort_all(&mut self) {
        for s in self.0.values_mut() {
            if let Some(t) = s.task.take() {
                t.abort();
            }
            s.wanted = false;
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for ReadSlots {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.abort_all();
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Ticket {
    pub agent_id: String,
    pub incarnation: u64,
    pub session_id: String,
    pub bind_gen: u64,
    /// `None` for a Codex binding watch, which runs before a request exists.
    pub request_id: Option<String>,
    pub op: u64,
    pub kind: ReadKind,
    pub read_seq: u64,
}

/// What holds now, gathered inside the delivery lock of `agent_id`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) struct Now {
    /// The pane map's incarnation for the agent, if it is still there.
    pub incarnation: Option<u64>,
    /// The session's current binding generation.
    pub bind_gen: Option<u64>,
    pub t3_owned: bool,
    /// `(op, latest read_seq for the ticket's kind)` of the ticket's
    /// track, if it still exists (for a Codex watch: the session's watch
    /// sequence, with `op = 0`).
    pub track: Option<(u64, u64)>,
    /// The request's status and path, if it still exists.
    pub request: Option<(RequestStatus, AnswerPath)>,
}

/// G5. `paths`: the answer paths a result may act on.
pub(crate) fn still_valid(t: &Ticket, now: &Now, paths: &[AnswerPath]) -> bool {
    let common = now.incarnation == Some(t.incarnation) && now.bind_gen == Some(t.bind_gen) && !now.t3_owned;
    match &t.request_id {
        None => common && now.track == Some((t.op, t.read_seq)),
        Some(_) => {
            common
                && now.track == Some((t.op, t.read_seq))
                && now.request.is_some_and(|(st, path)| st == RequestStatus::Open && paths.contains(&path))
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn ticket() -> Ticket {
        Ticket {
            agent_id: "herdr:h:w1:p1".into(), incarnation: 7, session_id: "s".into(), bind_gen: 3,
            request_id: Some("r1".into()), op: 11, kind: ReadKind::Capture, read_seq: 2,
        }
    }
    fn now() -> Now {
        Now {
            incarnation: Some(7), bind_gen: Some(3), t3_owned: false, track: Some((11, 2)),
            request: Some((RequestStatus::Open, AnswerPath::Hook)),
        }
    }
    const P: &[AnswerPath] = &[AnswerPath::Hook];

    #[test]
    fn a_fresh_ticket_is_valid() {
        assert!(still_valid(&ticket(), &now(), P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_change_invalidates() {
        let t = ticket();
        for (why, n) in [
            ("pane replaced", Now { incarnation: Some(8), ..now() }),
            ("pane gone", Now { incarnation: None, ..now() }),
            ("rebound", Now { bind_gen: Some(4), ..now() }),
            ("T3 took over", Now { t3_owned: true, ..now() }),
            ("occurrence ended (Stop, new prompt)", Now { track: None, ..now() }),
            ("another occurrence", Now { track: Some((12, 0)), ..now() }),
            ("request answering", Now { request: Some((RequestStatus::Answering, AnswerPath::Hook)), ..now() }),
            ("request resolved", Now { request: None, ..now() }),
            ("path changed", Now { request: Some((RequestStatus::Open, AnswerPath::None)), ..now() }),
        ] {
            assert!(!still_valid(&t, &n, P), "{why}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn out_of_order_reads_keep_the_latest() {
        // Two reads were spawned (read_seq 1, then 2); 1 completes last.
        let older = Ticket { read_seq: 1, ..ticket() };
        assert!(!still_valid(&older, &now(), P), "only the latest read applies");
        assert!(still_valid(&ticket(), &now(), P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_watch_without_a_request_checks_pane_binding_and_owner() {
        let w = Ticket { request_id: None, op: 0, kind: ReadKind::CodexBind, read_seq: 1, ..ticket() };
        assert!(still_valid(&w, &Now { track: Some((0, 1)), request: None, ..now() }, P));
        assert!(!still_valid(&w, &Now { track: Some((0, 2)), request: None, ..now() }, P), "a newer watch");
        assert!(!still_valid(&w, &Now { bind_gen: Some(9), track: Some((0, 1)), request: None, ..now() }, P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_read_that_is_not_admitted_does_not_bump_the_sequence() {
        // Sol N3: a skipped presence read must not make the outstanding
        // capture stale.
        let mut s = ReadSlots::default();
        assert_eq!(s.admit(ReadKind::Capture, true), Some(1));
        assert_eq!(s.admit(ReadKind::Presence, false), None);
        assert_eq!(s.latest(ReadKind::Capture), 1);
        assert_eq!(s.latest(ReadKind::Presence), 0);
        assert_eq!(s.wanted(), vec![ReadKind::Presence]);
        let capture = Ticket { kind: ReadKind::Capture, read_seq: 1, ..ticket() };
        let n = Now { track: Some((11, s.latest(ReadKind::Capture))), ..now() };
        assert!(still_valid(&capture, &n, P));
    }

    #[test]
    fn reads_of_different_kinds_do_not_invalidate_each_other() {
        let mut s = ReadSlots::default();
        let c = s.admit(ReadKind::Capture, true).unwrap();
        let h = s.admit(ReadKind::HoldEnded, true).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((c, h), (1, 1));
        assert_eq!(s.latest(ReadKind::Capture), 1, "a hold-end read leaves the capture current");
        assert_eq!(s.admit(ReadKind::Capture, true), Some(2), "a newer capture supersedes the older one");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn admitting_a_newer_read_aborts_the_older_task() {
        let mut s = ReadSlots::default();
        s.admit(ReadKind::Presence, true);
        let old = tokio::spawn(std::future::pending::<()>());
        s.started(ReadKind::Presence, old.abort_handle());
        s.admit(ReadKind::Presence, true);
        assert!(old.await.unwrap_err().is_cancelled(), "A4");
    }
}
```

`mod.rs` adds `mod tickets;`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Spawn reads with tickets and revalidate their results**

Remove the two `#[allow(dead_code)]` lines Task 14 put on `Phase` and
`Track`. In `Track`, replace `read_seq: u64` with `reads: ReadSlots`
(`ReadSlots::default()` in every constructor; dropping a track aborts its
reads, A4), and add `wanted_purpose: HashMap<ReadKind, Purpose>` (the
purpose to retry for a wanted kind) and `hold_end_tries: u8` (0 at
open). `on_tick` calls `self.retry_wanted()` after its other work, and
`spawn_scan` skips while `scanning` is set, sets it, and sends its
result with `blocking_send` (the `Scanned` arm clears it). `Reconciler`
gains `reads: Arc<tokio::sync::Semaphore>`
(`Semaphore::new(cfg.max_reads)`, A3). The internal channel becomes
`mpsc::channel(cfg.max_reads + 2)` (A3): every sender holds a read
permit or is the single scan task (`scanning: bool` on the reconciler
keeps scans one at a time), so `send` never waits long; senders use
`send(..).await`. Add to `state.rs`:

```rust
use acd_protocol::RequestStatus;
use tokio::sync::{OwnedSemaphorePermit, Semaphore};
use super::tickets::{Now, ReadKind, ReadSlots, Ticket, still_valid};
use crate::herdr::client::CALL_TIMEOUT;
use crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget;
use crate::herdr::reconciler::PaneRef;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum Purpose {
    /// Find this request's dialog within `capture_window`.
    Capture,
    /// One read: is the matched dialog still shown?
    Presence,
    /// One read after the hold closed (`expired: false`) or expired.
    HoldEnded { expired: bool },
}

impl Purpose {
    pub(crate) fn kind(self) -> ReadKind {
        match self {
            Purpose::Capture => ReadKind::Capture,
            Purpose::Presence => ReadKind::Presence,
            Purpose::HoldEnded { .. } => ReadKind::HoldEnded,
        }
    }
}

/// What a read found. `Unavailable` (failed or truncated) is never taken
/// as proof that a dialog is gone (Sol minor 1).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum ReadResult {
    Matched(String),
    NoMatch,
    Unavailable,
}

// `Internal` gains (the permit is released once the result is processed, A3):
//     Screen { ticket: Ticket, purpose: Purpose, result: ReadResult, permit: OwnedSemaphorePermit },
```

The read side:

```rust
    /// Checks that `rid`'s track can be read for `kind` (track, pane,
    /// incarnation, matcher). Pure: bumps nothing.
    fn read_target(&self, rid: &str) -> Option<(PaneRef, DialogMatch)> {
        let herdr = self.herdr.clone()?;
        let t = self.tracks.get(rid)?;
        let pane = t.pane.clone()?;
        let matcher = t.matcher.clone()?;
        let pref = herdr.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&pane.agent_id).cloned()?;
        // Replaced since binding: never read on its behalf.
        (pref.incarnation == pane.incarnation).then_some((pref, matcher))
    }

    /// Issues a ticket for an admitted read of `kind`. Call only after a
    /// read permit was obtained: this bumps the kind's sequence (Sol N3).
    fn ticket_for(&mut self, rid: &str, kind: ReadKind) -> Option<Ticket> {
        let t = self.tracks.get_mut(rid)?;
        let read_seq = t.reads.admit(kind, true)?;
        let ticket = Ticket {
            agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
            incarnation: t.pane.as_ref()?.incarnation,
            session_id: t.session_id.clone(),
            bind_gen: 0, // filled below
            request_id: Some(rid.to_owned()),
            op: t.op,
            kind,
            read_seq,
        };
        let bind_gen = self.bind_gen_of(&ticket.session_id)?;
        Some(Ticket { bind_gen, ..ticket })
    }

    /// G2 for Claude sessions; Task 17 extends it to Codex sessions.
    fn bind_gen_of(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.sessions.get(sid).map(|s| s.bind_gen)
    }

    /// Spawns one read (A3): only with a permit, never waiting for one.
    /// Without a permit the kind is marked wanted and `on_tick` retries
    /// it; nothing else changes. `Capture` polls until the matcher finds
    /// the dialog or `capture_window` passes; the others read once.
    fn spawn_read(&mut self, rid: &str, purpose: Purpose) {
        let kind = purpose.kind();
        let Some((pref, matcher)) = self.read_target(rid) else { return };
        let Ok(permit) = self.reads.clone().try_acquire_owned() else {
            if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) {
                t.reads.admit(kind, false); // wanted; the sequence is unchanged
                t.wanted_purpose.insert(kind, purpose);
            }
            return;
        };
        let Some(ticket) = self.ticket_for(rid, kind) else { return };
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let (window, poll) = (self.cfg.capture_window, self.cfg.capture_poll);
        let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let deadline = Instant::now() + window;
            loop {
                let read = pref
                    .herdr
                    .read_screen(&pref.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT)
                    .await
                    .ok()
                    .filter(|r| !r.truncated);
                let result = match read {
                    None => ReadResult::Unavailable,
                    Some(r) if matcher.find(&r.text).is_some() => ReadResult::Matched(r.text),
                    Some(_) => ReadResult::NoMatch,
                };
                let retry = purpose == Purpose::Capture
                    && !matches!(result, ReadResult::Matched(_))
                    && Instant::now() < deadline;
                if !retry {
                    let _ = tx.send(Internal::Screen { ticket, purpose, result, permit }).await;
                    return;
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            }
        });
        if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) {
            t.reads.started(kind, task.abort_handle());
        }
    }

    /// A3: retries wanted reads, at most `batch` per tick.
    fn retry_wanted(&mut self) {
        let mut todo = Vec::new();
        for (rid, t) in self.tracks.iter() {
            for kind in t.reads.wanted() {
                if let Some(p) = t.wanted_purpose.get(&kind) {
                    todo.push((rid.clone(), *p));
                }
            }
        }
        for (rid, purpose) in todo.into_iter().take(self.cfg.batch) {
            if self.reads.available_permits() == 0 {
                break;
            }
            if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                t.reads.unwant(purpose.kind());
            }
            self.spawn_read(&rid, purpose);
        }
    }

    /// G5's inputs, read inside the delivery lock of `t.agent_id`.
    fn now_for(&self, t: &Ticket) -> Now {
        let incarnation = self
            .herdr
            .as_ref()
            .and_then(|h| h.panes().read().expect("pane map").get(&t.agent_id).map(|p| p.incarnation));
        let track = t.request_id.as_ref().and_then(|rid| self.tracks.get(rid)).map(|tr| (tr.op, tr.reads.latest(t.kind)));
        let request = t.request_id.as_ref().and_then(|rid| self.core.request(rid)).map(|r| (r.status, r.answer_path));
        Now {
            incarnation,
            bind_gen: self.bind_gen_of(&t.session_id),
            t3_owned: self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&t.session_id).is_some()),
            track,
            request,
        }
    }
```

The result side, all inside the delivery lock (G5):

```rust
    async fn on_screen(&mut self, ticket: Ticket, purpose: Purpose, result: ReadResult) {
        // The read finished: its slot no longer holds a task (the caller
        // drops the permit after this returns, A3).
        if let Some(rid) = &ticket.request_id
            && let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid)
        {
            t.reads.finished(ticket.kind, ticket.read_seq);
        }
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let Ok(_g) = tokio::time::timeout(self.cfg.lock_wait, locks.lock(&ticket.agent_id)).await else {
            // A7: never wait longer than `lock_wait` for a read result; read again next tick.
            if let Some(rid) = &ticket.request_id
                && let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid)
            {
                t.reads.admit(ticket.kind, false);
                t.wanted_purpose.insert(ticket.kind, purpose);
            }
            return;
        };
        let paths: &[AnswerPath] = match purpose {
            Purpose::Capture => &[AnswerPath::Hook, AnswerPath::Keys, AnswerPath::None],
            _ => &[AnswerPath::Hook, AnswerPath::None],
        };
        if !still_valid(&ticket, &self.now_for(&ticket), paths) {
            tracing::debug!(?purpose, "dropping a stale screen read");
            return;
        }
        let rid = ticket.request_id.clone().expect("track reads carry a request id");
        match (purpose, result) {
            (Purpose::Capture, ReadResult::Matched(screen)) => {
                if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid) {
                    h.bound_captured = true;
                }
                let unheld = self.tracks.get(&rid).is_some_and(|t| t.phase == Phase::ReadOnly);
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                    t.capture = Some(screen.clone());
                }
                if unheld {
                    self.switch_to_keys_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation); // over the hold cap
                }
            }
            (Purpose::Capture, _) => {} // no match: hold-only (spec §6.2 step 6)
            (Purpose::Presence, ReadResult::Unavailable) => {}
            (Purpose::Presence, r) => {
                let gone = {
                    let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
                    t.absent = if matches!(r, ReadResult::Matched(_)) { 0 } else { t.absent + 1 };
                    t.absent >= 2
                };
                if gone && self.delivered(&rid).is_none() {
                    self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
                }
            }
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { .. }, ReadResult::Matched(screen)) => {
                self.switch_to_keys_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation);
            }
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { .. }, ReadResult::NoMatch) => self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Terminal),
            (Purpose::HoldEnded { expired }, ReadResult::Unavailable) => {
                // Unavailable is not "gone" (Sol minor 1): retry, then uncertain.
                let tries = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid).map(|t| {
                    t.hold_end_tries += 1;
                    t.hold_end_tries
                });
                if tries.is_some_and(|n| n < 3) {
                    self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::HoldEnded { expired });
                } else {
                    self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Uncertain);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn on_hold_ended(&mut self, hold: HoldId, expired: bool) {
        let Some(rid) = self.holds.remove(&hold) else { return };
        if let Some(h) = self.shared.held.lock().expect("held").get_mut(&rid) {
            h.reply = None; // a later deliver gets RequestGone or RequestNotReady
            h.permit = None; // A1: the close or expiry is consumed
        }
        if self.delivered(&rid).is_some() {
            return; // evidence decides
        }
        let now = Instant::now();
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) else { return };
        let captured = t.pane.is_some() && t.capture.is_some();
        t.phase = Phase::HoldEnded { since: now };
        if captured {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::HoldEnded { expired });
        } else if expired {
            // Hooks-only, or bound without a matched dialog (spec §6.2 step 6).
            self.end(&rid, ResolvedBy::Lost);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §6.2 steps 4 and 5, inside the delivery lock the caller holds:
    /// rebuild for keys (D9), register a target bound to the pane
    /// incarnation, and only then mark it ready.
    fn switch_to_keys_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String, incarnation: u64) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Some(dialog) = crate::herdr::screen::find_dialog(&screen) else { return };
        let mut obs = mapping::claude_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload);
        obs.answer_path = AnswerPath::Keys;
        obs.options = mapping::claude_keys_options();
        obs.ready = false;
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, mapping::claude_permission_keys(&dialog),
        )
        .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation));
        let open_on_keys = self
            .core
            .request(rid)
            .is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Ok(target) if open_on_keys && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            }
            _ => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                    agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                    key: t.key.clone(),
                    answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
                });
                t.phase = Phase::ReadOnly;
            }
        }
    }
```

A claim that lands after the hold closed but before the switch must not
say "answered elsewhere" while the dialog is still up. `HeldReq` (Task
11) gains `bound_captured: bool` (`false` at open; set by a matched
capture above). In `deliver` (Task 14), report `RequestNotReady` for
such a request, so the client retries for 3 s with the same command id
(spec §8) and lands on the keys path:

```rust
        let gone = |h: &HeldReq| {
            DeliverError::NotDelivered(if h.bound_captured { ErrorCode::RequestNotReady } else { ErrorCode::RequestGone })
        };
        // ...
        let Some(reply) = h.reply.take() else {
            return Err(gone(h));
        };
        // ...
        if reply.send(body).is_err() {
            h.delivered = None;
            return Err(gone(h));
        }
```

In `open_claude_permission`, before the final `self.sweep();`:

```rust
        if pane.is_some() {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture);
        }
```

Extend `on_internal`:

```rust
            Internal::Screen { ticket, purpose, result, permit } => {
                self.on_screen(ticket, purpose, result).await;
                drop(permit); // A3: released only once the result is processed
            }
```

In `on_tick`, iterate with `self.tracks.iter_mut()` and add the presence
arm after the two evidence arms:

```rust
        let mut reads = Vec::new();
        // inside the match:
                (Phase::Held | Phase::ReadOnly, None) if t.capture.is_some() && now >= t.next_check => {
                    t.next_check = now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll;

                }

```

A migration moves the request to the herdr agent (Task 2), so the track
must follow it. In `apply_owner`'s `Owner::Herdr` branch, right after
observing `AgentMigrated` (still inside that agent's lock), re-point every
track of the session and start a capture on the new pane. `bind_gen`
already moved, so reads issued for the hooks-only phase are dropped:

```rust
                    let pane = self.panes().into_iter().find(|p| p.agent_id == agent);
                    let moved: Vec<String> = self
                        .tracks
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid && t.agent_id == hooks_id)
                        .map(|(k, _)| k.clone())
                        .collect();
                    for rid in moved {
                        if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                            t.agent_id = agent.clone();
                            t.pane = pane.clone();
                        }
                        if pane.is_some() {
                            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture);
                        }
                    }
```

(`apply_owner` takes `sid: &str`; clone `hooks_id` and `agent` before
the borrow of `self.sessions` ends.)

```rust
    fn target(rid: &str) -> crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget {
        let dialog = std::fs::read_to_string(format!(
            "{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-permission-bash.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        crate::herdr::keys::KeysTarget::from_screen(
            rid.into(), "a".into(), acd_core::CorrelationKey("k".into()), &dialog,
            [("allow".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("deny".to_string(), "4".to_string())].into()).unwrap()

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_hub_uses_only_evidence_after_the_key_and_for_this_request() {
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = EvidenceHub::default();
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"), false);
        let mut post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json", HookName::PostToolUse);
        hub.publish(&post); // before the key: ignored
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        assert_eq!(
            tokio::time::timeout(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100), hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at)).await.ok(),
            None, "an event before sent_at is not evidence");
        post.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&post); // after the key: in the backlog
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::Consistent);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "deny", sent_at).await, Verdict::Contradicts);
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r2"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence, "never reused across ids");
        hub.forget("r1");
        assert_eq!(hub.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await, Verdict::NoEvidence);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn the_hub_waits_for_live_evidence() {
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = std::sync::Arc::new(EvidenceHub::default());
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"), false);
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let h2 = hub.clone();
        let waiting = tokio::spawn(async move { h2.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await });
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        let mut stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        stop.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&stop);
        assert_eq!(waiting.await.unwrap(), Verdict::Contradicts, "allow, then the turn ended without the tool");
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn a_shadowed_expectation_ignores_identity_evidence() {
        // O7: the PostToolUse may be an older identical occurrence's.
        use crate::herdr::keys::Evidence;
        let hub = std::sync::Arc::new(EvidenceHub::default());
        hub.expect("r1", bash("touch /tmp/acp0/t2.txt"), true);
        let sent_at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let h2 = hub.clone();
        let waiting = tokio::spawn(async move { h2.outcome(&target("r1"), "allow", sent_at).await });
        let mut post = ev("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json", HookName::PostToolUse);
        post.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&post);
        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
        assert!(!waiting.is_finished(), "no verdict from ambiguous evidence");
        let mut stop = ev("claude/payload-Stop.json", HookName::Stop);
        stop.at = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        hub.publish(&stop);
        assert_eq!(waiting.await.unwrap(), Verdict::NoEvidence, "uncertain: the turn end proves nothing");
    }
```

```rust
fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn question_rig() -> (Rig, String) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    let q = herdr_screen("claude-askuserquestion-single.txt");
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &q);
    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "visible", &q);
    f.on_keys(|f, pane, _| {
        let idle = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-detection.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        let prompt = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/claude-idle-prompt.txt", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap();
        f.set_screen(pane, "detection", &idle);
        f.set_screen(pane, "visible", &prompt);
        f.set_status(pane, "working");
    });
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    (r, agent)
}

fn post_answer(label: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    p["tool_response"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p["tool_input"]["answers"]["Pick a color"] = label.into();
    p
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_single_question_on_a_bound_pane_is_answered_with_its_number_key() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PermissionRequest", &fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().answer_path, AnswerPath::Keys);
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().last().map(|k| k.1.clone()), Some(vec!["1".to_string()]));
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Red"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Inbox);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_question_answered_otherwise_is_terminal() {
    let (r, agent) = question_rig().await;
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let h = bound_headers(&r);
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("question", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).filter(|p| p.kind == RequestKind::Question).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "o1").await;
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Blue"), &h).await;
    assert_eq!(resolution(&mut rx, &rid).await, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
}

fn pre_question(labels: &[&str]) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    p["tool_input"]["questions"][0]["options"] =
        labels.iter().map(|l| json!({"label": l, "description": "d"})).collect::<Vec<_>>().into();
    p
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_repeated_question_with_changed_options_ignores_the_older_answer() {
    // Sol R3-2: question identity is the question text, not the labels.
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &pre_question(&["Red", "Blue"]), &CLI).await;
    let q1 = until("Q1", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &pre_question(&["Blue", "Green", "Red"]), &CLI).await; // O1 ends Q1
    let q2 = until("Q2", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|i| *i != q1)).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post_answer("Red"), &CLI).await; // Q1's delayed answer
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&q2).map(|q| q.status), Some(RequestStatus::Open), "Q2 is shadowed (O7)");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn another_questions_outcome_neither_resolves_nor_counts() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &pre_question(&["Red", "Blue"]), &CLI).await;
    let q = until("Q", || r.agent(HOOKS_ONLY).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    let mut other = post_answer("Red");
    other["tool_response"]["answers"] = json!({"A different question?": "Yes"});
    other["tool_input"]["answers"] = json!({"A different question?": "Yes"});
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &other, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&q).map(|q| q.status), Some(RequestStatus::Open), "R3-2: no evidence for this question");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the hub**

Append to `evidence.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Mutex;

use async_trait::async_trait;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::Instant;

use crate::herdr::keys::{Evidence, KeysTarget};

const RECENT: usize = 256;

/// Expectations by request id, plus the recent and live hook events. The
/// keys engine asks it for a verdict after a key (P1 follow-ups: only
/// evidence for `target.request_id` that arrived after `sent_at`, never a
/// verdict reused across ids, bounded by the engine's `evidence_timeout`).
pub struct EvidenceHub {
    /// Request id -> (expectation, shadowed by O7).
    expects: Mutex<HashMap<String, (Expect, bool)>>,
    recent: Mutex<VecDeque<HookEvent>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<HookEvent>,
}

impl Default for EvidenceHub {
    fn default() -> Self {
        EvidenceHub { expects: Mutex::default(), recent: Mutex::default(), tx: broadcast::channel(RECENT).0 }
    }
}

impl EvidenceHub {
    pub fn expect(&self, request_id: &str, e: Expect, shadowed: bool) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").insert(request_id.to_owned(), (e, shadowed));
    }

    pub fn forget(&self, request_id: &str) {
        self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").remove(request_id);
    }

    pub fn publish(&self, ev: &HookEvent) {
        {
            let mut r = self.recent.lock().expect("evidence");
            r.push_back(ev.clone());
            while r.len() > RECENT {
                r.pop_front();
            }
        }
        let _ = self.tx.send(ev.clone());
    }
}

pub fn sent_for_option(expect: &Expect, option_id: &str) -> Option<Sent> {
    match expect {
        Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } => labels.get(option_id).cloned().map(Sent::Choose),
        _ => match option_id {
            "allow" | "allow_always" => Some(Sent::Allow),
            "deny" => Some(Sent::Deny),
            _ => None,
        },
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl Evidence for EvidenceHub {
    async fn outcome(&self, target: &KeysTarget, option_id: &str, sent_at: Instant) -> Verdict {
        let Some((expect, shadowed)) = self.expects.lock().expect("evidence").get(target.request_id()).cloned() else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        let Some(sent) = sent_for_option(&expect, option_id) else {
            return Verdict::NoEvidence;
        };
        // Subscribe before reading the backlog so nothing falls between.
        let mut rx = self.tx.subscribe();
        let backlog: Vec<HookEvent> =
            self.recent.lock().expect("evidence").iter().filter(|e| e.at >= sent_at).cloned().collect();
        // O7: a shadowed expectation never gets a verdict: identity
        // evidence is ignored, so a turn end proves nothing either.
        let decide = |seen: Seen| if shadowed { Verdict::NoEvidence } else { verdict(&sent, &seen) };
        for ev in &backlog {
            if let Some(seen) = observe_shadowed(&expect, ev, shadowed) {
                return decide(seen);
            }
        }
        loop {
            match rx.recv().await {
                Ok(ev) if ev.at >= sent_at => {
                    if let Some(seen) = observe_shadowed(&expect, &ev, shadowed) {
                        return decide(seen);
                    }
                }
                Ok(_) | Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => return Verdict::NoEvidence,
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
enum TrackKind {
    Permission,
    Question,
    Codex,
}
```

Set `kind: TrackKind::Permission` in `open_claude_permission`. At the top
of `on_event`, publish every event: `self.evidence.publish(&event);`.
In `forget`, also call `self.evidence.forget(rid)`. In
`switch_to_keys_locked`, after `t.phase = Phase::Keys`, call
`self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone(), t.shadowed)` (O7).

In `on_claude`, add the question branch next to the permission one:

```rust
        let ask = matches!(event.name, HookName::PreToolUse | HookName::PermissionRequest)
            && tool == Some("AskUserQuestion");
        if ask {
            self.open_claude_question(event).await;
        } else if event.name == HookName::PermissionRequest {

```

The question path implements O2 (the `PreToolUse` opens the occurrence;
the matching `PermissionRequest` merges into it) and uses the validated
`QuestionSpec` from Task 7 for the request, the screen match (M3) and
the evidence labels, so all three describe the same question (Sol
question 2):

```rust
    async fn open_claude_question(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Some(agent_id) = self.sessions.get(&sid).and_then(|s| s.agent_id.clone()) else { return };
        let pane = self.bound_pane(&sid);
        let Some(key) = claude_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        // O2: a PermissionRequest for the question merges into the open
        // occurrence its PreToolUse opened; anything else is a new one (O1).
        let open_same = self
            .core
            .request_id_for(&agent_id, &key)
            .filter(|rid| self.tracks.get(rid).is_some_and(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Question));
        if event.name == HookName::PermissionRequest && open_same.is_some() {
            return;
        }
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await;
        let Some(obs) =
            mapping::claude_question(&agent_id, key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &event.payload, pane.is_some())
        else {
            return;
        };
        let answerable = obs.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys;
        self.observe_locked(&agent_id, pane.is_some(), Observation::RequestSeen(obs)).await;
        let Some(rid) = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &key) else { return };
        // D25: malformed input is read-only, with no matcher and no labels.
        let spec = mapping::question_specs(&event.payload.tool_input).ok().and_then(|v| v.into_iter().next());
        let labels = spec
            .as_ref()
            .map(|q| q.options.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, (l, _))| (format!("o{}", i + 1), l.clone())).collect())
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let now = Instant::now();
        self.next_op += 1;
        self.tracks.insert(rid.clone(), Track {
            agent_id,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key,
            op: self.next_op,
            reads: ReadSlots::default(),
            wanted_purpose: HashMap::new(),
            expect: Expect::ClaudeQuestion {
                session_id: sid,
                question: spec.as_ref().map(|q| q.question.clone()).unwrap_or_default(),
                labels,
            },
            matcher: spec.filter(|_| answerable).map(|spec| DialogMatch::ClaudeQuestion { spec }),
            kind: TrackKind::Question,
            payload: event.payload,
            pane: pane.clone(),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
            hold_end_tries: 0,
            shadowed: false,
            identity_seen: false,
        });
        self.mark_shadow(&rid); // O7
        if answerable && pane.is_some() {
            self.spawn_read(&rid, Purpose::Capture);
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// A captured single question, inside the delivery lock the caller
    /// holds (G5): register its number keys bound to the pane incarnation,
    /// then mark it ready. `None` or any failure leaves it read-only.
    fn register_question_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: Option<String>, incarnation: u64) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let Expect::ClaudeQuestion { labels, .. } = &t.expect else { return };
        let target = screen.and_then(|s| {
            KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &s, mapping::question_keys(labels.len()))
                .ok()
                .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation))
        });
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        match target {
            Some(target) if open && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok() => {
                self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                t.phase = Phase::Keys;
                self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone(), t.shadowed);
            }
            _ => self.core.observe(Observation::RequestPath {
                agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(),
                key: t.key.clone(),
                answer_path: AnswerPath::None,
            }),
        }
    }
```

```rust
                match (kind, matched) {

                    (TrackKind::Permission, Some(screen)) if phase == Phase::ReadOnly => {

                }
```

### Task 17: Codex requests

Spec §6.3, Occurrence contract O3, Dialog match contract M2, Read ticket
contract (Sol B2), ruling B4 and Codex binding validation C1 to C4 (Sol
N1). A Codex `PermissionRequest` already got its empty reply from the
endpoint. The bind read is the only Codex read, for the first watch and
for every later enabling read: an unknown or truncated rival, a rival
that shows the command, or a rival inventory that changed during the
read never enables a request.

GATE 4: binding follows Task 10's `codex_bind`; a target is registered
only after the bound pane's own screen matches exactly (M2), no other
Codex pane shows the same command, and the binding read is still valid
inside the delivery lock. The tests encode gate 4: the env is ignored,
two Codex panes in one cwd stay read-only, and a session pane that never
shows the dialog (the misbinding gate 4 observed) opens nothing.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/state.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/keys.rs` (C6: validated rival inventory in the lease)
- Test: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_codex.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 10, 12 to 16 (`codex_bind`, `DialogMatch`, tickets, tracks, hub);
  `mapping::codex_permission`, `CODEX_KEYS`.
- Produces (crate-private, used by Tasks 18 and 19): `PaneSeen`,
  `pane_seen`, `CodexCtx`, `CodexBindRead`, `CodexDecision`,
  `spawn_codex_bind`, `codex_decision`, `on_codex_bound`,
  `codex_inventory`, `Internal::CodexBound`.
- Produces (herdr, C6): `keys::codex_inventory(panes: &PaneMap, except: &str) -> Vec<(String, u64)>`
  (every Codex pane but `except`, sorted, as `(agent_id, incarnation)`),
  `KeysTargets::set_rivals(&self, request_id: &str, inventory: Vec<(String, u64)>)`
  and `KeysTargets::rivals(&self, request_id: &str) -> Option<Vec<(String, u64)>>`
  (dropped with the target).

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_protocol::*;
use serde_json::{Value, json};

const CODEX_SID: &str = "01a116d3-e8a3-7ef0-9f57-79c5413ed009";

fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

/// The recorded payload, re-pointed at the command the recorded dialog shows.
fn permission(cwd: &str) -> Value {
    let mut p = fx("codex/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    p["tool_input"]["command"] = "touch ~/acp0_test_t5_1.txt".into();
    p["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    p
}

fn codex_pane(id: &str, cwd: &str, session: Option<&str>) -> Value {
    let mut a = fake_agent(id, "codex", "working");
    a["cwd"] = cwd.into();
    a["foreground_cwd"] = cwd.into();
    if let Some(s) = session {
        a["agent_session"] = json!({"source": "herdr:codex", "agent": "codex", "kind": "id", "value": s});
    }
    a
}

async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

fn agent_of(r: &Rig, pane: &str) -> String {
    r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().iter().find(|(_, p)| p.pane_id == pane).unwrap().0.clone()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_session_bound_codex_approval_is_answerable_within_a_second() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    let t = tokio::time::Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await, (200, String::new()));
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("ready target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3), "spec §14 item 2");
    let req = r.core.request(&rid).unwrap();
    assert_eq!((req.answer_path, req.options.len()), (AnswerPath::Keys, 3));
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "deny").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().len(), 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn stale_env_never_binds_and_two_panes_in_one_cwd_stay_read_only() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let other = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let r = rig(&[
        codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, None),
        codex_pane("w1:p2", &cwd, None),
        codex_pane("w1:p3", other.path().to_str().unwrap(), None),
    ], |_| {}).await;
    let dialog = herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt");
    for p in ["w1:p1", "w1:p2", "w1:p3"] {
        r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen(p, "detection", &dialog);
    }
    let sock = r.sock();
    // The env names pane w1:p3 on the right socket: still never used (gate 4).
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p3"), ("X-Herdr-Socket", &sock)]).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(900)).await;
    for p in ["w1:p1", "w1:p2", "w1:p3"] {
        let pending = r.agent(&agent_of(&r, p)).and_then(|a| a.pending);
        assert!(pending.is_none_or(|q| q.kind == RequestKind::BlockedUnparsed), "{p} got a structured request");
    }
    assert!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().is_empty());
}
```

Also add the reappearing-dialog case (P1 follow-up: a dialog with the
same identity that comes back within about two polls keeps the old
occurrence open, so answers get `screen_mismatch`):

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_repeated_dialog_opens_a_new_occurrence_with_a_fresh_target() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(&r, "w1:p1");
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    let r1 = until("first", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    until("first target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r1)).await;
    // Answered in the terminal and asked again before the watch saw it go.
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == r1
        {
            assert_eq!(by, ResolvedBy::Terminal);
            break;
        }
    }
    let r2 = until("second", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id).filter(|id| *id != r1)).await;
    until("second target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&r2)).await;
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &r2, "allow").await.is_ok());
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_codex_watch_after_t3_takeover_opens_nothing() {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let cwd = tmp.path().to_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let r = rig(&[codex_pane("w1:p1", &cwd, Some(CODEX_SID))], |_| {}).await;

    f.set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let gate = f.block_next("agent.read");
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(&cwd), &[]).await;
    gate.arrived().await;
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-9", Some(CODEX_SID));
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)).await;
    gate.release();
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(900)).await;
    assert!(r.agent(&agent_of(&r, "w1:p1")).and_then(|a| a.pending).is_none());
    assert!(f.keys_sent().is_empty());
}
```
