- [ ] **Step 2: Run them to verify they fail**

Run: `cargo test -p acd-adapters --test hooks_codex`
Expected: FAIL (no Codex requests open).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Codex sessions get their own binding generation (G2) and watch
bookkeeping. The `Reconciler` gains `codex: HashMap<String, CodexSession>`:

```rust
struct CodexSession {
    /// Bumped by every Codex event of the session, every T3 ownership
    /// change, every binding change and every revocation: a watch spawned
    /// before any of those is dropped (G5).
    bind_gen: u64,
    last_event: Instant,
    /// The bind read that runs before a request exists (kind `CodexBind`,
    /// `op = 0`, G4). Aborted by a newer one (A4).
    watch: ReadSlots,
    /// A3: a bind read that found no permit, retried on the next tick.
    wanted: Option<CodexCtx>,
}
```

Extend `bind_gen_of` (Task 15) to fall back to `self.codex`, and make
`now_for` read the watch sequence for a ticket without a request id:

```rust
    fn bind_gen_of(&self, sid: &str) -> Option<u64> {
        self.sessions.get(sid).map(|s| s.bind_gen).or_else(|| self.codex.get(sid).map(|c| c.bind_gen))
    }

    // in `now_for`, the `track` field:
    let track = match &t.request_id {
        Some(rid) => self.tracks.get(rid).map(|tr| (tr.op, tr.reads.latest(t.kind))),
        None => self.codex.get(&t.session_id).map(|c| (0, c.watch.latest(ReadKind::CodexBind))),
    };
```

In `on_tick`, drop Codex sessions with no event for `idle_expiry` (and no
open track).

The bind read and its validation (Codex binding validation C1 to C4):

```rust
use super::owner::{CodexBind, codex_bind};
use super::wire::codex_key;

/// C2: what one pane showed.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum PaneSeen {
    Shows(String),
    NotShown { collapsed: bool },
    /// Failed, timed out or truncated: never taken as "not shown".
    Unknown,
}

/// C2, pure.
pub(crate) fn pane_seen(read: Option<&crate::herdr::client::ScreenRead>, matcher: &DialogMatch) -> PaneSeen {
    match read {
        None => PaneSeen::Unknown,
        Some(r) if r.truncated => PaneSeen::Unknown,
        Some(r) if matcher.find(&r.text).is_some() => PaneSeen::Shows(r.text.clone()),
        Some(r) => PaneSeen::NotShown { collapsed: DialogMatch::collapsed_codex(&r.text) },
    }
}

/// What a bind read is for.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub(crate) struct CodexCtx {
    event: HookEvent,
    pane: PaneFact,
    key: CorrelationKey,
    command: String,
    /// `None` before the request exists.
    rid: Option<String>,
    /// Inventory-change retries so far (C3), at most 3.
    tries: u8,
}

pub(crate) struct CodexBindRead {
    bound: PaneSeen,
    /// The rival inventory this read covered (C2), sorted.
    inventory: Vec<(String, u64)>,
    rivals: Vec<PaneSeen>,
}

/// C3's outcome.
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub(crate) enum CodexDecision {
    Enable(String),
    Collapsed,
    /// Not shown on the bound pane, or a rival shows it or is unknown.
    Nothing,
    /// The rival inventory changed: read again.
    Retry,
    Stale,
}

// `Internal` gains:
//     CodexBound { ticket: Ticket, ctx: CodexCtx, read: CodexBindRead, permit: OwnedSemaphorePermit },
```

```rust
    /// A5: Codex sessions whose pre-request bind watch runs or waits.
    fn codex_watches(&self) -> usize {
        self.codex.values().filter(|c| c.wanted.is_some() || c.watch.busy(ReadKind::CodexBind)).count()
    }

    /// C2: every Codex pane but `bound`, now, as `(agent_id, incarnation)`.
    fn codex_inventory(&self, bound: &str) -> Vec<(String, u64)> {
        let mut v: Vec<(String, u64)> = self
            .panes()
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|p| p.kind == acd_protocol::AgentKind::Codex && p.agent_id != bound)
            .map(|p| (p.agent_id, p.incarnation))
            .collect();
        v.sort();
        v
    }

    async fn on_codex(&mut self, event: HookEvent) {
        let sid = event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let now = Instant::now();
        let c = self.codex.entry(sid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| CodexSession {
            bind_gen: 0, last_event: now, watch: ReadSlots::default(), wanted: None,
        });
        c.bind_gen += 1; // any later event supersedes an earlier watch
        c.last_event = now;
        c.watch.abort_all(); // A4
        c.wanted = None;
        self.apply_evidence(&event);
        if event.name != HookName::PermissionRequest {
            return;
        }
        if self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&sid).is_some()) {
            return; // T3 shows it (ownership rule 1 holds for Codex sessions too)
        }
        if event.hints.herdr_pane.is_some() {
            tracing::debug!("Codex HERDR_* hints are not used for binding (gate 4)");
        }
        let Some(key) = codex_key(&event.payload) else { return };
        self.end_session_occurrences(&sid).await; // O3
        // M2/M4: only a command that renders on one row can ever match.
        let Some(command) = command_of(&event.payload.tool_input).filter(|c| single_row(c)).map(str::to_owned) else {
            return;
        };
        let pane = match codex_bind(&sid, event.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &self.panes()) {
            CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) => p,
            CodexBind::Ambiguous | CodexBind::Unbound => {
                tracing::debug!("Codex request not bound; the herdr fallback shows it read-only");
                return;
            }
        };
        if self.codex_watches() >= self.cfg.max_tracks {
            tracing::warn!("too many pending Codex watches; this request is shown read-only by herdr");
            return; // A5
        }
        self.spawn_codex_bind(CodexCtx { event, pane, key, command, rid: None, tries: 0 });
    }

    /// C1/C2 and A3: the only Codex read. Reads the bound pane (polling
    /// for up to `codex_window` while there is no request yet), then,
    /// if it shows the command, every rival in the pane map at spawn time.
    fn spawn_codex_bind(&mut self, ctx: CodexCtx) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let sid = ctx.event.payload.session_id.clone();
        let Ok(permit) = self.reads.clone().try_acquire_owned() else {
            self.want_codex(ctx);
            return;
        };
        let (op, read_seq) = match &ctx.rid {
            Some(rid) => {
                let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
                (t.op, t.reads.admit(ReadKind::CodexBind, true).expect("admitted"))
            }
            None => {
                let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(&sid) else { return };
                (0, c.watch.admit(ReadKind::CodexBind, true).expect("admitted"))
            }
        };
        let Some(bind_gen) = self.bind_gen_of(&sid) else { return };
        let ticket = Ticket {
            agent_id: ctx.pane.agent_id.clone(),
            incarnation: ctx.pane.incarnation,
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            bind_gen,
            request_id: ctx.rid.clone(),
            op,
            kind: ReadKind::CodexBind,
            read_seq,
        };
        // Listed before taking the map lock (`panes()` takes it too).
        let inventory = self.codex_inventory(&ctx.pane.agent_id);
        let map = herdr.panes();
        let (pref, rivals) = {
            let map = map.read().expect("pane map");
            let Some(pref) = map.get(&ctx.pane.agent_id).cloned() else { return };
            let rivals: Vec<((String, u64), Option<PaneRef>)> = inventory
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(id, inc)| {
                    let r = map.get(&id).filter(|p| p.incarnation == inc).cloned();
                    ((id, inc), r)
                })
                .collect();
            (pref, rivals)
        };
        let rid_for_handle = ctx.rid.clone();
        let tx = self.internal_tx.clone();
        let poll_until = Instant::now() + if ctx.rid.is_none() { self.cfg.codex_window } else { Duration::ZERO };
        let poll = self.cfg.capture_poll;
        let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let matcher = DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command: ctx.command.clone() };
            let read = |p: PaneRef| async move {
                p.herdr.read_screen(&p.pane_id, "detection", None, Instant::now() + CALL_TIMEOUT).await.ok()
            };
            let bound = loop {
                let seen = pane_seen(read(pref.clone()).await.as_ref(), &matcher);
                if matches!(seen, PaneSeen::Shows(_)) || Instant::now() >= poll_until {
                    break seen;
                }
                tokio::time::sleep(poll).await;
            };
            let mut seen_rivals = Vec::new();
            if matches!(bound, PaneSeen::Shows(_)) {
                for (_, r) in &rivals {
                    seen_rivals.push(match r {
                        Some(r) => pane_seen(read(r.clone()).await.as_ref(), &matcher),
                        None => PaneSeen::Unknown, // left the map between listing and reading
                    });
                }
            }
            let inventory = rivals.into_iter().map(|(k, _)| k).collect();
            let read = CodexBindRead { bound, inventory, rivals: seen_rivals };
            let _ = tx.send(Internal::CodexBound { ticket, ctx, read, permit }).await;
        });
        // A4: keep the handle where the sequence was admitted.
        let handle = task.abort_handle();
        match rid_for_handle {
            Some(rid) => {
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                    t.reads.started(ReadKind::CodexBind, handle);
                }
            }
            None => {
                if let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(&sid) {
                    c.watch.started(ReadKind::CodexBind, handle);
                }
            }
        }
    }
```

```rust
    /// C3, pure over what the caller gathered inside the lock.
    fn codex_decision(&self, ticket: &Ticket, ctx: &CodexCtx, read: &CodexBindRead) -> CodexDecision {
        let still_bound = matches!(
            codex_bind(&ticket.session_id, ctx.event.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &self.panes()),
            CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) if p.agent_id == ctx.pane.agent_id && p.incarnation == ctx.pane.incarnation
        );
        let paths: &[AnswerPath] = &[AnswerPath::Keys];
        let valid = match &ticket.request_id {
            None => still_valid(ticket, &self.now_for(ticket), &[]),
            Some(_) => still_valid(ticket, &self.now_for(ticket), paths),
        };
        if !still_bound || !valid {
            return CodexDecision::Stale;
        }
        match &read.bound {
            PaneSeen::Shows(screen) => {
                if read.inventory != self.codex_inventory(&ctx.pane.agent_id) {
                    CodexDecision::Retry
                } else if read.rivals.iter().all(|r| matches!(r, PaneSeen::NotShown { .. })) {
                    CodexDecision::Enable(screen.clone())
                } else {
                    CodexDecision::Nothing // a rival shows it, or cannot be read (C3)
                }
            }
            PaneSeen::NotShown { collapsed: true } => CodexDecision::Collapsed,
            _ => CodexDecision::Nothing,
        }
    }

    /// Inside the delivery lock (C1): the only place a Codex request opens
    /// or is enabled.
    async fn on_codex_bound(&mut self, ticket: Ticket, ctx: CodexCtx, read: CodexBindRead) {
        // The read finished: its slot no longer holds a task (A3, A5).
        match &ticket.request_id {
            Some(rid) => {
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) {
                    t.reads.finished(ReadKind::CodexBind, ticket.read_seq);
                }
            }
            None => {
                if let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(&ticket.session_id) {
                    c.watch.finished(ReadKind::CodexBind, ticket.read_seq);
                }
            }
        }
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
        let Ok(_g) = tokio::time::timeout(self.cfg.lock_wait, locks.lock(&ticket.agent_id)).await else {
            self.want_codex(ctx); // A7: retry next tick
            return;
        };
        let decision = self.codex_decision(&ticket, &ctx, &read);
        match decision {
            CodexDecision::Stale => tracing::debug!("dropping a stale Codex bind read"),
            CodexDecision::Retry if ctx.tries < 3 => {
                drop(_g);
                self.spawn_codex_bind(CodexCtx { tries: ctx.tries + 1, ..ctx });
            }
            CodexDecision::Retry | CodexDecision::Nothing => {
                // A live target whose recheck failed is revoked (C5); a
                // request without one stays read-only; no request opens.
                if let Some(rid) = &ctx.rid
                    && self.tracks.get(rid).is_some_and(|t| t.phase == Phase::Keys)
                {
                    drop(_g);
                    self.revoke_session(&ticket.session_id).await;
                }
            }
            CodexDecision::Collapsed | CodexDecision::Enable(_) => {
                let rid = match &ctx.rid {
                    Some(rid) => rid.clone(),
                    None => {
                        let Some(rid) = self.open_codex_locked(&ctx, decision == CodexDecision::Collapsed) else { return };
                        rid
                    }
                };
                if let CodexDecision::Enable(screen) = decision {
                    if self.tracks.get(&rid).is_some_and(|t| t.phase == Phase::Keys) {
                        // C6: a recheck of a live target passed C3. Keep the
                        // immutable target, store the new validated
                        // inventory, then (and only then) restore readiness.
                        herdr.keys_targets().set_rivals(&rid, read.inventory.clone());
                        if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                            t.codex_inventory = read.inventory;
                            self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
                        }
                    } else {
                        self.register_codex_locked(&rid, screen, ticket.incarnation, read.inventory);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        self.sweep();
    }

    /// A3: the bind read waits for the next tick. A track keeps its
    /// `codex_ctx` from opening, so only the wanted flag is set there.
    fn want_codex(&mut self, ctx: CodexCtx) {
        let sid = ctx.event.payload.session_id.clone();
        match ctx.rid.clone() {
            Some(rid) => {
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(&rid) {
                    t.reads.admit(ReadKind::CodexBind, false);
                }
            }
            None => {
                if let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(&sid) {
                    c.wanted = Some(ctx);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /// Opens the request read-only (collapsed shows the note). Inside the lock.
    fn open_codex_locked(&mut self, ctx: &CodexCtx, collapsed: bool) -> Option<String> {
        if self.tracks.len() >= self.cfg.max_tracks {
            return None; // A5: open nothing; the herdr fallback still shows the dialog
        }
        let agent_id = ctx.pane.agent_id.clone();
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&agent_id, ctx.key.clone(), self.fallback_key(&agent_id), &ctx.event.payload, collapsed);
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let rid = self.core.request_id_for(&agent_id, &ctx.key)?;
        let now = Instant::now();
        let sid = ctx.event.payload.session_id.clone();
        self.next_op += 1;
        let op = self.next_op;
        self.tracks.entry(rid.clone()).or_insert_with(|| Track {
            agent_id: agent_id.clone(),
            session_id: sid.clone(),
            key: ctx.key.clone(),
            op,
            reads: ReadSlots::default(),
            wanted_purpose: HashMap::new(),
            expect: Expect::CodexTool {
                session_id: sid,
                tool_name: ctx.event.payload.tool_name.clone().unwrap_or_default(),
                command: ctx.command.clone(),
            },
            matcher: Some(DialogMatch::CodexCommand { command: ctx.command.clone() }),
            kind: TrackKind::Codex,
            payload: ctx.event.payload.clone(),
            pane: Some(ctx.pane.clone()),
            phase: Phase::ReadOnly,
            opened_at: now,
            capture: None,
            absent: 0,
            next_check: now + self.cfg.held_dialog_poll,
            hold_end_tries: 0,
            shadowed: false,
            identity_seen: false,
            codex_ctx: Some(CodexCtx { rid: Some(rid.clone()), tries: 0, ..ctx.clone() }),
            codex_inventory: Vec::new(),
        });
        self.mark_shadow(&rid); // O7
        Some(rid)
    }

    /// Inside the delivery lock the caller holds; only `on_codex_bound`
    /// calls it (C1).
    fn register_codex_locked(&mut self, rid: &str, screen: String, incarnation: u64, inventory: Vec<(String, u64)>) {
        let Some(herdr) = self.herdr.clone() else { return };
        let Some(t) = self.tracks.get_mut(rid) else { return };
        let keys = mapping::CODEX_KEYS.iter().map(|(o, k)| (o.to_string(), k.to_string())).collect();
        let target = KeysTarget::from_screen(rid.to_owned(), t.agent_id.clone(), t.key.clone(), &screen, keys)
            .map(|k| k.for_incarnation(incarnation));
        // Drop the "Enlarge the pane" note now that the command is visible.
        let obs = mapping::codex_permission(&t.agent_id, t.key.clone(), None, &t.payload, false);
        self.core.observe(Observation::RequestSeen(obs));
        let open = self.core.request(rid).is_some_and(|r| r.status == RequestStatus::Open && r.answer_path == AnswerPath::Keys);
        if let Ok(target) = target
            && open
            && herdr.keys_targets().set(target).is_ok()
        {
            // C6: the lease refuses once the pane map's rivals differ.
            herdr.keys_targets().set_rivals(rid, inventory.clone());
            self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: true });
            t.phase = Phase::Keys;
            t.capture = Some(screen);
            t.codex_inventory = inventory;
            self.evidence.expect(rid, t.expect.clone(), t.shadowed);
        }
    }
```

`herdr/keys.rs` (C6). `KeysTargets` gains a `rivals:
Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<(String, u64)>>>` side table, cleared by
`remove`; the target stays immutable:

```rust
/// C6: every Codex pane but `except`, as `(agent_id, incarnation)`, sorted.
pub fn codex_inventory(panes: &PaneMap, except: &str) -> Vec<(String, u64)> {
    let mut v: Vec<(String, u64)> = panes
        .read()
        .expect("pane map")
        .iter()
        .filter(|(id, p)| p.kind == acd_protocol::AgentKind::Codex && id.as_str() != except)
        .map(|(id, p)| (id.clone(), p.incarnation))
        .collect();
    v.sort();
    v
}

impl KeysTargets {
    /// Called only under the target agent's delivery lock.
    pub fn set_rivals(&self, request_id: &str, inventory: Vec<(String, u64)>) {
        self.rivals.lock().expect("rivals").insert(request_id.to_owned(), inventory);
    }
    pub fn rivals(&self, request_id: &str) -> Option<Vec<(String, u64)>> {
        self.rivals.lock().expect("rivals").get(request_id).cloned()
    }
}
```

and `lease_held` (Task 6) adds, after `same_pane`:

```rust
        // C6: a validated rival inventory must still be the current one.
        let rivals_ok = self
            .targets
            .rivals(&target.request_id)
            .is_none_or(|v| v == codex_inventory(panes, &target.agent_id));
```

`claimed && same_pane && rivals_ok && …`. When `rivals_ok` alone fails,
`deliver` returns `NotDelivered(RequestNotReady)` (the client retries
for 3 s while the recheck runs) and sends nothing. Task 17's
`codex_inventory` in `state.rs` becomes a thin call to this function so
both sides compute the same list.

`Track` gains `codex_ctx: Option<CodexCtx>` (the context it was opened
with, `rid` set; every re-read spawns `spawn_codex_bind(t.codex_ctx.clone())`)
and `codex_inventory: Vec<(String, u64)>` (the rivals checked when the
target was registered); every other constructor sets `codex_ctx: None,
codex_inventory: Vec::new()`.

Wire it up:

- `on_internal`: `Internal::CodexBound { ticket, ctx, read, permit } => { self.on_codex_bound(ticket, ctx, read).await; drop(permit); }`.
- Codex tracks never use `spawn_read(Purpose::Capture)`; `on_screen`
  has no Codex arm (C1).
- `on_tick`, after `retry_wanted`: retry wanted Codex bind reads (each
  session's `wanted.take()`, and each track's `codex_ctx` when its
  `CodexBind` kind is in `reads.wanted()`), at most `batch` per tick; then, for each Codex track
  in `Phase::ReadOnly` with no capture (collapsed) and
  `now >= t.next_check`, set `next_check = now + capture_window +
  held_dialog_poll` and spawn a bind read for it (C4); then call
  `self.codex_transitions().await` (Task 18 defines it).

A Codex request answered in the terminal ends through the herdr target
watch (dialog gone, `terminal`) once it has a target, and through
`apply_evidence` (`PostToolUse` or `Stop`, `terminal`) while collapsed.

```rust
async fn live_codex_target(r: &Rig, cwd: &str, pane: &str) -> String {
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen(pane, "detection", &herdr_screen("codex-permission-bash-detection.txt"));
    let agent = agent_of(r, pane);
    r.hook("codex", "PermissionRequest", &permission(cwd), &[]).await;

    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    rid
}

async fn revoked(r: &Rig, rx: &mut tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver<ServerMessage>, rid: &str) {
    loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestResolved { request_id, by, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request_id == rid
        {
            assert_eq!(by, ResolvedBy::Lost);
            break;
        }
    }
    assert!(r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(rid).is_none(), "target retired");
    assert!(r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().keys_sent().is_empty());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```rust
    /// Ownership transitions: revoke every request of `sid`. Inside each
    /// track's delivery lock: retire the target, end the request
    /// (`uncertain` while answering, else `lost`), mark it gone, release
    /// the hold and drop the expectation. Never removes a herdr agent.
    /// Bumps the session's `bind_gen`, so every outstanding read fails G5.
    async fn revoke_session(&mut self, sid: &str) {
        if let Some(s) = self.sessions.get_mut(sid) {
            s.bind_gen += 1;
        }
        if let Some(c) = self.codex.get_mut(sid) {
            c.bind_gen += 1;
        }
        let ids: Vec<String> = self.tracks.iter().filter(|(_, t)| t.session_id == sid).map(|(k, _)| k.clone()).collect();
        for rid in ids {
            let agent = self.tracks.get(&rid).map(|t| t.agent_id.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
            let locks = self.herdr.as_ref().map(|h| h.agent_locks());
            let _g = match &locks {
                Some(l) => Some(l.lock(&agent).await),
                None => None,
            };
            if let Some(h) = &self.herdr {
                h.keys_targets().remove(&rid);
            }
            let by = match self.core.request(&rid).map(|r| r.status) {
                Some(RequestStatus::Answering) => ResolvedBy::Uncertain,
                _ => ResolvedBy::Lost,
            };
            self.end(&rid, by); // `end` also marks it gone and releases the hold
        }
    }
```

Rework `apply_owner` (Task 11) so it follows the table. Compute
`old = s.owner.clone()` first, and when `old != owner`:

```rust
        let revoke = match (&old, &owner) {
            (Owner::Herdr(a), Owner::Herdr(b)) => a != b,
            (Owner::Herdr(_), _) => true,                       // to T3, Unknown or Hooks
            (Owner::Hooks, Owner::T3(_) | Owner::Unknown) => true,
            _ => false,                                         // Hooks -> Herdr migrates instead
        };
        if revoke {
            self.revoke_session(sid).await;
        }
```

then the existing per-owner actions (migration for `Hooks` → `Herdr`,
`AgentGone(claude:<sid>)` for `Hooks` → `T3`/`Unknown`, upsert for
`→ Hooks`). Re-borrow `self.sessions` after the await.

The Codex rows of the transitions table, C5 and C6. A Codex track's owner is
`Bound(a)` while `codex_bind` still picks its pane `a` with the same
incarnation and T3 does not own the session; anything else revokes. A
live target whose rival inventory changed is rechecked through the bind
read (C1), and `on_codex_bound` revokes it unless the recheck enables
again:

```rust
    async fn codex_transitions(&mut self) {
        let mut revoke = Vec::new();
        let mut recheck = Vec::new();
        for t in self.tracks.values().filter(|t| t.kind == TrackKind::Codex) {
            let Some(pane) = &t.pane else { continue };
            let t3 = self.t3.as_ref().is_some_and(|m| m.owner(&t.session_id).is_some());
            let bound = matches!(
                codex_bind(&t.session_id, t.payload.cwd.as_deref(), &self.panes()),
                CodexBind::Session(p) | CodexBind::OnlyInCwd(p) if p.agent_id == pane.agent_id && p.incarnation == pane.incarnation
            );
            if t3 || !bound {
                revoke.push(t.session_id.clone());
            } else if t.phase == Phase::Keys
                && !t.reads.busy(ReadKind::CodexBind)
                && t.codex_inventory != self.codex_inventory(&pane.agent_id)
            {
                if let Some(ctx) = t.codex_ctx.clone() {
                    recheck.push(ctx);
                }
            }
        }
        revoke.sort();
        revoke.dedup();
        for sid in revoke {
            self.revoke_session(&sid).await;
        }
        for ctx in recheck {
            // C6: suspend first, under the delivery lock (a state change:
            // no timeout, A7); readiness returns only after C3 passes.
            if let (Some(herdr), Some(rid)) = (self.herdr.clone(), ctx.rid.clone()) {
                let locks = herdr.agent_locks();
                let _g = locks.lock(&ctx.pane.agent_id).await;
                if let Some(t) = self.tracks.get(&rid) {
                    self.core.observe(Observation::RequestReady { agent_id: t.agent_id.clone(), key: t.key.clone(), ready: false });
                }
            }
            self.spawn_codex_bind(ctx);
        }
    }
```

`reevaluate_all` calls `self.codex_transitions().await` after the Claude
sessions. `on_tick` calls it last (Task 17 wired the call). A recheck
that is already running is not doubled: `spawn_codex_bind` aborts the
older read of the same kind (A4), and `codex_transitions` skips a track
whose bind read is still running.

### Task 19: Bounded work and fair service

Admission and service bounds A5 to A8 (Sol N2, R3-3, R3-5). Task 8
bounded holds and the control channel (A1, A2); Task 15 bounded reads
(A3, A4). This task caps open tracks, session maps and generation
history, replaces the reconciler's biased `select!` with batched turns
that serve the tick and ownership changes first, counts retained work
for tests (queue depths read live), and pins the bounds under a stalled
delivery lock, a real stalled keys holder and floods.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/state.rs`, `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/mod.rs`,
  `crates/acd-adapters/src/hooks/endpoint.rs` (`MailboxTx::queued`)
- Create: `crates/acd-adapters/tests/hooks_bounds.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 8, 11, 14 to 18.
- Produces:
  - `hooks::HookStats { control_queued: usize, events_queued: usize, internal_queued: usize, read_tasks: usize, tracks: usize, holds: usize, codex_watches: usize, sessions: usize, codex_sessions: usize, unconsumed_sessions: usize, generation_ids: usize, sessions_refused: u64, generation_ids_dropped: u64 }`
    (`Clone, Copy, Debug, Default`).
  - `HooksAdapter::stats(&self) -> HookStats` (`control_queued`,
    `events_queued`, `internal_queued` and `read_tasks` are live, read
    from kept sender clones and counters, so they are correct while the
    reconciler is blocked; the rest is published at the end of every
    turn).
  - `endpoint::MailboxTx::queued(&self) -> (usize, usize)` (control,
    events), from `max_capacity() - capacity()` of the two senders.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn a_stalled_delivery_lock_keeps_retained_work_bounded() {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |c| c.max_holds = 8).await;
    let (agent, h) = bound(&r).await;
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    let locks = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().agent_locks();
    let guard = locks.lock(&agent).await; // the reconciler stalls on its next lock wait
    let p = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let mut max_control = 0;
    for _ in 0..100 {
        let s = send(r.port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &h, &p.to_string()).await;
        drop(s); // Claude went away
        max_control = max_control.max(r.hooks.stats().control_queued);
    }
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    let st = r.hooks.stats();
    assert!(st.control_queued <= 2 * 8 && max_control <= 2 * 8, "A2: {st:?}, max {max_control}");
    assert!(st.read_tasks <= 8, "A3: {st:?}");
    assert!(st.internal_queued <= 8 + 2, "A3, read live while the consumer is blocked: {st:?}");
    // Overflow fails open at once while the lock is stalled.
    let t = Instant::now();
    let (status, body) = read_response(send(r.port, "/hook/claude/PermissionRequest", TOKEN, &h, &p.to_string()).await).await;
    assert_eq!((status, body.as_str()), (200, ""));
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(500));
    drop(guard);
    // Once the lock frees, the queues drain and the slots come back.
    until("drained", || {
        let st = r.hooks.stats();
        (st.control_queued == 0 && st.holds <= 1).then_some(())
    })
    .await;
    let _held = r.hold(&p, &h).await;
    until("a new hold is admitted", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending)).await;
}

async fn hold_churn_cannot_starve_a_takeover_or_the_tick() {
    let r = rig(&[], |c| c.max_holds = 4).await;

    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("request", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).and_then(|a| a.pending)).await;

        loop {

            tokio::task::yield_now().await;
        }

    let t = Instant::now();
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", Some(SID));
    assert_eq!(read_response(held).await, (200, String::new()), "takeover released the hold");
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(500), "A6: takeover served within one turn ({:?})", t.elapsed());
    churn.abort();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn evicted_ambiguity_still_shadows_new_requests() {
    // Sol R3-3 (round-3 verification): real unconsumed identities, more
    // sessions than the cap, then an identical request in an evicted session.
    let r = rig(&[], |c| {
        c.max_sessions = 8;
        c.evidence_window = Duration::from_millis(50);
    })
    .await;
    let sid = |i: usize| format!("6b000000-0000-4000-8000-{i:012}");
    for i in 0..40 {
        // A, then B in the same session: O1 ends A without evidence (an
        // unconsumed identity); closing B's hold ends it `uncertain` after
        // the evidence window (another one). No turn end clears them.
        let a = r.hold(&permission(&sid(i), "touch /tmp/a"), &CLI).await;
        let id = format!("claude:{}", sid(i));
        until("A", || r.agent(&id).and_then(|q| q.pending)).await;
        let b = r.hold(&permission(&sid(i), "touch /tmp/b"), &CLI).await;
        drop((a, b));
        until("tracks ended", || (r.hooks.stats().tracks == 0).then_some(())).await;
    }
    let st = r.hooks.stats();
    assert!(st.unconsumed_sessions <= 8, "A8: ambiguity storage is capped: {st:?}");
    assert!(st.sessions <= 8, "A8: {st:?}");
    // Session 0's identities were evicted long ago; a new identical request
    // there must still be shadowed (A8's global shadow), so a PostToolUse
    // for the same command cannot resolve it.
    let _held = r.hold(&permission(&sid(0), "touch /tmp/a"), &CLI).await;
    let id0 = format!("claude:{}", sid(0));
    let rid = until("new A", || r.agent(&id0).and_then(|q| q.pending).map(|q| q.id)).await;
    let mut post = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-Bash.json");
    post["session_id"] = sid(0).into();
    post["tool_input"] = permission(&sid(0), "touch /tmp/a")["tool_input"].clone();
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &post, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).map(|q| q.status), Some(acd_protocol::RequestStatus::Open), "still shadowed after eviction");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn another_questions_outcome_does_not_consume_an_unconsumed_identity() {
    // Sol R3-2, observed through the A8 counters.
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let mut q = fx("claude/payload-PreToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &q, &CLI).await;
    until("Q1", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).and_then(|a| a.pending)).await;
    q["tool_input"]["questions"][0]["options"][0]["label"] = "Green".into();
    r.hook("claude", "PreToolUse", &q, &CLI).await; // O1 ends Q1 without evidence
    until("one entry", || (r.hooks.stats().unconsumed_sessions == 1).then_some(())).await;
    let mut other = fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json");
    other["tool_response"]["answers"] = serde_json::json!({"A different question?": "Yes"});
    other["tool_input"]["answers"] = serde_json::json!({"A different question?": "Yes"});
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &other, &CLI).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    assert_eq!(r.hooks.stats().unconsumed_sessions, 1, "not consumed by another question's outcome");
    r.hook("claude", "PostToolUse", &fx("claude/payload-PostToolUse-AskUserQuestion.json"), &CLI).await;
    until("consumed", || (r.hooks.stats().unconsumed_sessions == 0).then_some(())).await;
}

/// Sol R3-5: the keys engine itself holds the delivery lock while one of
/// its herdr calls (`method`) is held by the fake until the client's call
/// deadline; a takeover must complete within 2 x CALL_TIMEOUT (+ slack).
async fn l1_case(method: &str) {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |c| c.held_dialog_poll = Duration::from_secs(60)).await;
    let (agent, h) = bound(&r).await;
    let h: Vec<(&str, &str)> = h.iter().map(|(k, v)| (*k, v.as_str())).collect();
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&agent).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(700)).await; // captured
    drop(held); // switch to keys
    until("target", || r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().keys_targets().get(&rid)).await;
    let f = r.fake.as_ref().unwrap();
    let gate = f.block_next(method); // never released: the client deadline ends the call
    let core = r.core.clone();
    let rid2 = rid.clone();
    let answering = tokio::spawn(async move {
        core.handle("c", acd_protocol::ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
            id: "a1".into(), request_id: rid2, revision: None, option_id: Some("allow".into()), text: None,
        }).await
    });
    // Handshake from the actual holder: the engine's call reached herdr,
    // and the delivery lock is provably taken (a probe cannot get it).
    gate.arrived().await;
    let locks = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().agent_locks();
    assert!(
        tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_millis(50), locks.lock(&agent)).await.is_err(),
        "the keys engine holds the delivery lock during its {method} call"
    );
    let t = Instant::now();
    r.t3.set_for_tests("t3:thread-1", Some(SID));
    until("revoked", || r.core.request(&rid).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    let limit = 2 * acd_adapters::herdr::client::CALL_TIMEOUT + Duration::from_millis(1000);
    assert!(t.elapsed() <= limit, "L1: takeover waited {:?}", t.elapsed());
    assert!(f.keys_sent().is_empty(), "the held write never took effect");
    let _ = answering.await;
    drop(gate);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_real_keys_holder_stalled_in_its_guard_read_releases_within_l1() {
    l1_case("agent.read").await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_real_keys_holder_stalled_in_its_key_write_releases_within_l1() {
    l1_case("agent.send_keys").await;
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn holds_beyond_the_track_cap_are_released_at_once() {
    let r = rig(&[], |c| c.max_tracks = 4).await;
    let mut held = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..4 {
        let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
        p["session_id"] = format!("5f4996ff-0000-4000-8000-{i:012}").into();
        held.push(r.hold(&p, &CLI).await);
    }
    until("four tracks", || (r.hooks.stats().tracks == 4).then_some(())).await;
    let mut p = fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");

    let t = Instant::now();
    assert_eq!(read_response(r.hold(&p, &CLI).await).await, (200, String::new()), "A5: the terminal answers it");
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(500));
    assert_eq!(r.hooks.stats().tracks, 4);
}
```

Run: `cargo test -p acd-adapters --test hooks_bounds`
Expected: FAIL to compile (`stats`, `HookStats` unknown).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`mod.rs`:

```rust
/// Retained work, for tests and diagnostics (A1 to A5).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct HookStats {
    pub control_queued: usize,
    pub events_queued: usize,
    pub internal_queued: usize,
    /// Spawned read tasks still alive (running or about to send).
    pub read_tasks: usize,
    pub tracks: usize,
    pub holds: usize,
    pub codex_watches: usize,
}

impl HooksAdapter {
    pub fn stats(&self) -> HookStats {
        let mut s = *self.shared.stats.lock().expect("stats");
        s.read_tasks = self.shared.read_tasks.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
        s
    }
}
```

`Shared` gains `stats: Mutex<HookStats>` and
`read_tasks: std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize`. Every spawned read task
(Task 15's `spawn_read`, Task 17's `spawn_codex_bind`) holds a
`TaskCount` guard, incremented on spawn and decremented on drop, so an
aborted task is counted out too:

```rust
pub(crate) struct TaskCount(Arc<Shared>);

impl TaskCount {
    pub(crate) fn new(shared: &Arc<Shared>) -> TaskCount {
        shared.read_tasks.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
        TaskCount(shared.clone())
    }
}

impl Drop for TaskCount {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.0.read_tasks.fetch_sub(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::SeqCst);
    }
}
```

(Create it with `let count = TaskCount::new(&self.shared);` before
`tokio::spawn` and move it into the task.)

A5 in the opening paths: `open_claude_permission` and the question
opener return before observing anything when
`self.tracks.len() >= self.cfg.max_tracks` (a held request's `HoldIn` is
dropped there, which replies empty and frees its permit); Task 17's
`open_codex_locked` already checks.

The loop (A6) replaces Task 11's `run`:

```rust
    pub(crate) async fn run(mut self) {
        let mut t3_changes = self.t3.as_ref().map(|m| m.changes());
        let mut next_tick = Instant::now();
        let mut next_scan = Instant::now();
        loop {
            // The tick and ownership changes first, every turn.
            let now = Instant::now();
            if now >= next_tick {
                next_tick = now + self.cfg.tick;
                if now >= next_scan {
                    next_scan = now + self.cfg.scan_interval;
                    self.spawn_scan();
                }
                self.on_tick().await;
            }
            if let Some(rx) = t3_changes.as_mut()
                && rx.has_changed().unwrap_or(false)
            {
                rx.borrow_and_update();
                self.reevaluate_all().await;
            }
            let mut busy = false;
            for _ in 0..self.cfg.batch {
                let Ok(m) = self.mailbox.control.try_recv() else { break };
                busy = true;
                self.on_inbound(m).await;
            }
            for _ in 0..self.cfg.batch {
                let Ok(m) = self.internal_rx.try_recv() else { break };
                busy = true;
                self.on_internal(m).await;
            }
            for _ in 0..self.cfg.batch {
                match self.mailbox.events.try_recv() {
                    Ok(m) => {
                        busy = true;
                        self.take_event(m).await;
                    }
                    Err(mpsc::error::TryRecvError::Empty) => break,
                    Err(mpsc::error::TryRecvError::Disconnected) => return,
                }
            }
            self.publish_stats();
            if busy {
                continue;
            }
            // Idle: wait for any source or the next tick. A message taken
            // here is handled now; the next turn serves the tick first.
            tokio::select! {
                _ = tokio::time::sleep_until(next_tick) => {}
                _ = changed(&mut t3_changes) => self.reevaluate_all().await,
                Some(m) = self.mailbox.control.recv() => self.on_inbound(m).await,
                Some(m) = self.internal_rx.recv() => self.on_internal(m).await,
                m = self.mailbox.events.recv() => match m {
                    Some(m) => self.take_event(m).await,
                    None => return,
                },
            }
        }
    }

    async fn take_event(&mut self, m: Inbound) {
        if let Inbound::Event { bytes, .. } = &m {
            self.mailbox.release(*bytes);
        }
        self.on_inbound(m).await;
    }

    fn publish_stats(&self) {
        *self.shared.stats.lock().expect("stats") = super::HookStats {
            control_queued: 0, // live, see `stats()`
            events_queued: 0,  // live
            internal_queued: self.internal_rx.len(),
            read_tasks: 0, // live counter, read by `stats()`
            tracks: self.tracks.len(),
            holds: self.holds.len(),
            codex_watches: self.codex_watches(),
        };
    }
```

(`mpsc::Receiver::len` exists in the locked tokio; if it does not, use
`max_capacity() - capacity()` on a kept `Sender` clone and report the
change.)

`control_queued` in the stalled-lock test is read while the reconciler
is blocked, so `stats()` overrides the published queue lengths with
`self.tx.queued()`:

```rust
// endpoint.rs
impl MailboxTx {
    pub fn queued(&self) -> (usize, usize) {
        (
            self.control.max_capacity() - self.control.capacity(),
            self.events.max_capacity() - self.events.capacity(),
        )
    }
}

// mod.rs, in `stats()` before returning:
        (s.control_queued, s.events_queued) = self.tx.queued();
```

Session and history bounds (A8, Sol R3-3). `Reconciler` gains
`stats_counts: SessionCounts { sessions_refused: u64, generation_ids_dropped: u64 }`
and a global `shadow_all: Option<Instant>` (checked by `mark_shadow`
next to the per-session flag). Every place that inserts a new Claude or
Codex session (`on_claude`, `on_scan`, `on_codex`) first calls
`admit_session`:

```rust
    /// A8: makes room for a new session in `map`, or refuses it.
    fn admit_session(&mut self, sid: &str, codex: bool) -> bool {
        let len = if codex { self.codex.len() } else { self.sessions.len() };
        if (codex && self.codex.contains_key(sid)) || (!codex && self.sessions.contains_key(sid)) {
            return true;
        }
        if len < self.cfg.max_sessions {
            return true;
        }
        let busy: HashSet<String> = self.tracks.values().map(|t| t.session_id.clone()).collect();
        let victim = if codex {
            self.codex
                .iter()
                .filter(|(id, c)| !busy.contains(*id) && c.wanted.is_none() && !c.watch.busy(ReadKind::CodexBind))
                .min_by_key(|(_, c)| c.last_event)
                .map(|(id, _)| id.clone())
        } else {
            self.sessions.iter().filter(|(id, _)| !busy.contains(*id)).min_by_key(|(_, s)| s.last_event).map(|(id, _)| id.clone())
        };
        let Some(victim) = victim else {
            self.stats_counts.sessions_refused += 1;
            return false; // the caller drops the event; a hold drops and replies empty
        };
        if codex {
            self.codex.remove(&victim);
        } else if let Some(s) = self.sessions.remove(&victim)
            && s.hooks_only
        {
            self.core.observe(Observation::AgentGone { agent_id: format!("claude:{victim}") });
        }
        if self.unconsumed.remove(&victim).is_some() {
            self.shadow_all = Some(Instant::now()); // A8: summarize, never forget
        }
        true
    }
```

`note_unconsumed` applies the same cap to `unconsumed` (evicting the
oldest session's entries sets `shadow_all`). In `on_event`, the
`by_generation` insert becomes:

```rust
            let set = g.entry(generation).or_default();
            if set.len() < self.cfg.max_generation_ids || set.contains(&event.payload.session_id) {
                set.insert(event.payload.session_id.clone());
            } else {
                // A8: losing this observation only keeps the previous token longer.
                self.stats_counts.generation_ids_dropped += 1;
            }
```

Stored `cwd` values longer than 4096 bytes are not stored (`None`).
`publish_stats` fills `sessions`, `codex_sessions`,
`unconsumed_sessions` (`self.unconsumed.len()`), `generation_ids` (sum
over `by_generation`), `sessions_refused` and `generation_ids_dropped`.
`internal_queued` becomes live like the control queue: `Shared` keeps a
clone of the internal `Sender`, and `stats()` reads
`max_capacity() - capacity()` from it.

For the L1 test, `acd_adapters::herdr::client::CALL_TIMEOUT` must be
public (it is `pub const` in `client.rs`; export the module under
feature `testing` if it is private).

### Task 20: Secrets hardening and `init` repair

P1 follow-ups: `check_mode` must not follow symlinks and must check the
owner; `write_secret` must clean up its temp file on failure and fsync
the directory after the rename; `init` must check the mode of files it
keeps and reject an empty `machine-id`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd-securefs/src/lib.rs` (`check_private_file`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/identity.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`init`)
- Test: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs` unit tests, `crates/acd/tests/cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `acd_securefs::check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError>`.
  - `secrets::check_mode(path, max_mode)`: same signature, now no-follow
    and owner-checked; the "Fix with: chmod" hint is kept for mode errors.
  - `secrets::read_secret(path)`: reads through `read_private` (0600, 4 KiB).
  - `secrets::write_secret(path, value)`: unique temp name ending in
    `.tmp`, removed on any failure, parent directory fsynced.
  - `secrets::repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool>`: for
    `init`; refuses symlinks and foreign owners, tightens a loose mode and
    returns whether it changed.
  - `identity::machine_id(dir)` errors on an empty id.

```rust
/// No-follow open, regular file, owned by the euid, mode within `max_mode`.
pub fn check_private_file(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<(), SecureFsError> {
    let file = open_path(path, false)?;
    verify(&file, path, false, max_mode)
}
```

`secrets.rs`:

```rust
use acd_securefs::SecureFsError;

fn explain(e: SecureFsError) -> anyhow::Error {
    match e {
        SecureFsError::Mode { path, mode, want } => anyhow::anyhow!(
            "{} has mode {:o}, which lets other users read it. Fix with: chmod {:o} {}",
            path.display(), mode & 0o777, want, path.display()
        ),
        SecureFsError::Symlink(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", p.display()),
        SecureFsError::Owner(p) => anyhow::anyhow!("{} is not owned by you", p.display()),
        other => anyhow::anyhow!("{other}"),
    }
}

/// Fails if `path` is a symlink, is not owned by the effective user, or
/// grants any permission bit outside `max_mode`.
pub fn check_mode(path: &Path, max_mode: u32) -> Result<()> {
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    let r = if meta.is_dir() {
        acd_securefs::check_private_dir(path, max_mode)
    } else {
        acd_securefs::check_private_file(path, max_mode)
    };
    r.map_err(explain)
}

pub fn read_secret(path: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = acd_securefs::read_private(path, 0o600, 4096).map_err(explain)?;
    Ok(String::from_utf8(bytes).context("secret is not UTF-8")?.trim().to_string())
}

/// Writes `value` atomically with mode 0600: a unique temp file in the
/// same directory, fsync, rename, fsync of the directory. The temp file is
/// removed on any failure.
pub fn write_secret(path: &Path, value: &str) -> Result<()> {
    let dir = path.parent().context("secret path has no parent")?;
    let mut rnd = [0u8; 8];
    getrandom::fill(&mut rnd).expect("OS randomness is available");
    let name = path.file_name().context("secret path has no file name")?.to_string_lossy();
    let tmp = dir.join(format!(
        "{name}.{}.{}.tmp",
        std::process::id(),
        rnd.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect::<String>()
    ));
    let result = (|| -> Result<()> {
        let mut f = OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).mode(0o600).open(&tmp)
            .with_context(|| format!("creating {}", tmp.display()))?;
        f.write_all(value.as_bytes())?;
        f.write_all(b"\n")?;
        f.sync_all()?;
        fs::rename(&tmp, path).with_context(|| format!("replacing {}", path.display()))?;
        fs::File::open(dir)?.sync_all()?;
        Ok(())
    })();
    if result.is_err() {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
    }
    result
}

/// For `acd init`: tightens a loose mode on a regular file the user owns.
/// Refuses symlinks and foreign owners. Returns whether it changed.
pub fn repair_mode(path: &Path, mode: u32) -> Result<bool> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    let meta = fs::symlink_metadata(path).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display()))?;
    if meta.file_type().is_symlink() {
        bail!("{} is a symlink; replace it with a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if !meta.is_file() {
        bail!("{} is not a regular file", path.display());
    }
    if meta.uid() != acd_securefs::euid() {
        bail!("{} is not owned by you", path.display());
    }
    if meta.mode() & 0o777 & !mode == 0 {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    fs::set_permissions(path, fs::Permissions::from_mode(mode))?;
    Ok(true)
}
```

### Task 21: Hook token store

Hook token store contract T1 to T3 and T6, private structured files F1
(Sol I1, I2, N7, N9; ruling: one state file written atomically is the
transaction). This task creates the writer lock, the structured-file
helpers and the store, migrates `hook.token`, and gives
`acd token rotate hook` its first, store-only step; Tasks 26 and 27 add
retention and the header rewrite.

Lock discipline (T6): `load` is read-only and never migrates or locks.
Every writer has a `*_locked` form that takes `&WriterLock`; the plain
forms (`migrate`, `ensure`, `rotate_store`) acquire the lock once and
call the locked form. No locked form acquires the lock, so no path
reacquires `hooks.lock`.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd/src/writer_lock.rs`
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hook_token.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/secrets.rs` (`read_structured`, `write_structured`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`init`, `preflight_prerequisites`, `token rotate hook`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/tests/cli.rs` (Task 20's tests that named `hook.token`)
- Test: unit tests in `hook_token.rs`, `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 20
  (`write_secret`, `read_secret`, `repair_mode`, `parse_token`).
- Produces:
  - `hook_token::STORE_FILE = "hook-tokens.json"`, `LEGACY_FILE = "hook.token"`,
    `MAX_RETENTION_MS: i64 = 30 * 86_400_000`.
  - `writer_lock::{LOCK_FILE = "hooks.lock", WriterLock, acquire(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<WriterLock>}`
    (blocking `flock(LOCK_EX)` on a 0600 file).
  - `secrets::STRUCTURED_MAX = 256 * 1024`,
    `secrets::read_structured(path: &Path) -> Result<String>`,
    `secrets::write_structured(path: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<()>` (F1:
    refuses more than `STRUCTURED_MAX` bytes before writing).
  - `hook_token::{Store, Previous, Headers, HeaderState}` (serde, as in T1).
  - `hook_token::load(dir: &Path) -> Result<Store>`: read-only; an absent
    store is an error (naming `acd init` when a legacy file waits).
  - `hook_token::migrate(dir) -> Result<Store>`,
    `hook_token::migrate_locked(dir, &WriterLock) -> Result<Store>`
    (create the store from a legacy `hook.token`, then remove it; a
    no-op load when the store exists).
  - `hook_token::ensure(dir: &Path, force: bool) -> Result<bool>`,
    `hook_token::ensure_locked(dir, &WriterLock, force) -> Result<bool>`
    (for `acd init`; whether it created or replaced the store).
  - `hook_token::save_locked(dir, &WriterLock, &Store) -> Result<()>`.
  - `hook_token::rotate_store(dir, now_ms) -> Result<Store>`,
    `hook_token::rotate_store_locked(dir, &WriterLock, now_ms) -> Result<Store>`:
    T3 step 1 only.
  - `hook_token::fail_at(stage: &str) -> Result<()>`: debug-only failure
    injection (`ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT=<stage>`, error text
    `injected failure at <stage>`), a no-op in release builds.
  - `hook_token::pause_at(stage: &str)`: debug-only gate
    (`ACD_TEST_PAUSE_AT=<stage>=<path>`: creates `<path>.reached`, then
    waits up to 30 s for `<path>` to exist), a no-op in release builds.

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn dir() -> tempfile::TempDir {
        let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(d.path()).unwrap();
        d
    }

    #[test]
    fn ensure_creates_generation_one_without_a_previous_token() {
        let d = dir();
        assert!(ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap());
        let s = load(d.path()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((s.generation, s.previous.is_none(), s.headers.state), (1, true, HeaderState::Done));
        crate::secrets::parse_token(&s.current).unwrap();
        assert!(!ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap(), "idempotent");
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), s);
    }

    fn legacy_dir() -> (tempfile::TempDir, String) {
        let d = dir();
        let legacy = crate::secrets::new_token();
        crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.path().join(LEGACY_FILE), &legacy).unwrap();
        (d, legacy)
    }

    /// Runs `f` on a thread and fails (instead of hanging) after 5 s: a
    /// recursive `flock` blocks forever (Sol N7).
    fn within_5s<T: Send + 'static>(f: impl FnOnce() -> T + Send + 'static) -> T {
        let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let _ = tx.send(f());
        });
        rx.recv_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).expect("finished without self-deadlock")
    }

    #[test]
    fn load_never_migrates() {
        let (d, _) = legacy_dir();
        let e = format!("{:#}", load(d.path()).unwrap_err());
        assert!(e.contains("acd init"), "{e}");
        assert!(d.path().join(LEGACY_FILE).exists(), "load is read-only");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_legacy_hook_token_is_migrated_and_removed() {
        let (d, legacy) = legacy_dir();
        let p = d.path().to_path_buf();
        let s = within_5s(move || migrate(&p).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(s.current, legacy);
        assert!(!d.path().join(LEGACY_FILE).exists());
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), s);
    }

    #[test]
    fn ensure_on_a_legacy_only_dir_does_not_reacquire_the_lock() {
        let (d, legacy) = legacy_dir();
        let p = d.path().to_path_buf();
        assert!(!within_5s(move || ensure(&p, false).unwrap()));
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap().current, legacy);
    }

    #[test]
    fn concurrent_migrations_agree() {
        let (d, legacy) = legacy_dir();
        let handles: Vec<_> = (0..4)
            .map(|_| {
                let p = d.path().to_path_buf();
                std::thread::spawn(move || migrate(&p).unwrap().current)
            })
            .collect();
        let tokens: Vec<String> = within_5s(move || handles.into_iter().map(|h| h.join().unwrap()).collect());
        assert!(tokens.iter().all(|t| *t == legacy), "one migration, the others load it");
    }

    #[test]
    fn structured_files_are_bounded_before_writing() {
        let d = dir();
        let p = d.path().join("big.json");
        crate::secrets::write_structured(&p, &"x".repeat(10_000)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(crate::secrets::read_structured(&p).unwrap().len(), 10_000, "more than 4 KiB round-trips (F1)");
        let e = crate::secrets::write_structured(&p, &"y".repeat(crate::secrets::STRUCTURED_MAX + 1)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(format!("{e:#}").contains("too large"), "{e:#}");
        assert_eq!(crate::secrets::read_structured(&p).unwrap().len(), 10_000, "the old file is untouched");
    }

    #[test]
    fn rotation_keeps_the_previous_token_for_at_most_30_days() {
        let d = dir();
        assert!(ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap());
        let before = load(d.path()).unwrap();
        let held = crate::writer_lock::acquire(d.path()).unwrap();
        let after = rotate_store_locked(d.path(), &held, 1_000).unwrap();
        drop(held);
        assert_eq!(after.generation, before.generation + 1);
        let prev = after.previous.clone().unwrap();
        assert_eq!((prev.token, prev.generation), (before.current, before.generation));
        assert_eq!(prev.expires_at_ms, 1_000 + MAX_RETENTION_MS);
        assert_eq!(after.headers.state, HeaderState::Pending);
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), after, "committed");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_corrupt_store_is_an_error_naming_the_file() {
        let d = dir();
        crate::secrets::write_secret(&d.path().join(STORE_FILE), "{not json").unwrap();
        let e = format!("{:#}", load(d.path()).unwrap_err());
        assert!(e.contains(STORE_FILE), "{e}");
    }
}
```

```rust
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir), before);
}

/// Runs `cmd` and fails the test (killing it) after `secs`.
fn run_within(mut cmd: std::process::Command, secs: u64) -> std::process::Output {
    let mut child = cmd.stdout(std::process::Stdio::piped()).stderr(std::process::Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(secs);
    while child.try_wait().unwrap().is_none() {
        if std::time::Instant::now() > deadline {
            let _ = child.kill();
            panic!("did not finish within {secs} s (lock reacquired?)");
        }
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(20));
    }
    child.wait_with_output().unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn init_migrates_a_legacy_only_dir_without_hanging() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let token = hook_token_of(&dir);
    std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("hook-tokens.json")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("hook.token"), format!("{token}\n")).unwrap();
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::set_permissions(dir.join("hook.token"), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
    let mut init = acd_home(&dir, home.path());
    init.arg("init");
    assert!(run_within(init, 10).status.success());
    assert_eq!(hook_token_of(&dir), token);
    assert!(!dir.join("hook.token").exists());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the store**

```rust
//! The hook token store (contract T1 to T5): `hook-tokens.json`, written
//! atomically at 0600, is the single source of truth and the rotation
//! transaction. Tasks 26 and 27 add retention and header rewrites.
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::secrets;
use crate::writer_lock::{self, WriterLock};

pub const STORE_FILE: &str = "hook-tokens.json";
pub const LEGACY_FILE: &str = "hook.token";
pub const MAX_RETENTION_MS: i64 = 30 * 86_400_000;

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Previous {
    pub token: String,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub rotated_at_ms: i64,
    pub expires_at_ms: i64,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
pub enum HeaderState {
    Pending,
    Done,
    Failed,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Headers {
    /// The generation the managed Claude headers carry once `state` is `done`.
    pub generation: u64,
    pub state: HeaderState,
    /// When the headers last became `done` (T4 counts sessions older than this).
    pub updated_at_ms: i64,
    pub failed: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Store {
    pub version: u32,
    pub generation: u64,
    pub current: String,
    pub previous: Option<Previous>,
    pub headers: Headers,
}

/// Debug-only failure injection at a named stage (`ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT`).
pub fn fail_at(stage: &str) -> Result<()> {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if std::env::var("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT").is_ok_and(|s| s == stage) {
        bail!("injected failure at {stage}");
    }
    let _ = stage;
    Ok(())
}

/// Debug-only gate for race tests (`ACD_TEST_PAUSE_AT=<stage>=<path>`).
pub fn pause_at(stage: &str) {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Ok(v) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_PAUSE_AT")
        && let Some((s, path)) = v.split_once('=')
        && s == stage
    {
        let _ = std::fs::write(format!("{path}.reached"), b"");
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(30);
        while !Path::new(path).exists() && std::time::Instant::now() < deadline {
            std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));
        }
    }
    let _ = stage;
}

fn now_ms() -> i64 {
    chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis()
}

pub fn save_locked(dir: &Path, _held: &WriterLock, s: &Store) -> Result<()> {
    secrets::parse_token(&s.current).context("refusing to save an invalid hook token")?;
    fail_at("store-write")?;
    secrets::write_structured(&dir.join(STORE_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(s)?)
}

fn fresh(token: String, generation: u64) -> Store {
    Store {
        version: 1,
        generation,
        current: token,
        previous: None,
        headers: Headers { generation, state: HeaderState::Done, updated_at_ms: now_ms(), failed: vec![] },
    }
}

/// Read-only (T6): never migrates, never locks.
pub fn load(dir: &Path) -> Result<Store> {
    let path = dir.join(STORE_FILE);
    if path.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
        if dir.join(LEGACY_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok() {
            bail!("{} has not been migrated from hook.token yet; run `acd init` (or start acd)", path.display());
        }
        bail!("{} is missing; run `acd init`", path.display());
    }
    let s: Store = serde_json::from_str(&secrets::read_structured(&path)?)
        .with_context(|| format!("{} is corrupt; run `acd init --force`", path.display()))?;
    secrets::parse_token(&s.current).with_context(|| format!("{} holds an invalid token", path.display()))?;
    Ok(s)
}

pub fn migrate(dir: &Path) -> Result<Store> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    migrate_locked(dir, &held)
}

/// Rechecks inside the lock: another process may have migrated already.
pub fn migrate_locked(dir: &Path, held: &WriterLock) -> Result<Store> {
    if dir.join(STORE_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok() {
        return load(dir);
    }
    let legacy = dir.join(LEGACY_FILE);
    if legacy.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
        return load(dir); // the "missing" error
    }
    let token = secrets::parse_token(&secrets::read_secret(&legacy)?)
        .context("hook.token is invalid; run `acd init --force`")?;
    let s = fresh(token, 1);
    save_locked(dir, held, &s)?;
    std::fs::remove_file(&legacy).with_context(|| format!("removing {}", legacy.display()))?;
    Ok(s)
}

/// For `acd init`. `force` replaces the store with a fresh generation.
pub fn ensure(dir: &Path, force: bool) -> Result<bool> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    ensure_locked(dir, &held, force)
}

pub fn ensure_locked(dir: &Path, held: &WriterLock, force: bool) -> Result<bool> {
    let store = dir.join(STORE_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok();
    let legacy = dir.join(LEGACY_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok();
    if (store || legacy) && !force {
        migrate_locked(dir, held)?;
        return Ok(false);
    }
    let generation = if store { load(dir).map(|s| s.generation + 1).unwrap_or(1) } else { 1 };
    save_locked(dir, held, &fresh(secrets::new_token(), generation))?;
    if legacy {
        std::fs::remove_file(dir.join(LEGACY_FILE))?;
    }
    Ok(true)
}

/// Task 21's store-only rotation step; Task 27 replaces its only caller
/// and deletes it.
pub fn rotate_store(dir: &Path, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Store> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    rotate_store_locked(dir, &held, now_ms)
}

/// T3 step 1: commit the new generation; the old token stays accepted
/// until retired (Task 26), never beyond `MAX_RETENTION_MS`.
pub fn rotate_store_locked(dir: &Path, held: &WriterLock, now_ms: i64) -> Result<Store> {
    let old = load(dir)?;
    let generation = old.generation + 1;
    let s = Store {
        version: 1,
        generation,
        current: secrets::new_token(),
        previous: Some(Previous {
            token: old.current,
            generation: old.generation,
            rotated_at_ms: now_ms,
            expires_at_ms: now_ms + MAX_RETENTION_MS,
        }),
        headers: Headers { generation, state: HeaderState::Pending, updated_at_ms: old.headers.updated_at_ms, failed: vec![] },
    };
    save_locked(dir, held, &s)?;
    Ok(s)
}
```

`writer_lock.rs`:

```rust
//! `<config_dir>/hooks.lock` (contract T6). Acquired only by top-level
//! entry points; every helper that needs it takes `&WriterLock`.
use std::fs::OpenOptions;
use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
use std::os::unix::io::AsRawFd;
use std::path::Path;

use anyhow::{Result, bail};

pub const LOCK_FILE: &str = "hooks.lock";

pub struct WriterLock(#[allow(dead_code)] std::fs::File);

pub fn acquire(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<WriterLock> {
    let path = config_dir.join(LOCK_FILE);
    let f = OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .read(true)
        .write(true)
        .mode(0o600)
        .custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
        .open(&path)?;
    // SAFETY: a valid open descriptor; flock has no other preconditions.
    if unsafe { libc::flock(f.as_raw_fd(), libc::LOCK_EX | libc::LOCK_NB) } == 0 {
        return Ok(WriterLock(f));
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    tests_support::mark_contended(&path); // the caller now waits (Task 26's handshake)
    // SAFETY: as above.
    if unsafe { libc::flock(f.as_raw_fd(), libc::LOCK_EX) } != 0 {
        bail!("could not lock {}", path.display());
    }
    Ok(WriterLock(f))
}

/// Unit-test handshake: proves a caller reached a contested `acquire`.
#[cfg(test)]
pub mod tests_support {
    use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
    use std::sync::Mutex;

    static CONTENDED: Mutex<Vec<PathBuf>> = Mutex::new(Vec::new());

    pub(super) fn mark_contended(path: &Path) {
        CONTENDED.lock().expect("contended").push(path.to_path_buf());
    }

    pub fn contended(path: &Path) -> bool {
        CONTENDED.lock().expect("contended").iter().any(|p| p == path)
    }
}
```

```rust
/// F1: install records, journals and the token store.
pub const STRUCTURED_MAX: usize = 256 * 1024;

pub fn read_structured(path: &Path) -> Result<String> {
    let bytes = acd_securefs::read_private(path, 0o600, STRUCTURED_MAX).map_err(explain)?;
    String::from_utf8(bytes).with_context(|| format!("{} is not UTF-8", path.display()))
}

/// Refuses, before writing, anything `read_structured` could not read back.
pub fn write_structured(path: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<()> {
    if text.len() + 1 > STRUCTURED_MAX {
        bail!("{} would be too large ({} bytes, at most {STRUCTURED_MAX})", path.display(), text.len() + 1);
    }
    write_secret(path, text)
}
```

### Task 22: `acd hook codex <event>`: the Codex shim

Spec §6.1: reads stdin up to 1 MiB, forwards it with the hook token,
prints acd's body and exits 0; any failure prints nothing; connect

```rust
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::TcpListener;
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::sync::mpsc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// A one-shot HTTP server: records the request, replies with `status` and
/// `body`, or never replies when `stall`.
fn one_shot(status: u16, body: &'static str, stall: bool) -> (u16, mpsc::Receiver<String>) {
    let l = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let port = l.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel();
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        let Ok((mut s, _)) = l.accept() else { return };
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2))).unwrap();
        let mut req = Vec::new();
        let mut buf = [0u8; 65536];
        loop {
            match s.read(&mut buf) {
                Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                Ok(n) => {
                    req.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&req);
                    if let Some((head, rest)) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n") {
                        let len = head.lines().find_map(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().strip_prefix("content-length: ").map(|v| v.trim().parse::<usize>().unwrap())).unwrap_or(0);
                        if rest.len() >= len { break; }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let _ = tx.send(String::from_utf8_lossy(&req).into_owned());
        if stall {
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));
            return;
        }
        let _ = write!(s, "HTTP/1.1 {status} X\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len());
    });
    (port, rx)
}

fn shim_dir(port: u16) -> (tempfile::TempDir, std::path::PathBuf) {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let dir = init_dir(tmp.path());
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), format!("hook_port = {port}\n")).unwrap();
    (tmp, dir)
}

fn run_shim(dir: &std::path::Path, event: &str, stdin: &[u8], env: &[(&str, &str)]) -> (std::process::Output, Duration) {
    let mut cmd = acd(dir);
    for (k, v) in env { cmd.env(k, v); }
    let mut child = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", event, "acd-managed:v1"])
        .stdin(Stdio::piped()).stdout(Stdio::piped()).stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
    let t = Instant::now();
    child.stdin.take().unwrap().write_all(stdin).ok();
    let out = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
    (out, t.elapsed())
}

fn codex_fixture(name: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
    std::fs::read(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/hooks/codex/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_forwards_the_hook_and_prints_the_reply() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "{\"ok\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let token = hook_token_of(&dir);
    let body = codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json");
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &body, &[("HERDR_PANE_ID", "w1:p2"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "")]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "{\"ok\":1}");
    let req = rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(2)).unwrap();
    assert!(req.starts_with("POST /hook/codex/PermissionRequest HTTP/1.1\r\n"), "{req}");
    assert!(req.contains(&format!("X-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n")));
    assert!(req.contains("X-Herdr-Pane: w1:p2\r\n"));
    assert!(!req.contains("X-Herdr-Socket"), "empty env values are not sent");
    assert!(req.ends_with(std::str::from_utf8(&body).unwrap()));
    assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).contains(&token) && !String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains(&token));
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_is_down() {
    let port = free_port(); // nothing listens
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert!(out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_secs(1), "{took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_fails_open_when_acd_stalls() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(200, "", true);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, took) = run_shim(&dir, "PermissionRequest", &codex_fixture("payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(took < Duration::from_millis(1200), "inside Codex's 1 s budget plus process start: {took:?}");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_prints_nothing_for_a_non_2xx_reply() {
    let (port, _rx) = one_shot(401, "{\"no\":1}", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{\"session_id\":\"s\"}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_refuses_oversized_input_without_sending() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let big = vec![b'a'; (1 << 20) + 1];
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", &big, &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err(), "nothing was sent");
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_without_a_token_prints_nothing() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "x", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("hook-tokens.json")).unwrap();
    let (out, _) = run_shim(&dir, "Stop", b"{}", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success() && out.stdout.is_empty() && out.stderr.is_empty());
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300)).is_err());
}

#[test]
fn the_shim_honours_an_explicit_config_dir() {
    let (port, rx) = one_shot(200, "ok", false);
    let (_tmp, dir) = shim_dir(port);
    let other_home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mut cmd = acd_home(other_home.path(), other_home.path());
    cmd.env_remove("ACD_CONFIG_DIR");
    let out = cmd.args(["hook", "codex", "Stop", "--config-dir"]).arg(&dir).arg("acd-managed:v1")
        .stdin(Stdio::null()).output().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout), "ok");
    assert!(rx.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
}
```
