`hook_shim.rs`:

```rust
//! `acd hook codex <event>` (spec §6.1): Codex's command hook. Reads the
//! payload on stdin, forwards it to the local hook endpoint, prints the
//! reply body and exits 0. Any failure prints nothing and still exits 0, so
//! Codex shows its own dialog. It never logs and never prints the token.
use std::ffi::OsString;
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::net::{SocketAddr, TcpStream};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

pub const MAX_BODY: usize = 1 << 20;
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

pub fn main(args: &[OsString]) -> i32 {
    if let Some(body) = run(args) {
        let mut out = std::io::stdout().lock();
        let _ = out.write_all(body.as_bytes());
        let _ = out.flush();
    }
    0
}

/// Inside Codex's own timeouts (§6.3: 1 s for PermissionRequest, 2 s else).
fn budget(event: &str) -> Duration {
    if event == "PermissionRequest" { Duration::from_millis(800) } else { Duration::from_millis(1500) }
}

fn header_safe(v: &str) -> bool {
    !v.is_empty() && v.len() <= 1024 && !v.chars().any(char::is_control)
}

fn run(args: &[OsString]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut it = args.iter().map(|a| a.to_str());
    let agent = it.next()??;
    let event = it.next()??;
    if agent != "codex" || event.is_empty() || event.len() > 64 || !event.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()) {
        return None;
    }
    let mut config_dir: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    while let Some(a) = it.next() {
        match a? {
            "--config-dir" => config_dir = Some(PathBuf::from(it.next()??)),
            "acd-managed:v1" => {}
            _ => return None,
        }
    }
    let deadline = Instant::now() + budget(event);
    let mut body = Vec::new();
    std::io::stdin().lock().take(MAX_BODY as u64 + 1).read_to_end(&mut body).ok()?;
    if body.len() > MAX_BODY {
        return None;
    }
    let dir = match config_dir {
        Some(d) => d,
        None => crate::paths::config_dir().ok()?,
    };
    let token = crate::hook_token::load(&dir).ok()?.current;
    let port = crate::config::Config::load(&dir).ok()?.hook_port;
    let mut headers = vec![("X-Acd-Token".to_string(), token)];
    // Hints only (D17): acd never binds Codex with them.
    for (env, header) in [("HERDR_PANE_ID", "X-Herdr-Pane"), ("HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "X-Herdr-Socket")] {
        if let Ok(v) = std::env::var(env)
            && header_safe(&v)
        {
            headers.push((header.to_string(), v));
        }
    }
    post(port, &format!("/hook/codex/{event}"), &headers, &body, deadline)
}

fn post(port: u16, path: &str, headers: &[(String, String)], body: &[u8], deadline: Instant) -> Option<String> {
    let addr = SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], port));
    let mut s = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, CONNECT_TIMEOUT).ok()?;
    let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
    s.set_write_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    s.write_all(body).ok()?;
    let mut resp = Vec::new();
    let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
    loop {
        let left = deadline.checked_duration_since(Instant::now())?;
        s.set_read_timeout(Some(left)).ok()?;
        match s.read(&mut buf) {
            Ok(0) => break,
            Ok(n) => {
                resp.extend_from_slice(&buf[..n]);
                if resp.len() > MAX_BODY + 8192 {
                    return None;
                }
            }
            Err(_) => return None,
        }
    }
    parse_response(&resp)
}

/// The body of a 2xx reply with a plain (non-chunked) body.
fn parse_response(resp: &[u8]) -> Option<String> {
    let text = std::str::from_utf8(resp).ok()?;
    let (head, body) = text.split_once("\r\n\r\n")?;
    let status: u16 = head.split_whitespace().nth(1)?.parse().ok()?;
    if !(200..300).contains(&status) {
        return None;
    }
    if head.lines().any(|l| l.to_ascii_lowercase().starts_with("transfer-encoding:")) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(body.to_owned())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn only_plain_2xx_bodies_are_printed() {
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 2\r\n\r\n{}").as_deref(), Some("{}"));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\ncontent-length: 0\r\n\r\n").as_deref(), Some(""));
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\r\n\r\nno"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nTransfer-Encoding: chunked\r\n\r\n2\r\n{}\r\n0\r\n\r\n"), None);
        assert_eq!(parse_response(b"garbage"), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn unexpected_arguments_send_nothing() {
        let a = |v: &[&str]| v.iter().map(OsString::from).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert!(run(&a(&["claude", "Stop"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop;rm"])).is_none());
        assert!(run(&a(&["codex", "Stop", "--surprise"])).is_none());
    }
}
```

### Task 23: Settings editing primitives

The building blocks of the installer: order-preserving JSON edits (D16),
the owned entries with exact ownership (D22), raw-preserving group edits
(D16), and atomic writes with a chosen mode. GATE 2: the Claude entries
carry `$VAR` header values (2a: interpolated when listed in
`allowedEnvVars`; unlisted ones become `""`) and omit `SessionStart`
(Claude never sends it over http).

- Produces:

`crates/acd/Cargo.toml`: `serde_json = { workspace = true, features = ["raw_value"] }`.

`hooks_install/json.rs`:

```rust
//! Order-preserving edits of a JSON object (D16): only the keys acd edits
//! are re-serialized; every other value keeps its original bytes.
use serde::de::{Deserializer, MapAccess, Visitor};
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Obj(pub Vec<(String, Box<RawValue>)>);

impl<'de> serde::Deserialize<'de> for Obj {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Obj, D::Error> {
        struct V;
        impl<'de> Visitor<'de> for V {
            type Value = Obj;
            fn expecting(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter) -> std::fmt::Result {
                f.write_str("a JSON object")
            }
            fn visit_map<A: MapAccess<'de>>(self, mut m: A) -> Result<Obj, A::Error> {
                let mut out = Vec::new();
                while let Some((k, v)) = m.next_entry::<String, Box<RawValue>>()? {
                    out.push((k, v));
                }
                Ok(Obj(out))
            }
        }
        d.deserialize_map(V)
    }
}

impl Obj {
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Obj> {
        if text.trim().is_empty() {
            return Ok(Obj::default());
        }
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(text)?)
    }

    pub fn get(&self, k: &str) -> Option<&RawValue> {
        self.0.iter().find(|(key, _)| key == k).map(|(_, v)| v.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn set(&mut self, k: &str, v: Box<RawValue>) {
        match self.0.iter_mut().find(|(key, _)| key == k) {
            Some(e) => e.1 = v,
            None => self.0.push((k.to_owned(), v)),
        }
    }

    pub fn remove(&mut self, k: &str) {
        self.0.retain(|(key, _)| key != k)
    }

    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.0.is_empty()
    }

    /// Two-space indent at this level; nested values keep their bytes.
    pub fn render(&self) -> String {
        if self.0.is_empty() {
            return "{}".into();
        }
        let mut s = String::from("{\n");
        for (i, (k, v)) in self.0.iter().enumerate() {
            s.push_str("  ");
            s.push_str(&serde_json::to_string(k).expect("a string serializes"));
            s.push_str(": ");
            s.push_str(v.get());
            s.push_str(if i + 1 < self.0.len() { ",\n" } else { "\n" });
        }
        s.push('}');
        s
    }
}

pub fn raw(v: &serde_json::Value) -> Box<RawValue> {
    RawValue::from_string(serde_json::to_string_pretty(v).expect("json")).expect("valid json")
}

pub fn raw_text(s: String) -> anyhow::Result<Box<RawValue>> {
    Ok(RawValue::from_string(s)?)
}
```

`hooks_install/entries.rs`:

```rust
//! The hook entries acd owns (spec §6.2, §6.3 tables). Every entry carries
//! `acd-managed:v1`; ownership is matched by that marker only.
use serde_json::{Value, json};

use super::{Ctx, MARKER};

/// Claude: `(event, matcher, timeout seconds)`. No SessionStart: Claude
/// never sends it over http (gate 2 draft).
pub fn claude_events(permission_hold: u64) -> Vec<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>, u64)> {
    vec![
        ("UserPromptSubmit", None, 1),
        ("PermissionRequest", None, permission_hold + 10),
        ("PreToolUse", Some("AskUserQuestion"), 1),
        ("PostToolUse", None, 1),
        ("Stop", None, 1),
        ("SessionEnd", None, 1),
    ]
}

pub const CODEX_EVENTS: [(&str, u64); 5] =
    [("SessionStart", 2), ("UserPromptSubmit", 2), ("PermissionRequest", 1), ("PostToolUse", 2), ("Stop", 2)];

pub fn claude_group(ctx: &Ctx, token: &str, event: &str, matcher: Option<&str>, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let entry = json!({
        "type": "http",
        "url": format!("http://127.0.0.1:{}/hook/claude/{event}?marker={MARKER}", ctx.hook_port),
        "timeout": timeout,
        "headers": {
            "X-Acd-Token": token,
            "X-Herdr-Pane": "$HERDR_PANE_ID",
            "X-Herdr-Socket": "$HERDR_SOCKET_PATH",
            "X-Claude-Entrypoint": "$CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"
        },
        "allowedEnvVars": ["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]
    });
    match matcher {
        Some(m) => json!({"matcher": m, "hooks": [entry]}),
        None => json!({"hooks": [entry]}),
    }
}

/// POSIX single-quote quoting.
pub fn sh_quote(s: &str) -> String {
    format!("'{}'", s.replace('\'', "'\\''"))
}

pub fn codex_group(ctx: &Ctx, event: &str, timeout: u64) -> Value {
    let command = format!(
        "{} hook codex {event} --config-dir {} {MARKER}",
        sh_quote(&ctx.exe.to_string_lossy()),
        sh_quote(&ctx.config_dir.to_string_lossy())
    );
    json!({"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": command, "timeout": timeout}]})
}

// Ownership (`classify`) is added in Step 6.
```

```rust
//! `acd hooks install|uninstall claude|codex` (spec §6.1 Installation).
// Task 24 uses everything here; until then nothing calls it.
#![allow(dead_code)]
mod entries;
mod json;

use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result};

pub const MARKER: &str = "acd-managed:v1";

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, clap::ValueEnum)]
pub enum Target {
    Claude,
    Codex,
}

pub struct Ctx {
    pub home: PathBuf,
    pub claude_dir: PathBuf,
    pub codex_dir: PathBuf,
    pub config_dir: PathBuf,
    pub exe: PathBuf,
    pub hook_port: u16,
    pub permission_hold: u64,
}

impl Ctx {
    pub fn from_env(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<Ctx> {
        let home = PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("HOME").context("HOME is not set")?);
        let cfg = crate::config::Config::load(config_dir)?;
        let env_dir = |k: &str| std::env::var_os(k).filter(|v| !v.is_empty()).map(PathBuf::from);
        Ok(Ctx {
            claude_dir: env_dir("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".claude")),
            codex_dir: env_dir("CODEX_HOME").unwrap_or_else(|| home.join(".codex")),
            home,
            config_dir: std::fs::canonicalize(config_dir)?,
            exe: std::fs::canonicalize(std::env::current_exe()?)?,
            hook_port: cfg.hook_port,
            permission_hold: cfg.claude.permission_hold,
        })
    }

    pub fn settings_path(&self, t: Target) -> PathBuf {
        match t {
            Target::Claude => self.claude_dir.join("settings.json"),
            Target::Codex => self.codex_dir.join("hooks.json"),
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Exact ownership and raw group edits (D16, D22; Sol I5)**

Revision 1 called any entry whose URL or command contained the marker
"owned", so a foreign hook that merely mentioned it would be removed or
get the token. Ownership is now exact. Add to `entries.rs`:

```rust
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Ownership {
    /// Exactly acd's entry for this event.
    Owned,
    /// Mentions the marker but is not exactly acd's entry: never edited,
    /// never given a token; install and uninstall warn, rotation refuses.
    Suspicious,
    Foreign,
}

/// Splits a command into words: bare words without shell metacharacters
/// and single-quoted words (`'\''` escapes). Anything else is `None`.
pub fn sh_words(cmd: &str) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    let mut words = Vec::new();
    let mut cur = String::new();
    let mut in_word = false;
    let mut chars = cmd.chars().peekable();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        match c {
            ' ' => {
                if in_word {
                    words.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur));
                    in_word = false;
                }
            }
            '\'' => {
                in_word = true;
                loop {
                    match chars.next()? {
                        '\'' => break,
                        ch => cur.push(ch),
                    }
                }
                // `'\''`: a closing quote, an escaped quote, a new quote.
                if chars.peek() == Some(&'\\') {
                    chars.next();
                    if chars.next()? != '\'' {
                        return None;
                    }
                    cur.push('\'');
                    if chars.next()? != '\'' {
                        return None;
                    }
                    loop {
                        match chars.next()? {
                            '\'' => break,
                            ch => cur.push(ch),
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            c if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || "-_./:".contains(c) => {
                in_word = true;
                cur.push(c);
            }
            _ => return None, // ; $ ` " \ | & ( ) < > and anything else
        }
    }
    if in_word {
        words.push(cur);
    }
    Some(words)
}

fn claude_owned(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
    if e.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("http") {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(url) = e.get("url").and_then(Value::as_str) else { return false };
    let Some(rest) = url.strip_prefix("http://127.0.0.1:") else { return false };
    let Some((port, path)) = rest.split_once('/') else { return false };
    port.parse::<u16>().is_ok_and(|p| p > 0)
        && !port.starts_with('0')
        && path == format!("hook/claude/{event}?marker={MARKER}")
}

fn codex_owned(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> bool {
    if e.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("command") {
        return false;
    }
    let Some(words) = e.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str).and_then(sh_words) else { return false };
    matches!(words.as_slice(),
        [exe, h, c, ev, flag, dir, marker]
            if exe.starts_with('/') && h == "hook" && c == "codex" && ev == event
               && flag == "--config-dir" && dir.starts_with('/') && marker == MARKER)
}

pub fn classify(event: &str, e: &serde_json::Value) -> Ownership {
    if claude_owned(event, e) || codex_owned(event, e) {
        Ownership::Owned
    } else if e.to_string().contains(MARKER) {
        Ownership::Suspicious
    } else {
        Ownership::Foreign
    }
}
```

Add to `json.rs` a group editor that keeps foreign entries as raw bytes:

```rust
pub enum GroupEdit {
    /// No owned entry: keep the group's bytes.
    Keep,
    /// Only owned entries: drop the group.
    Drop,
    /// Owned entries removed; every other key and entry keeps its bytes.
    Rewrite(Box<RawValue>),
}

/// `classify` is `entries::classify` bound to the event.
pub fn edit_group(group: &RawValue, classify: impl Fn(&serde_json::Value) -> bool) -> anyhow::Result<GroupEdit> {
    let mut obj = Obj::parse(group.get())?;
    let Some(list) = obj.get("hooks") else { return Ok(GroupEdit::Keep) };
    let items: Vec<Box<RawValue>> = serde_json::from_str(list.get())?;
    let mut kept = Vec::new();
    let mut dropped = 0;
    for item in items {
        let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(item.get())?;
        if classify(&v) {
            dropped += 1;
        } else {
            kept.push(item);
        }
    }
    Ok(match (dropped, kept.is_empty()) {
        (0, _) => GroupEdit::Keep,
        (_, true) => GroupEdit::Drop,
        _ => {
            obj.set("hooks", raw_text(render_array(&kept))?);
            GroupEdit::Rewrite(raw_text(obj.render())?)
        }
    })
}

pub fn render_array(items: &[Box<RawValue>]) -> String {
    if items.is_empty() {
        return "[]".into();
    }
    format!("[\n{}\n]", items.iter().map(|i| i.get().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(",\n"))
}
```

```rust
    #[test]

        let c = ctx();
        let owned_c = claude_group(&c, "tok", "Stop", None, 1)["hooks"][0].clone();
        let owned_x = codex_group(&c, "Stop", 2)["hooks"][0].clone();
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &owned_c), Ownership::Owned);
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &owned_x), Ownership::Owned);
        assert_eq!(classify("PostToolUse", &owned_c), Ownership::Suspicious, "wrong event");
        for v in [
            serde_json::json!({"type": "command", "command": "echo acd-managed:v1 >> log"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "https://example.com/x?note=acd-managed:v1"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "http://10.0.0.1:47801/hook/claude/Stop?marker=acd-managed:v1"}),
            serde_json::json!({"type": "http", "url": "http://127.0.0.1:47801/hook/claude/Stop?marker=acd-managed:v1&x=1"}),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &v), Ownership::Suspicious, "{v}");
        }
        assert_eq!(classify("Stop", &serde_json::json!({"type": "command", "command": "say done"})), Ownership::Foreign);
    }

    #[test]
    fn sh_words_round_trips_the_quoting() {
        assert_eq!(sh_words(&format!("{} hook", sh_quote("/opt/my tools/a'b"))).unwrap(), vec!["/opt/my tools/a'b", "hook"]);
        for bad in ["a;b", "a $(x)", "a \"b\"", "a `b`", "a\\b", "'open"] {
            assert!(sh_words(bad).is_none(), "{bad}");
        }
    }
```

```rust
    fn a_mixed_group_keeps_foreign_entries_byte_exact() {

        let raw = RawValue::from_string(group.into()).unwrap();
        let GroupEdit::Rewrite(out) = edit_group(&raw, |v| v["url"] == "OWNED").unwrap() else { panic!() };
        assert!(out.get().contains(r#"{"type":"command",   "command":"guard.sh"}"#), "{}", out.get());
        assert!(out.get().contains(r#""matcher": "Bash""#) || out.get().contains(r#""matcher":"Bash""#));
        assert!(!out.get().contains("OWNED"));
    }
```

### Task 24: `acd hooks install|uninstall claude|codex`

Spec §6.1 "Installation" and "Token storage", §14 item 9, and Sol I4 to
I6: one acd writer at a time, change detection before the rename, a
journal for settings plus record, records keyed by canonical
destination, Git tracked/untracked/unknown, and a Git recheck before
every token-bearing write.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/git.rs`, `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/journal.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs` (lock, records, install, uninstall, token rewrite)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`Hooks` subcommand)
- Test: `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 21, 23.
- Produces:
  - `hooks_install::Flags { allow_tracked, allow_unknown_git }`.
  - `hooks_install::install(ctx: &Ctx, target: Target, flags: Flags) -> Result<Report>`,
    `hooks_install::uninstall(ctx: &Ctx, target: Target) -> Result<Report>`,
    `hooks_install::rewrite_claude_token(ctx: &Ctx, held: &WriterLock, token: &str, flags: Flags) -> Result<RewriteReport>`
    (the caller holds the writer lock; a second `flock` on a new
    descriptor in the same process would block)
    (`RewriteReport { updated: Vec<PathBuf>, failed: Vec<(PathBuf, String)> }`; Task 27 calls it).
  - `hooks_install::Record { version, files: BTreeMap<PathBuf, FileRecord> }` at
    `<config_dir>/hooks-installed.json` (0600), keyed by canonical destination;
    `hooks_install::recorded_claude_roots(config_dir) -> Vec<PathBuf>` (Task 30).
  - `hooks_install::{WriterLock, lock_writers}`: re-exports of Task 21's
    `writer_lock::{WriterLock, acquire}` (T6).
  - `git::{GitStatus, status, check}`, `journal::{Journal, recover}`.
  - CLI: `acd hooks install claude|codex [--allow-tracked] [--allow-unknown-git]`,
    `acd hooks uninstall claude|codex`.

```rust
fn install(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str, extra: &[&str]) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "install", target]).args(extra).output().unwrap()
}

fn uninstall(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path, target: &str) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "uninstall", target]).output().unwrap()
}

fn json_of(p: &std::path::Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(p).unwrap()).unwrap()
}

fn owned_count(v: &serde_json::Value) -> usize {
    v["hooks"].as_object().map_or(0, |events| events.values()
        .flat_map(|groups| groups.as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .flat_map(|g| g["hooks"].as_array().into_iter().flatten())
        .filter(|e| e.to_string().contains("acd-managed:v1"))
        .count())
}

const FOREIGN_CLAUDE: &str = r#"{
 "permissions": {"allow": ["Bash(npm test)"]},
 "hooks": {
  "Stop": [{"hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "say done"}]}],
  "PreToolUse": [{"matcher": "Bash", "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "guard.sh"}]}]
 },
 "model": "opus"
}"#;

fn claude_install_writes_owned_entries_with_the_token_at_mode_0600() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), "hook_port = 47999\n").unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    let settings = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    assert_eq!(mode(&settings), 0o600);
    let v = json_of(&settings);
    let token = hook_token_of(&dir);
    for (event, timeout) in [("UserPromptSubmit", 1), ("PermissionRequest", 3610), ("PreToolUse", 1), ("PostToolUse", 1), ("Stop", 1), ("SessionEnd", 1)] {
        let e = &v["hooks"][event][0]["hooks"][0];
        assert_eq!(e["type"], "http", "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["timeout"], timeout, "{event}");
        assert_eq!(e["url"], format!("http://127.0.0.1:47999/hook/claude/{event}?marker=acd-managed:v1"));
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"], token.as_str());
        assert_eq!(e["headers"]["X-Herdr-Pane"], "$HERDR_PANE_ID");
        assert_eq!(e["allowedEnvVars"], serde_json::json!(["HERDR_PANE_ID", "HERDR_SOCKET_PATH", "CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT"]));
    }
    assert_eq!(v["hooks"]["PreToolUse"][0]["matcher"], "AskUserQuestion");
    assert!(v["hooks"].get("SessionStart").is_none(), "Claude sends no http SessionStart (gate 2)");
    assert_eq!(mode(&dir.join("hooks-installed.json")), 0o600);
    for o in [&out.stdout, &out.stderr] {
        assert!(!String::from_utf8_lossy(o).contains(&token), "the token is never printed");
    }
}

fn install_twice_then_uninstall_is_identity() {
    for (target, rel, original) in [
        ("claude", ".claude/settings.json", FOREIGN_CLAUDE),

        let path = home.path().join(rel);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, original).unwrap();

        assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), target, &[]).status.success());
        let v = json_of(&path);
        assert_eq!(owned_count(&v), if target == "claude" { 6 } else { 5 }, "{target}: one entry per event");
        let out = uninstall(&dir, home.path(), target);
        assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
        assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(original).unwrap(), "{target}");
    }

#[test]
fn refuses_symlink_and_tracked_settings() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let claude = home.path().join(".claude");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&claude).unwrap();
    let real = home.path().join("dotfiles-settings.json");
    std::fs::write(&real, "{}").unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&real, claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("symlink"));
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&real).unwrap(), "{}", "untouched");

    std::fs::remove_file(claude.join("settings.json")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(claude.join("settings.json"), "{}").unwrap();
    let git = |args: &[&str]| std::process::Command::new("git").current_dir(&claude).args(args)
        .env("HOME", home.path()).env("GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM", "1").env("GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL", "/dev/null")
        .env("GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "t").env("GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL", "t@t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_NAME", "t").env("GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL", "t@t")
        .output().unwrap();
    git(&["init", "-q"]);
    git(&["add", "settings.json"]);
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
    assert!(err.contains("tracked by Git") && err.contains("--allow-tracked"), "{err}");
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-tracked"]).status.success());
}

#[test]
fn uninstall_survives_claude_reformatting() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]);
    // Claude rewrites the whole file (indent 2) when it persists a rule (gate 2e).
    let mut v = json_of(&path);
    v["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    std::fs::write(&path, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    let mut want: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    want["permissions"]["allow"].as_array_mut().unwrap().push("Bash(touch /tmp/*)".into());
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), want);
}

    assert!(dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
    assert_eq!(owned_count(&json_of(&path)), 6, "settings were written");
    // The next command recovers the record, so uninstall restores the original.

    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap());
    assert!(!dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Git status (`hooks_install/git.rs`, D23)**

```rust
//! Whether Git tracks a settings file: tracked, untracked or unknown.
//! Unknown (git missing or failing) is refused unless the user says
//! otherwise (Sol I6).
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

use anyhow::{Result, bail};

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum GitStatus {
    Tracked,
    Untracked,
    Unknown(String),
}

fn git_program() -> String {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Ok(p) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_GIT") {
        return p;
    }
    "git".into()
}

/// Works for an absent file too: a deleted file that is still in the
/// index counts as tracked.
pub fn status(path: &Path) -> GitStatus {
    let Some(name) = path.file_name() else { return GitStatus::Unknown("no file name".into()) };
    let mut dir = path.parent().map(Path::to_path_buf).unwrap_or_default();
    let mut rel = std::path::PathBuf::from(name);
    while !dir.is_dir() {
        let Some(parent) = dir.parent() else { return GitStatus::Untracked };
        rel = std::path::PathBuf::from(dir.file_name().unwrap_or_default()).join(rel);
        dir = parent.to_path_buf();
    }
    let out = Command::new(git_program())
        .arg("-C").arg(&dir)
        .args(["ls-files", "--error-unmatch", "--"]).arg(&rel)
        .env("GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT", "0")
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .output();
    match out {
        Err(e) => GitStatus::Unknown(format!("git could not run: {e}")),
        Ok(o) if o.status.success() => GitStatus::Tracked,
        Ok(o) => {
            let err = String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr);
            match o.status.code() {
                Some(1) => GitStatus::Untracked,
                Some(128) if err.contains("not a git repository") => GitStatus::Untracked,
                _ => GitStatus::Unknown(err.lines().next().unwrap_or("git failed").to_owned()),
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn check(path: &Path, allow_tracked: bool, allow_unknown: bool) -> Result<()> {
    match status(path) {
        GitStatus::Untracked => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Tracked if allow_tracked => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Tracked => bail!(
            "{} is tracked by Git, and the hook token would be committed. Untrack it, or pass --allow-tracked",
            path.display()
        ),
        GitStatus::Unknown(_) if allow_unknown => Ok(()),
        GitStatus::Unknown(why) => bail!(
            "cannot tell whether {} is tracked by Git ({why}). Fix Git, or pass --allow-unknown-git",
            path.display()
        ),
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The journal (`hooks_install/journal.rs`)**

```rust
//! Settings and record updates are journaled (Sol I4): the journal names
//! the destination, its content hash before and after, and the record
//! entry that belongs to "after". Recovery applies the record only if the
//! settings file is in the "after" state.
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use super::{FileRecord, Record};

pub const JOURNAL_FILE: &str = "hooks-journal.json";

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Journal {
    pub destination: PathBuf,
    pub before: Option<String>,
    pub after: Option<String>,
    /// `None` means the record entry is removed (uninstall).
    pub record_after: Option<FileRecord>,
}

pub fn digest(text: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    text.map(|t| Sha256::digest(t.as_bytes()).iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect())
}

pub fn begin(config_dir: &Path, j: &Journal) -> Result<()> {
    crate::secrets::write_structured(&config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(j)?)
}

pub fn finish(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    match std::fs::remove_file(config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE)) {
        Err(e) if e.kind() != std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Err(e.into()),
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

/// Run at the start of every installer command, under the writer lock.
pub fn recover(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let path = config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE);
    if path.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let j: Journal = serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_structured(&path)?)?;
    let now = digest(super::read_settings(&j.destination)?.as_deref());
    if now == j.after {
        let mut record = Record::load(config_dir)?;
        match j.record_after {
            Some(r) => record.files.insert(j.destination.clone(), r),
            None => record.files.remove(&j.destination),
        };
        record.save(config_dir)?;
    } else if now != j.before {
        eprintln!(
            "warning: {} changed since an interrupted acd hooks command; its install record was left as it was",
            j.destination.display()
        );
    }
    finish(config_dir)
}
```

```rust
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

use anyhow::bail;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use serde_json::value::RawValue;

use self::entries::{Ownership, classify};
use self::json::{GroupEdit, Obj, edit_group, raw, raw_text, render_array};
use self::journal::{Journal, digest};

pub const RECORD_FILE: &str = "hooks-installed.json";
```

`Target` also derives `Serialize, Deserialize` with
`#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]`. Then add:

```rust
/// One acd writer at a time (Sol I4, T6): Task 21's lock.
pub use crate::writer_lock::{WriterLock, acquire as lock_writers};

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct FileRecord {
    pub target: Target,
    pub created_file: bool,
    pub created_hooks_key: bool,
    pub created_events: Vec<String>,
}

/// Keyed by canonical destination (D24): a changed `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`
/// is a second destination, not a rewrite of the first.
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Default, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Record {
    pub version: u32,
    pub files: BTreeMap<PathBuf, FileRecord>,
}

impl Record {
    pub fn load(dir: &Path) -> Result<Record> {
        let p = dir.join(RECORD_FILE);
        if p.symlink_metadata().is_err() {
            return Ok(Record { version: 2, ..Default::default() });
        }
        Ok(serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_structured(&p)?)?) // F1
    }
    pub fn save(&self, dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        crate::hook_token::fail_at("record-write")?;
        crate::secrets::write_structured(&dir.join(RECORD_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(self)?)
    }
    pub fn claude_destinations(&self) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
        self.files.iter().filter(|(_, r)| r.target == Target::Claude).map(|(p, _)| p.clone()).collect()
    }
}

/// The `sessions` dirs of every recorded Claude destination (Task 30
/// feeds them to the registry scan, D24).
#[allow(dead_code)] // called from Task 30, which removes this line
pub fn recorded_claude_roots(config_dir: &Path) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    Record::load(config_dir)
        .map(|r| r.claude_destinations().iter().filter_map(|p| p.parent().map(|d| d.join("sessions"))).collect())
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn canonical_destination(path: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    let parent = path.parent().context("settings path has no parent")?;
    std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
    Ok(std::fs::canonicalize(parent)?.join(path.file_name().context("settings path has no file name")?))
}

pub struct Report {
    pub path: PathBuf,
    pub backup: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub removed_file: bool,
    /// Entries that mention the marker but are not exactly acd's (D22).
    pub suspicious: usize,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Default)]
pub struct Flags {
    pub allow_tracked: bool,
    pub allow_unknown_git: bool,
}

/// Drops owned entries from every event; returns how many suspicious
/// entries were left in place.
fn strip_owned(hooks: &mut Obj) -> Result<usize> {
    let mut suspicious = 0;
    for (event, groups_raw) in hooks.0.iter_mut() {
        let Ok(groups) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Box<RawValue>>>(groups_raw.get()) else { continue };
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut changed = false;
        for g in groups {
            let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(g.get())?;
            suspicious += v["hooks"].as_array().map_or(0, |a| a.iter().filter(|e| classify(event, e) == Ownership::Suspicious).count());
            match edit_group(&g, |e| classify(event, e) == Ownership::Owned)? {
                GroupEdit::Keep => out.push(g),
                GroupEdit::Drop => changed = true,
                GroupEdit::Rewrite(r) => {
                    changed = true;
                    out.push(r);
                }
            }
        }
        if changed {
            *groups_raw = raw_text(render_array(&out))?;
        }
    }
    Ok(suspicious)
}

fn groups_for(ctx: &Ctx, held: &WriterLock, t: Target) -> Result<Vec<(String, Value)>> {
    Ok(match t {
        Target::Claude => {
            // Under the lock the caller holds: migrate, never reacquire (T6).
            let token = crate::hook_token::migrate_locked(&ctx.config_dir, held)?.current;
            entries::claude_events(ctx.permission_hold)
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(e, m, t)| (e.to_string(), entries::claude_group(ctx, &token, e, m, t)))
                .collect()
        }
        Target::Codex => entries::CODEX_EVENTS.iter().map(|(e, t)| (e.to_string(), entries::codex_group(ctx, e, *t))).collect(),
    })
}

/// Refuses symlinks and foreign owners. `None` when the file is absent.
pub(crate) fn read_settings(path: &Path) -> Result<Option<String>> {
    use std::os::unix::fs::MetadataExt;
    match std::fs::symlink_metadata(path) {
        Ok(m) if m.file_type().is_symlink() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: it is a symlink", path.display()),
        Ok(m) if m.uid() != acd_securefs::euid() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: it is not owned by you", path.display()),
        Ok(m) if !m.is_file() => bail!("refusing to edit {}: not a regular file", path.display()),
        Ok(_) => Ok(Some(std::fs::read_to_string(path)?)),
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
        Err(e) => Err(e).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display())),
    }
}

fn backup(ctx: &Ctx, path: &Path, text: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    let dir = ctx.config_dir.join("backups");
    crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(&dir)?;
    let ms = std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_millis();
    let name = path.file_name().map(|n| n.to_string_lossy().into_owned()).unwrap_or_default();
    let b = dir.join(format!("{name}.{ms}.bak"));
    crate::secrets::write_atomic(&b, text.as_bytes(), 0o600)?;
    Ok(b)
}

fn file_mode(path: &Path, t: Target) -> u32 {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    match t {
        Target::Claude => 0o600, // holds the token (spec §6.1)
        Target::Codex => std::fs::metadata(path).map(|m| m.permissions().mode() & 0o777).unwrap_or(0o600),
    }
}

/// Debug-only: simulates Claude rewriting the file between acd's read and
/// its rename (`ACD_TEST_TOUCH_SETTINGS=<content>`, first attempt only).
fn test_touch(path: &Path, attempt: usize) {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if attempt == 0
        && let Ok(content) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_TOUCH_SETTINGS")
    {
        let _ = std::fs::write(path, content);
    }
    let _ = (path, attempt);
}

/// Edits the settings at `path` with `edit`, journaled and with change
/// detection: if the file changed between the read and the rename, the
/// edit is redone on the new content (at most 3 attempts).
fn edit_settings(
    ctx: &Ctx,
    t: Target,
    path: &Path,
    flags: Flags,
    record_after: impl Fn(Option<&str>, &Obj) -> Option<FileRecord>,
    edit: impl Fn(&mut Obj) -> Result<usize>,
) -> Result<Report> {
    let dest = canonical_destination(path)?;
    for attempt in 0..3 {
        let before = read_settings(path)?;
        if t == Target::Claude {
            git::check(path, flags.allow_tracked, flags.allow_unknown_git)?; // before every token-bearing write
        }
        let mut doc = Obj::parse(before.as_deref().unwrap_or_default())
            .with_context(|| format!("{} is not a JSON object", path.display()))?;
        let suspicious = edit(&mut doc)?;
        let remove = doc.is_empty() && record_after(before.as_deref(), &doc).is_none()
            && Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.files.get(&dest).is_some_and(|r| r.created_file);
        let text = if remove { None } else { Some(format!("{}\n", doc.render())) };
        let backup = before.as_deref().map(|b| backup(ctx, path, b)).transpose()?;
        let rec = record_after(before.as_deref(), &doc);
        journal::begin(&ctx.config_dir, &Journal {
            destination: dest.clone(),
            before: digest(before.as_deref()),
            after: digest(text.as_deref()),
            record_after: rec.clone(),
        })?;
        test_touch(path, attempt);
        if read_settings(path)? != before {
            journal::finish(&ctx.config_dir)?;
            continue; // someone else wrote it: redo on the new content
        }
        // J1: a failure before the rename changed nothing; drop the journal.
        let written = crate::hook_token::fail_at("settings-write").and_then(|()| match &text {
            Some(text) => crate::secrets::write_atomic(path, text.as_bytes(), file_mode(path, t)),
            None => std::fs::remove_file(path).map_err(Into::into),
        });
        if let Err(e) = written {
            journal::finish(&ctx.config_dir)?;
            return Err(e);
        }
        let mut record = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?;
        match rec {
            Some(r) => record.files.insert(dest.clone(), r),
            None => record.files.remove(&dest),
        };
        record.save(&ctx.config_dir)?; // a failure here is repaired by `journal::recover`
        journal::finish(&ctx.config_dir)?;
        return Ok(Report { path: path.to_path_buf(), backup, removed_file: text.is_none(), suspicious });
    }
    bail!("{} kept changing while acd edited it; try again", path.display())
}

pub fn install(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target, flags: Flags) -> Result<Report> {
    let report = install_entries(ctx, t, flags)?; // takes and releases the writer lock
    // Task 27 adds the header repair here, after the lock is released.
    Ok(report)
}

fn install_entries(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target, flags: Flags) -> Result<Report> {
    let held = lock_writers(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    journal::recover(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let path = ctx.settings_path(t);
    let dest = canonical_destination(&path)?;
    let groups = groups_for(ctx, &held, t)?;
    let prior = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.files.get(&dest).cloned();
    let created = std::cell::RefCell::new((false, Vec::<String>::new()));
    let report = edit_settings(
        ctx, t, &path, flags,
        |before, _| {
            let (hooks_key, events) = created.borrow().clone();
            let mut r = prior.clone().unwrap_or(FileRecord {
                target: t, created_file: before.is_none(), created_hooks_key: false, created_events: vec![],
            });
            r.created_hooks_key |= hooks_key;
            for e in events {
                if !r.created_events.contains(&e) {
                    r.created_events.push(e);
                }
            }
            Some(r)
        },
        |doc| {
            let mut c = created.borrow_mut();
            *c = (false, Vec::new());
            let mut hooks = match doc.get("hooks") {
                Some(h) => Obj::parse(h.get())?,
                None => {
                    c.0 = true;
                    Obj::default()
                }
            };
            let suspicious = strip_owned(&mut hooks)?;
            for (event, group) in &groups {
                let mut items: Vec<Box<RawValue>> = match hooks.get(event) {
                    Some(a) => serde_json::from_str(a.get()).with_context(|| format!("hooks.{event} is not an array"))?,
                    None => {
                        c.1.push(event.clone());
                        Vec::new()
                    }
                };
                items.push(raw(group));
                hooks.set(event, raw_text(render_array(&items))?);
            }
            doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
            Ok(suspicious)
        },
    )?;
    Ok(report)
}

pub fn uninstall(ctx: &Ctx, t: Target) -> Result<Report> {
    let _lock = lock_writers(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    journal::recover(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let path = ctx.settings_path(t);
    let dest = canonical_destination(&path)?;
    let rec = Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.files.get(&dest).cloned();
    let created_events = rec.as_ref().map(|r| r.created_events.clone()).unwrap_or_default();
    let created_hooks_key = rec.as_ref().is_some_and(|r| r.created_hooks_key);
    // Removing the token never needs the Git check.
    edit_settings(ctx, t, &path, Flags { allow_tracked: true, allow_unknown_git: true },
        |_, _| None,
        |doc| {
            let Some(h) = doc.get("hooks") else { return Ok(0) };
            let mut hooks = Obj::parse(h.get())?;
            let suspicious = strip_owned(&mut hooks)?;
            for event in &created_events {
                if hooks.get(event).is_some_and(|a| serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Value>>(a.get()).is_ok_and(|v| v.is_empty())) {
                    hooks.remove(event);
                }
            }
            if hooks.is_empty() && created_hooks_key {
                doc.remove("hooks");
            } else {
                doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
            }
            Ok(suspicious)
        },
    )
}

pub struct RewriteReport {
    pub updated: Vec<PathBuf>,
    pub failed: Vec<(PathBuf, String)>,
}

/// Task 27: sets `X-Acd-Token` on every owned Claude entry of every
/// recorded destination. Idempotent. A destination with a suspicious
/// entry, a symlink, or a Git status the flags do not allow fails and is
/// reported; the others are still updated.
#[allow(dead_code)] // called from Task 27, which removes this line
pub fn rewrite_claude_token(ctx: &Ctx, _held: &WriterLock, token: &str, flags: Flags) -> Result<RewriteReport> {
    journal::recover(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let mut report = RewriteReport { updated: vec![], failed: vec![] };
    for dest in Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.claude_destinations() {
        let result = edit_settings(ctx, Target::Claude, &dest, flags,
            |_, _| Record::load(&ctx.config_dir).ok().and_then(|r| r.files.get(&dest).cloned()),
            |doc| {
                let Some(h) = doc.get("hooks") else { return Ok(0) };
                let mut hooks = Obj::parse(h.get())?;
                let mut suspicious = 0;
                for (event, groups_raw) in hooks.0.iter_mut() {
                    let Ok(mut groups) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<Value>>(groups_raw.get()) else { continue };
                    let mut touched = false;
                    for g in groups.iter_mut() {
                        for e in g.get_mut("hooks").and_then(Value::as_array_mut).into_iter().flatten() {
                            match classify(event, e) {
                                Ownership::Owned if e.get("headers").is_some() => {
                                    e["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"] = token.into();
                                    touched = true;
                                }
                                Ownership::Suspicious => suspicious += 1,
                                _ => {}
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    if touched {
                        *groups_raw = raw(&Value::Array(groups));
                    }
                }
                if suspicious > 0 {
                    bail!("{suspicious} unexpected entries carry the acd marker; remove them by hand");
                }
                doc.set("hooks", raw_text(hooks.render())?);
                Ok(0)
            },
        );
        match result {
            Ok(_) => report.updated.push(dest),
            Err(e) => report.failed.push((dest, format!("{e:#}"))),
        }
    }
    Ok(report)
}
```

(In `rewrite_claude_token` the groups of an event with owned entries are
re-serialized as a whole; a foreign entry that shares a group with an
owned one is not byte-preserved there. Install and uninstall, which run
far more often, preserve it exactly.)

```rust
        Cmd::Hooks { cmd } => {
            if !dir.is_dir() {
                bail!("{} not initialized; run `acd init`", dir.display());
            }
            preflight(&dir)?;
            let ctx = hooks_install::Ctx::from_env(&dir)?;
            match cmd {

                    let r = hooks_install::install(&ctx, target, hooks_install::Flags { allow_tracked, allow_unknown_git })?;

                    }
                    println!("Installed acd hooks in {}", r.path.display());
                    if let Some(b) = r.backup {
                        println!("Backup: {}", b.display());
                    }
                    match target {
                        hooks_install::Target::Claude => println!(
                            "Claude reads hooks when a session starts: restart running sessions or run /clear in them."
                        ),
                        hooks_install::Target::Codex => println!(
                            "Codex may ask you to review these hooks the first time it runs them. Codex sessions that share Codex's background app-server can be answered from the inbox only when acd can tell which herdr pane they run in; otherwise their prompts are shown read-only."
                        ),
                    }
                }
                HooksCmd::Uninstall { target } => {
                    let r = hooks_install::uninstall(&ctx, target)?;
                    if r.removed_file {
                        println!("Removed {} (acd created it)", r.path.display());
                    } else {
                        println!("Removed acd hooks from {}", r.path.display());
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(())
        }
```

### Task 25: Installer journal conflicts and recovery

Installer journal contract J1 to J4 (Sol N6) and F1's size tests (Sol
N9). Task 24's journal deletes itself whenever the settings file is in
neither its `before` nor its `after` state, so a Claude rewrite between
acd's settings write and its record save loses the install record; and a
multi-destination token rewrite keeps going after a record failure,
overwriting the unresolved journal. Here recovery rebuilds ownership from
the acd entries the file holds, an unreadable file becomes an explicit
conflict that every installer command reports, `begin` never overwrites,
and the batch stops.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/journal.rs`, `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`acd hooks status`)
- Test: unit tests in `journal.rs` and `mod.rs`, `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 21, 24.
- Produces:
  - `journal::Journal` gains `target: Target` and
    `conflict: Option<String>` (`#[serde(default)]`).
  - `journal::begin` refuses when a journal exists (J1);
    `journal::pending(config_dir) -> bool`.
  - `journal::recover(config_dir) -> Result<()>` follows J2 and J3 (an
    error names the conflict).
  - `hooks_install::owned_entries(text: &str) -> Result<usize>`.
  - `hooks_install::status(ctx: &Ctx) -> Result<String>` (takes the
    writer lock, runs recovery, reports records and any conflict; a
    T6 entry point).
  - CLI: `acd hooks status`.
  - `rewrite_claude_token` stops at the first failure that leaves a
    journal (J4) and returns that error.
  - `hooks_install::journal` becomes `pub(crate) mod journal` (Task 27
    calls `recover` before rotating).

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::hooks_install::Target;

    fn j(dest: &str) -> Journal {
        Journal { destination: dest.into(), before: None, after: None, record_after: None, target: Target::Claude, conflict: None }
    }

    #[test]
    fn begin_never_overwrites_a_journal() {
        let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(d.path()).unwrap();
        begin(d.path(), &j("/a/settings.json")).unwrap();
        assert!(begin(d.path(), &j("/b/settings.json")).is_err(), "J1");
        let kept: Journal = serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_structured(&d.path().join(JOURNAL_FILE)).unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(kept.destination, std::path::PathBuf::from("/a/settings.json"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_journal_for_a_long_path_round_trips() {
        let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        crate::secrets::ensure_private_dir(d.path()).unwrap();
        let long = format!("/{}/settings.json", "deep/".repeat(900)); // about 4.5 KiB
        begin(d.path(), &j(&long)).unwrap();
        assert!(pending(d.path()));
        let back: Journal = serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_structured(&d.path().join(JOURNAL_FILE)).unwrap()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(back.destination, std::path::PathBuf::from(long), "F1: more than 4 KiB");
    }
}
```

```rust
fn rewrite_like_claude(path: &std::path::Path, edit: impl FnOnce(&mut serde_json::Value)) {
    let mut v = json_of(path);
    edit(&mut v);
    let tmp = path.with_extension("tmp-claude");
    std::fs::write(&tmp, serde_json::to_string_pretty(&v).unwrap()).unwrap();
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::set_permissions(&tmp, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
    std::fs::rename(&tmp, path).unwrap(); // an atomic rewrite, as Claude does (gates)
}

fn hooks_status(dir: &std::path::Path, home: &std::path::Path) -> std::process::Output {
    acd_home(dir, home).args(["hooks", "status"]).output().unwrap()
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn a_rewrite_after_a_record_failure_recovers_ownership_from_the_entries() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT", "record-write")
        .args(["hooks", "install", "claude", "--allow-unknown-git"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("injected failure at record-write"));
    rewrite_like_claude(&path, |v| v["model"] = "opus".into()); // neither before nor after now
    let out = hooks_status(&dir, home.path());
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    assert!(!dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists(), "J2: recovered, not discarded");
    let record = json_of(&dir.join("hooks-installed.json"));
    assert_eq!(record["files"].as_object().unwrap().len(), 1, "the destination is recorded");
    assert!(uninstall(&dir, home.path(), "claude").status.success());
    let mut want: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    want["model"] = "opus".into();
    assert_eq!(json_of(&path), want, "uninstall removed exactly acd's entries");
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn a_record_failure_stops_a_two_destination_rewrite_and_both_recover() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let (a, b) = (home.path().join("cfg-a"), home.path().join("cfg-b"));
    for root in [&a, &b] {
        let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR", root)
            .args(["hooks", "install", "claude", "--allow-unknown-git"]).output().unwrap();
        assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    }
    let old = hook_token_of(&dir);
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT", "record-write")
        .args(["token", "rotate", "hook", "--allow-unknown-git"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("injected failure at record-write"), "the stage was reached");
    assert!(dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists(), "J4: the batch stopped with its journal intact");
    let new = hook_token_of(&dir);
    let token_in = |root: &std::path::Path| json_of(&root.join("settings.json"))["hooks"]["Stop"][0]["hooks"][0]["headers"]["X-Acd-Token"]
        .as_str().unwrap().to_owned();
    let tokens = [token_in(&a), token_in(&b)];
    assert_eq!(tokens.iter().filter(|t| **t == new).count(), 1, "only the first destination was rewritten: {tokens:?}");
    assert_eq!(tokens.iter().filter(|t| **t == old).count(), 1);
    // Repair: recovery applies the journal, then the header update covers both.
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR", &a)
        .args(["hooks", "install", "claude", "--allow-unknown-git"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    assert!(!dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
    assert_eq!((token_in(&a), token_in(&b)), (new.clone(), new));
    assert_eq!(json_of(&dir.join("hooks-installed.json"))["files"].as_object().unwrap().len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir)["headers"]["state"], "done");
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn an_unreadable_settings_file_is_a_reported_conflict() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let path = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(&path, FOREIGN_CLAUDE).unwrap();
    let out = acd_home(&dir, home.path()).env("ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT", "record-write")
        .args(["hooks", "install", "claude", "--allow-unknown-git"]).output().unwrap();
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("injected failure at record-write"));
    let moved = home.path().join("elsewhere.json");
    std::fs::rename(&path, &moved).unwrap();
    std::os::unix::fs::symlink(&moved, &path).unwrap();
    let st = hooks_status(&dir, home.path());
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&st.stdout);
    assert!(text.contains("conflict") && text.contains("symlink"), "{text}");
    assert!(dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists(), "J3: kept");
    let out = install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-unknown-git"]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("settings.json"));
    let before = store_of(&dir);
    assert!(!acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).status().unwrap().success());
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir), before, "rotation refuses while a conflict exists");
    std::fs::remove_file(&path).unwrap();
    std::fs::rename(&moved, &path).unwrap();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-unknown-git"]).status.success());
    assert!(!dir.join("hooks-journal.json").exists());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`journal.rs`:

```rust
#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Journal {
    pub destination: PathBuf,
    pub before: Option<String>,
    pub after: Option<String>,
    /// `None` means the record entry is removed (uninstall).
    pub record_after: Option<FileRecord>,
    pub target: super::Target,
    /// J3: why recovery could not decide; kept until it can.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub conflict: Option<String>,
}

pub fn pending(config_dir: &Path) -> bool {
    config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE).symlink_metadata().is_ok()
}

/// J1: never overwrites an unresolved journal.
pub fn begin(config_dir: &Path, j: &Journal) -> Result<()> {
    if pending(config_dir) {
        anyhow::bail!("an unresolved acd hooks journal exists; run `acd hooks status`");
    }
    crate::secrets::write_structured(&config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(j)?)
}

fn apply(config_dir: &Path, dest: &Path, rec: Option<FileRecord>) -> Result<()> {
    let mut record = Record::load(config_dir)?;
    match rec {
        Some(r) => record.files.insert(dest.to_path_buf(), r),
        None => record.files.remove(dest),
    };
    record.save(config_dir)
}

/// J3: keep the journal, record why, and refuse.
fn conflict(config_dir: &Path, mut j: Journal, why: String) -> Result<()> {
    if j.conflict.as_deref() != Some(why.as_str()) {
        j.conflict = Some(why.clone());
        crate::secrets::write_structured(&config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE), &serde_json::to_string_pretty(&j)?)?;
    }
    anyhow::bail!(
        "an interrupted acd hooks command left {} unresolved: {why}. Fix the file, then run `acd hooks status` or any `acd hooks` command again",
        j.destination.display()
    )
}

/// J2 and J3. Run at the start of every installer entry point, under the
/// writer lock.
pub fn recover(config_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {
    let path = config_dir.join(JOURNAL_FILE);
    if !pending(config_dir) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let j: Journal = serde_json::from_str(&crate::secrets::read_structured(&path)?)?;
    let text = match super::read_settings(&j.destination) {
        Ok(t) => t,
        Err(e) => return conflict(config_dir, j, format!("{e:#}")),
    };
    let now = digest(text.as_deref());
    if now == j.after {
        apply(config_dir, &j.destination, j.record_after.clone())?;
    } else if now != j.before {
        // Someone else (typically Claude) rewrote the file. Ownership
        // follows the acd entries it holds now.
        let owned = match text.as_deref().map(super::owned_entries).transpose() {
            Ok(n) => n.unwrap_or(0),
            Err(e) => return conflict(config_dir, j, format!("not valid JSON: {e:#}")),
        };
        let rec = (owned > 0).then(|| {
            j.record_after.clone().unwrap_or(FileRecord {
                target: j.target,
                created_file: false,
                created_hooks_key: false,
                created_events: vec![],
            })
        });
        eprintln!(
            "note: {} changed during an interrupted acd hooks command; its install record now follows the {owned} acd entries it holds",
            j.destination.display()
        );
        apply(config_dir, &j.destination, rec)?;
    }
    finish(config_dir)
}
```

`mod.rs`:

```rust
/// J2: how many entries are exactly acd's.
pub fn owned_entries(text: &str) -> Result<usize> {
    let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(text)?;
    let Some(hooks) = v.get("hooks").and_then(Value::as_object) else { return Ok(0) };
    Ok(hooks
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|(event, groups)| groups.as_array().into_iter().flatten().map(move |g| (event, g)))
        .flat_map(|(event, g)| g.get("hooks").and_then(Value::as_array).into_iter().flatten().map(move |e| (event, e)))
        .filter(|(event, e)| classify(event, e) == Ownership::Owned)
        .count())
}

/// `acd hooks status`: a T6 entry point.
pub fn status(ctx: &Ctx) -> Result<String> {
    let _held = lock_writers(&ctx.config_dir)?;
    let mut out = String::new();
    match journal::recover(&ctx.config_dir) {
        Ok(()) => out.push_str("journal: none\n"),
        Err(e) => out.push_str(&format!("journal: conflict: {e:#}\n")),
    }
    for (path, r) in Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.files {
        let target = match r.target {
            Target::Claude => "claude",
            Target::Codex => "codex",
        };
        out.push_str(&format!("{target}: {}\n", path.display()));
    }
    Ok(out)
}
```

### Task 26: Hook token retention and retirement

Contract T1, T4, T5 and T6 at the store level (Sol I1, I2, N5, N7; GATE
7). Retirement is a T6 entry point: it takes the writer lock, reads the
clock only after holding it, and does nothing unless the store on disk
equals the store it evaluated, field for field. A scan counts only if it
is complete, started at least `REGISTRY_GRACE_MS` after the headers were
updated, and finished within `fresh_within` of the clock read inside the
lock.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hook_token.rs`
- Test: unit tests in `hook_token.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 21 (`writer_lock`, `save_locked`, `rotate_store_locked`).
- Produces:
  - `hook_token::REGISTRY_GRACE_MS: i64 = 5_000`.
  - `hook_token::LiveSession { session_id: String, started_at_ms: Option<i64> }`.
  - `hook_token::ScanView { started_at_ms: i64, finished_at_ms: i64, live: Vec<LiveSession> }`
    (a complete registry scan, wall-clock times).
  - `hook_token::pending(store: &Store, live: &[LiveSession], on_current: &HashSet<String>) -> usize`.
  - `hook_token::KeepReason { HeadersNotDone, NoFreshScan, Pending(usize) }`,
    `hook_token::Retire { Nothing, Moved, Kept(KeepReason), Retired }`.
  - `hook_token::retire_if_done(dir: &Path, expected: &Store, scan: Option<&ScanView>, fresh_within: Duration, on_current: &HashSet<String>, clock: &dyn Fn() -> i64) -> Result<Retire>`
    (takes the writer lock, so async callers use `spawn_blocking`).
  - `hook_token::status_message(store: &Store, kept: Option<&KeepReason>, rejected: usize, retire_error: Option<&str>) -> Option<(SourceState, String)>`.

```rust
    use std::sync::Arc;
    use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicI64, Ordering};

    fn live(id: &str, started: Option<i64>) -> LiveSession {
        LiveSession { session_id: id.into(), started_at_ms: started }
    }

    /// Generation 2 with a previous token; headers done at `done_at` if given.
    fn rotated(d: &Path, done_at: Option<i64>) -> Store {
        ensure(d, false).unwrap();
        let held = crate::writer_lock::acquire(d).unwrap();
        let mut s = rotate_store_locked(d, &held, 1_000).unwrap();
        if let Some(t) = done_at {
            s.headers = Headers { generation: s.generation, state: HeaderState::Done, updated_at_ms: t, failed: vec![] };
            save_locked(d, &held, &s).unwrap();
        }
        s
    }

    fn scan(started: i64, finished: i64, live: Vec<LiveSession>) -> ScanView {
        ScanView { started_at_ms: started, finished_at_ms: finished, live }
    }

    const FRESH: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
    const DONE: i64 = 2_000;
    const AFTER: i64 = DONE + REGISTRY_GRACE_MS; // the first scan start that counts

    fn at(t: i64) -> impl Fn() -> i64 {
        move || t
    }

    #[test]
    fn retention_excludes_only_sessions_seen_on_the_new_generation() {
        // Sol I1: a session that authenticated before the rotation stays on
        // the old token; only seeing it on the new generation releases it.
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        let sessions = [live("a", Some(500)), live("b", Some(3_000)), live("c", None)];
        let mut on_new = HashSet::new();
        assert_eq!(pending(&s, &sessions, &on_new), 2, "a (older than the headers) and c (unknown start)");
        on_new.insert("a".to_string());
        assert_eq!(pending(&s, &sessions, &on_new), 1, "a moved to the new token (for example after /clear)");
    }

    #[test]
    fn retirement_needs_done_headers_and_a_complete_scan_that_started_after_them() {
        let none = HashSet::new();
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), None); // headers still pending
        assert_eq!(retire_if_done(d.path(), &s, Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![])), FRESH, &none, &at(AFTER)).unwrap(),
                   Retire::Kept(KeepReason::HeadersNotDone));
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        let r = |sc: Option<&ScanView>, now: i64| retire_if_done(d.path(), &s, sc, FRESH, &none, &at(now)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(r(None, AFTER), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan));
        assert_eq!(r(Some(&scan(DONE - 1, AFTER, vec![])), AFTER), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan),
                   "Sol N5: a scan that started before the headers changed does not count");
        assert_eq!(r(Some(&scan(AFTER - 1, AFTER, vec![])), AFTER), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan),
                   "a scan within the registry grace does not count either");
        assert_eq!(r(Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![])), AFTER + 61_000), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan), "stale");
        assert_eq!(r(Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![live("a", Some(10))])), AFTER), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::Pending(1)));
        assert_eq!(r(Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![])), AFTER), Retire::Retired);
        assert!(load(d.path()).unwrap().previous.is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn freshness_is_measured_after_the_lock_is_held() {
        // Sol N5: the scan is fresh when retirement is called, but the lock
        // is held elsewhere until it no longer is.
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        let clock = Arc::new(AtomicI64::new(AFTER));
        let held = crate::writer_lock::acquire(d.path()).unwrap();
        let (p, c, s2) = (d.path().to_path_buf(), clock.clone(), s.clone());
        let t = std::thread::spawn(move || {
            retire_if_done(&p, &s2, Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![])), FRESH, &HashSet::new(), &|| c.load(Ordering::SeqCst)).unwrap()
        });
        // Sol R3-4: advance the clock only once the worker provably waits in
        // `acquire`; a clock read before the lock would see the old time.
        let lock_path = d.path().join(crate::writer_lock::LOCK_FILE);
        let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
        while !crate::writer_lock::tests_support::contended(&lock_path) {
            assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "the worker never reached the contested lock");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
        }
        clock.store(AFTER + 120_000, Ordering::SeqCst); // two minutes pass while it waits
        drop(held);
        assert_eq!(t.join().unwrap(), Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan));
        assert!(load(d.path()).unwrap().previous.is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn retirement_does_nothing_when_any_field_of_the_store_changed() {
        // Same generation, different header state: still "moved" (Sol N5).
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        let mut changed = s.clone();
        changed.headers.updated_at_ms = DONE + 10;
        {
            let held = crate::writer_lock::acquire(d.path()).unwrap();
            save_locked(d.path(), &held, &changed).unwrap();
        }
        assert_eq!(
            retire_if_done(d.path(), &s, Some(&scan(AFTER + 10, AFTER + 10, vec![])), FRESH, &HashSet::new(), &at(AFTER + 10)).unwrap(),
            Retire::Moved
        );
        assert_eq!(load(d.path()).unwrap(), changed, "untouched");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_30_day_cap_always_wins() {
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), None); // headers pending, no scan
        let later = 1_000 + MAX_RETENTION_MS;
        assert_eq!(retire_if_done(d.path(), &s, None, FRESH, &HashSet::new(), &at(later)).unwrap(), Retire::Retired);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failed_retirement_is_an_error_and_keeps_the_previous_token() {
        if acd_securefs::euid() == 0 {
            return; // root writes into a read-only directory anyway
        }
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        std::fs::set_permissions(d.path(), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o500)).unwrap();
        let r = retire_if_done(d.path(), &s, Some(&scan(AFTER, AFTER, vec![])), FRESH, &HashSet::new(), &at(AFTER));
        std::fs::set_permissions(d.path(), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
        assert!(r.is_err());
        assert!(load(d.path()).unwrap().previous.is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn status_messages() {
        let d = dir();
        let s = rotated(d.path(), Some(DONE));
        let (st, m) = status_message(&s, Some(&KeepReason::Pending(2)), 0, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Ok);
        assert!(m.contains("2 Claude sessions"), "{m}");
        let (st, m) = status_message(&s, Some(&KeepReason::NoFreshScan), 0, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Ok);
        assert!(m.contains("waiting for a complete registry scan"), "{m}");
        let (st, m) = status_message(&s, None, 3, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Degraded);
        assert!(m.contains("retired hook token"), "{m}");
        let (st, m) = status_message(&s, None, 0, Some("disk full")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Degraded);
        assert!(m.contains("could not retire"), "{m}");
        let mut failed = s.clone();
        failed.headers.state = HeaderState::Failed;
        let (st, m) = status_message(&failed, None, 0, None).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(st, SourceState::Degraded);
        assert!(m.contains("acd hooks install claude"), "{m}");
        let mut done = s.clone();
        done.previous = None;
        assert_eq!(status_message(&done, None, 0, None), None);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `hook_token.rs`:

```rust
use std::collections::HashSet;
use std::time::Duration;

use acd_protocol::SourceState;

/// T4: Claude writes its registry entry shortly after it starts; a scan
/// must start this long after the header update to see every session
/// that read the old headers.
pub const REGISTRY_GRACE_MS: i64 = 5_000;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LiveSession {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub started_at_ms: Option<i64>,
}

/// A complete registry scan (T4).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ScanView {
    pub started_at_ms: i64,
    pub finished_at_ms: i64,
    pub live: Vec<LiveSession>,
}

/// T4: sessions that may still carry the previous token.
pub fn pending(store: &Store, live: &[LiveSession], on_current: &HashSet<String>) -> usize {
    live.iter()
        .filter(|s| !on_current.contains(&s.session_id))
        .filter(|s| s.started_at_ms.is_none_or(|t| t < store.headers.updated_at_ms))
        .count()
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum KeepReason {
    HeadersNotDone,
    NoFreshScan,
    Pending(usize),
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Retire {
    Nothing,
    /// The store differs from the one the caller evaluated (T5).
    Moved,
    Kept(KeepReason),
    Retired,
}

/// T4, T5 and T6. The 30-day cap always wins; otherwise headers must be
/// done for the current generation and the scan must have started after
/// the header update (plus the grace) and be fresh by a clock read after
/// the lock is held. A write failure is an error and keeps the token.
pub fn retire_if_done(
    dir: &Path,
    expected: &Store,
    scan: Option<&ScanView>,
    fresh_within: Duration,
    on_current: &HashSet<String>,
    clock: &dyn Fn() -> i64,
) -> Result<Retire> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    let now_ms = clock(); // after the lock: waiting may have taken long
    let mut s = load(dir)?;
    if s != *expected {
        return Ok(Retire::Moved);
    }
    let Some(prev) = s.previous.clone() else { return Ok(Retire::Nothing) };
    if now_ms < prev.expires_at_ms {
        if s.headers.state != HeaderState::Done || s.headers.generation != s.generation {
            return Ok(Retire::Kept(KeepReason::HeadersNotDone));
        }
        let fresh = |sc: &&ScanView| {
            sc.started_at_ms >= s.headers.updated_at_ms + REGISTRY_GRACE_MS
                && now_ms - sc.finished_at_ms <= fresh_within.as_millis() as i64
        };
        let Some(sc) = scan.filter(fresh) else {
            return Ok(Retire::Kept(KeepReason::NoFreshScan));
        };
        let n = pending(&s, &sc.live, on_current);
        if n > 0 {
            return Ok(Retire::Kept(KeepReason::Pending(n)));
        }
    }
    s.previous = None;
    save_locked(dir, &held, &s)?;
    Ok(Retire::Retired)
}

pub fn status_message(store: &Store, kept: Option<&KeepReason>, rejected: usize, retire_error: Option<&str>) -> Option<(SourceState, String)> {
    if let Some(e) = retire_error {
        return Some((SourceState::Degraded, format!("could not retire the previous hook token: {e}")));
    }
    match store.headers.state {
        HeaderState::Failed => {
            return Some((SourceState::Degraded, format!(
                "hook token headers could not be updated in {}: run `acd hooks install claude`",
                store.headers.failed.join(", ")
            )));
        }
        HeaderState::Pending => {
            return Some((SourceState::Degraded, "hook token rotation incomplete: run `acd hooks install claude`".into()));
        }
        HeaderState::Done => {}
    }
    if rejected > 0 {
        return Some((SourceState::Degraded, format!(
            "{rejected} Claude session(s) use a retired hook token; restart them or run /clear in them"
        )));
    }
    match kept {
        Some(KeepReason::Pending(n)) => {
            return Some((SourceState::Ok, format!("hook token rotation pending: {n} Claude sessions still use the previous token")));
        }
        Some(KeepReason::NoFreshScan) => {
            return Some((SourceState::Ok, "hook token rotation waiting for a complete registry scan".into()));
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    None
}
```

`pending`, `retire_if_done`, `status_message`, `LiveSession`,
`ScanView`, `KeepReason` and `Retire` are first called by the daemon in
Task 31: give each a line-level `#[allow(dead_code)] // used from Task 31`
that Task 31 removes.

### Task 27: `acd token rotate hook` and header repair

Contract T3, T6 and J3 (Sol I1, I2, I5, I6, N5, N7, N8). Rotation is a
T6 entry point: it takes the writer lock once and calls only `*_locked`
helpers. It refuses, before committing anything, while a journal
conflict exists, when the last rotation is incomplete, when a previous
token is still retained (unless `--force`), or when a recorded
destination fails the precheck, which reads each file with the same
safe reader the writes use. It then commits the store, rewrites the
managed Claude headers idempotently (rechecking Git per file), and
records `done` with the clock read after the last write, or `failed`.
`acd hooks install claude` repairs headers that a crash or a failure
left behind.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hook_token.rs` (`update_headers`, `rotate`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/hooks_install/mod.rs` (`precheck_claude`;
  Claude install repairs headers)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`token rotate hook` flags)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/status.rs` (print source messages)
- Test: `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 21, 24, 26.
- Produces:
  - `hooks_install::precheck_claude(ctx: &Ctx, held: &WriterLock, flags: Flags) -> Result<()>`.
  - `hook_token::update_headers(dir: &Path, ctx: &hooks_install::Ctx, flags: hooks_install::Flags) -> Result<hooks_install::RewriteReport>`
    (T6 entry point).
  - `hook_token::rotate(dir, ctx, flags, force: bool, now_ms) -> Result<(Store, hooks_install::RewriteReport)>`
    (T6 entry point; `now_ms` is only the rotation time).
  - Task 21's `hook_token::rotate_store` is deleted (its CLI caller now
    uses `rotate`).
  - CLI: `acd token rotate hook [--allow-tracked] [--allow-unknown-git] [--force]`.

```rust
#[test]
fn header_completion_is_recorded_after_the_last_write() {
    // Sol N5: a session that starts while the headers are being written
    // reads the old ones, so it must count as older than the update.
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &["--allow-unknown-git"]).status.success());
    let gate = home.path().join("gate");
    let mut child = acd_home(&dir, home.path())
        .env("ACD_TEST_PAUSE_AT", format!("before-headers-state={}", gate.display()))
        .args(["token", "rotate", "hook", "--allow-unknown-git"])
        .stdout(std::process::Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .unwrap();
    let reached = home.path().join("gate.reached");
    let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(10);
    while !reached.exists() {
        assert!(std::time::Instant::now() < deadline, "the rotation never reached the gate");
        std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(10));
    }
    let session_started = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis(); // a session launched in the gap
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(20));
    std::fs::write(&gate, b"").unwrap();
    assert!(child.wait().unwrap().success());
    let updated = store_of(&dir)["headers"]["updated_at_ms"].as_i64().unwrap();
    assert!(updated > session_started, "updated_at_ms {updated} must follow the gap ({session_started})");
}

fn rotation_refuses_a_tracked_file_before_committing() {

fn rotation_refuses_a_suspicious_entry_before_committing() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let before = store_of(&dir);
    let p = home.path().join(".claude/settings.json");
```
