```rust
    let out = rotate(&dir, home.path(), &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success(), "a marked entry at another destination never gets the token (Sol I5)");
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir), before);
    assert!(json_of(&p)["hooks"]["Stop"].as_array().unwrap().last().unwrap()["hooks"][0]["headers"].as_object().unwrap().is_empty());

fn a_second_rotation_needs_force_while_the_previous_token_is_retained() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    assert!(rotate(&dir, home.path(), &[]).status.success());
    let gen2 = hook_token_of(&dir);
    let out = rotate(&dir, home.path(), &[]);
    assert!(!out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("--force"));
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir)["generation"], 2);
    let out = rotate(&dir, home.path(), &["--force"]);
    assert!(out.status.success());
    assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("no longer accepted"), "says who is cut off");
    assert_eq!(store_of(&dir)["previous"]["token"], gen2.as_str());
}
```

```rust
/// Before a rotation commits: every recorded Claude destination passes
/// the Git check and carries no suspicious marked entry.
pub fn precheck_claude(ctx: &Ctx, _held: &WriterLock, flags: Flags) -> Result<()> {
    for dest in Record::load(&ctx.config_dir)?.claude_destinations() {
        // Sol N8: the same safe reader as the writes (no symlink, owned
        // by the user, a regular file), then Git, then the markers.
        let Some(text) = read_settings(&dest)? else { continue };
        git::check(&dest, flags.allow_tracked, flags.allow_unknown_git)?;
        let v: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).with_context(|| format!("{} is not valid JSON", dest.display()))?;
        let Some(hooks) = v.get("hooks").and_then(Value::as_object) else { continue };
        for (event, groups) in hooks {
            for g in groups.as_array().into_iter().flatten() {
                for e in g.get("hooks").and_then(Value::as_array).into_iter().flatten() {
                    if classify(event, e) == Ownership::Suspicious {
                        bail!("{}: an unexpected entry for {event} carries the acd marker; remove it by hand", dest.display());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
```

and `install` gains the header repair after `install_entries` released
the lock (replacing the Task 24 comment):

`hook_token.rs`:

```rust
use crate::hooks_install::{self, Flags, RewriteReport, WriterLock};

fn update_headers_locked(dir: &Path, ctx: &hooks_install::Ctx, held: &WriterLock, flags: Flags) -> Result<RewriteReport> {
    let mut s = load(dir)?;
    let r = hooks_install::rewrite_claude_token(ctx, held, &s.current, flags)?;
    pause_at("before-headers-state");
    fail_at("before-headers-state")?;
    // T1, Sol N5: the completion time is read after the last header write.
    let completed_ms = now_ms();
    let already = s.headers.state == HeaderState::Done && s.headers.generation == s.generation;
    s.headers = if !r.failed.is_empty() {
        Headers {
            generation: s.generation,
            state: HeaderState::Failed,
            updated_at_ms: s.headers.updated_at_ms,
            failed: r.failed.iter().map(|(p, _)| p.display().to_string()).collect(),
        }
    } else if already {
        // A repeated install does not move the retention boundary.
        s.headers.clone()
    } else {
        Headers { generation: s.generation, state: HeaderState::Done, updated_at_ms: completed_ms, failed: vec![] }
    };
    save_locked(dir, held, &s)?;
    Ok(r)
}

/// T3 steps 2 and 3, idempotent. A T6 entry point.
pub fn update_headers(dir: &Path, ctx: &hooks_install::Ctx, flags: Flags) -> Result<RewriteReport> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    hooks_install::journal::recover(dir)?; // J3: refuse while a conflict exists
    migrate_locked(dir, &held)?;
    update_headers_locked(dir, ctx, &held, flags)
}

/// A T6 entry point: one lock, only `*_locked` helpers inside.
pub fn rotate(dir: &Path, ctx: &hooks_install::Ctx, flags: Flags, force: bool, now_ms: i64) -> Result<(Store, RewriteReport)> {
    let held = writer_lock::acquire(dir)?;
    hooks_install::journal::recover(dir)?; // J3
    let s = migrate_locked(dir, &held)?;
    if s.headers.state != HeaderState::Done || s.headers.generation != s.generation {
        bail!("the last hook token rotation is incomplete: run `acd hooks install claude` first");
    }
    if let Some(p) = &s.previous
        && now_ms < p.expires_at_ms
    {
        if !force {
            bail!(
                "the previous hook token is still accepted for Claude sessions started before the last rotation \
                 (see `acd status`); rotating again cuts them off. Restart those sessions and wait, or pass --force"
            );
        }
        eprintln!("warning: the token before the last rotation is no longer accepted; restart Claude sessions that still use it");
    }
    hooks_install::precheck_claude(ctx, &held, flags)?;
    rotate_store_locked(dir, &held, now_ms)?; // the commit point (T3)
    fail_at("after-store")?;
    let r = update_headers_locked(dir, ctx, &held, flags)?;
    Ok((load(dir)?, r))
}
```

Delete Task 21's `rotate_store` (the unlocked wrapper): its only
caller, the `token rotate hook` arm, now calls `rotate`. Every unit test
that used it calls `rotate_store_locked` with a held lock.

`main.rs`: `TokenCmd::Rotate` gains `#[arg(long)] allow_tracked: bool`,
`#[arg(long)] allow_unknown_git: bool` and `#[arg(long)] force: bool`
(used only for `hook`), and the hook arm becomes:

```rust
                }

                    store.generation
```

### Task 28: `acd service install`

Spec §9: a launchd agent on macOS, a systemd user unit on Linux, both
restarting acd on any non-zero exit (Task 29 defines the exit codes).

Sol minor 3: the generated file carries an ownership marker. A foreign
file at the same path is refused unless `--force`; a reinstall backs up
the previous file. Linux reinstall uses `daemon-reload`, `enable`, then
`restart`, so a running service always picks up the new unit. A failing
`launchctl` or `systemctl` call fails the command and names the call.

The service does not carry `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` (Sol question 3): the
daemon reads Claude config roots from the install records (D24), so the
registry scan and rotation see the same roots however acd is started.

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/acd/src/service.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/main.rs` (`Service` subcommand)
- Test: unit tests in `service.rs`, `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `service::LABEL = "dev.agent-caller.acd"`.
  - `service::Runner` trait (`fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> anyhow::Result<()>`),
    `service::SystemRunner`, `service::runner() -> Box<dyn Runner>`
    (debug builds honour `ACD_TEST_SERVICE_RUNNER=<file>`: every call is
    appended to that file as one line and nothing runs).
    Debug builds also honour `ACD_TEST_SERVICE_FAIL=<word>`: a recorded
    call containing that word fails.
  - `service::MARKER = "acd-managed:v1"`.
  - `service::launchd_plist(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, log: &Path) -> String`,
    `service::systemd_unit(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path) -> String`
    (both contain `MARKER` in a comment).
  - `service::install(home: &Path, exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, print: bool, force: bool, runner: &dyn Runner) -> anyhow::Result<String>`.
  - CLI: `acd service install [--print] [--force]`.

`service.rs` tests:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn the_plist_restarts_on_failure_only_and_sets_a_usable_path() {
        let p = launchd_plist(Path::new("/opt/acd & co/acd"), Path::new("/Users/u/.config/acd"), Path::new("/Users/u/Library/Logs/acd.log"));
        assert!(p.contains("<string>/opt/acd &amp; co/acd</string>"), "XML-escaped");
        assert!(p.contains("<key>SuccessfulExit</key>\n\t\t<false/>"), "restart on any non-zero exit (1, 70, 75)");
        assert!(p.contains("/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin"));
        assert!(p.contains("<key>ACD_CONFIG_DIR</key>"));
        assert!(p.contains("<string>run</string>"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_unit_restarts_on_failure() {
        let u = systemd_unit(Path::new("/usr/local/bin/acd"), Path::new("/home/u/.config/acd"));
        assert!(u.contains("ExecStart=\"/usr/local/bin/acd\" run"));
        assert!(u.contains("Restart=on-failure"));
        assert!(u.contains("Environment=\"ACD_CONFIG_DIR=/home/u/.config/acd\""));
        assert!(u.contains("WantedBy=default.target"));
        assert!(u.contains("# acd-managed:v1"));
        assert!(!u.contains("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR"), "roots come from the install records (D24)");
    }

    #[derive(Default)]
    struct Fake {
        calls: std::cell::RefCell<Vec<String>>,
        fail: Option<&'static str>,
    }

    impl Runner for Fake {
        fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
            let line = format!("{program} {}", args.join(" "));
            self.calls.borrow_mut().push(line.clone());
            match self.fail {
                Some(w) if line.contains(w) => bail!("{line} failed (exit status: 5)"),
                _ => Ok(()),
            }
        }
    }

    fn def_path(home: &Path) -> PathBuf {
        if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
            home.join("Library/LaunchAgents").join(format!("{LABEL}.plist"))
        } else {
            home.join(".config/systemd/user/acd.service")
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_foreign_definition_is_refused_without_force() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let p = def_path(home.path());
        std::fs::create_dir_all(p.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&p, "someone else's service\n").unwrap();
        let f = Fake::default();
        let e = install(home.path(), Path::new("/x/acd"), Path::new("/c"), false, false, &f).unwrap_err();
        assert!(e.to_string().contains("--force"), "{e}");
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&p).unwrap(), "someone else's service\n");
        assert!(f.calls.borrow().is_empty());
        install(home.path(), Path::new("/x/acd"), Path::new("/c"), false, true, &f).unwrap();
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(&p).unwrap().contains(MARKER));
        let bak = std::fs::read_dir(p.parent().unwrap()).unwrap()
            .filter_map(|e| e.ok()).find(|e| e.file_name().to_string_lossy().contains(".bak-"));
        assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(bak.expect("backup").path()).unwrap(), "someone else's service\n");
    }

    #[test]
    fn reinstall_backs_up_and_reloads_a_running_service() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let f = Fake::default();
        install(home.path(), Path::new("/old/acd"), Path::new("/c"), false, false, &f).unwrap();
        f.calls.borrow_mut().clear();
        install(home.path(), Path::new("/new/acd"), Path::new("/c"), false, false, &f).unwrap();
        assert!(std::fs::read_to_string(def_path(home.path())).unwrap().contains("/new/acd"));
        let calls = f.calls.borrow().join("\n");
        if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
            assert!(calls.find("bootout").unwrap() < calls.find("bootstrap").unwrap(), "{calls}");
        } else {
            for w in ["daemon-reload", "enable acd.service", "restart acd.service"] {
                assert!(calls.contains(w), "{w}: {calls}");
            }
            assert!(calls.find("daemon-reload").unwrap() < calls.find("restart").unwrap());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failing_runner_call_fails_install_and_names_the_call() {
        let home = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        let word = if cfg!(target_os = "macos") { "bootstrap" } else { "restart" };
        let f = Fake { fail: Some(word), ..Default::default() };
        let e = install(home.path(), Path::new("/x/acd"), Path::new("/c"), false, false, &f).unwrap_err();
        assert!(format!("{e:#}").contains(word), "{e:#}");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `service.rs`**

```rust
//! `acd service install` (spec §9): a launchd agent on macOS, a systemd
//! user unit on Linux. Restarts on any non-zero exit (1 dead server, 70
//! panic, 75 Tailscale address change) and not after a clean stop (D15).
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

use anyhow::{Context, Result, bail};

pub const LABEL: &str = "dev.agent-caller.acd";
const PATH_MAC: &str = "/opt/homebrew/bin:/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin";
const PATH_LINUX: &str = "/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin";

pub trait Runner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()>;
}

pub struct SystemRunner;

impl Runner for SystemRunner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        let st = std::process::Command::new(program).args(args).status().with_context(|| format!("running {program}"))?;
        if !st.success() {
            bail!("{program} {} failed ({st})", args.join(" "));
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Records calls instead of running them (debug builds, tests only).
#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
struct FileRunner(PathBuf, Option<String>);

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
impl Runner for FileRunner {
    fn run(&self, program: &str, args: &[String]) -> Result<()> {
        use std::io::Write;
        let line = format!("{program} {}", args.join(" "));
        let mut f = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).append(true).open(&self.0)?;
        writeln!(f, "{line}")?;
        match &self.1 {
            Some(w) if line.contains(w.as_str()) => bail!("{line} failed (injected)"),
            _ => Ok(()),
        }
    }
}

pub fn runner() -> Box<dyn Runner> {
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Some(p) = std::env::var_os("ACD_TEST_SERVICE_RUNNER") {
        return Box::new(FileRunner(PathBuf::from(p), std::env::var("ACD_TEST_SERVICE_FAIL").ok()));
    }
    Box::new(SystemRunner)
}

pub const MARKER: &str = "acd-managed:v1";

/// Write `text` at `path`. An existing file without `MARKER` is refused
/// unless `force`; any existing file that differs is kept as
/// `<name>.bak-<unix seconds>`.
fn write_definition(path: &Path, text: &str, force: bool) -> Result<()> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().expect("has parent"))?;
    match std::fs::read_to_string(path) {
        Ok(old) if old == text => return Ok(()),
        Ok(old) => {
            if !old.contains(MARKER) && !force {
                bail!("{} exists and was not written by acd; rerun with --force to replace it (a backup is kept)", path.display());
            }
            let name = path.file_name().expect("has name").to_string_lossy();
            let bak = path.with_file_name(format!("{name}.bak-{}", chrono::Utc::now().timestamp()));
            std::fs::copy(path, &bak).with_context(|| format!("backing up {}", path.display()))?;
        }
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {}
        Err(e) => return Err(e).with_context(|| format!("reading {}", path.display())),
    }
    crate::secrets::write_atomic(path, text.as_bytes(), 0o644)
}

fn xml(s: &str) -> String {
    s.replace('&', "&amp;").replace('<', "&lt;").replace('>', "&gt;")
}

pub fn launchd_plist(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, log: &Path) -> String {
    let (exe, dir, log) = (xml(&exe.to_string_lossy()), xml(&config_dir.to_string_lossy()), xml(&log.to_string_lossy()));
    format!(
r#"<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>
<!DOCTYPE plist PUBLIC "-//Apple//DTD PLIST 1.0//EN" "http://www.apple.com/DTDs/PropertyList-1.0.dtd">
<plist version="1.0">
<!-- {MARKER} -->
<dict>
	<key>Label</key>
	<string>{LABEL}</string>
	<key>ProgramArguments</key>
	<array>
		<string>{exe}</string>
		<string>run</string>
	</array>
	<key>EnvironmentVariables</key>
	<dict>
		<key>ACD_CONFIG_DIR</key>
		<string>{dir}</string>
		<key>PATH</key>
		<string>{PATH_MAC}</string>
	</dict>
	<key>RunAtLoad</key>
	<true/>
	<key>KeepAlive</key>
	<dict>
		<key>SuccessfulExit</key>
		<false/>
	</dict>
	<key>ThrottleInterval</key>
	<integer>10</integer>
	<key>StandardErrorPath</key>
	<string>{log}</string>
	<key>StandardOutPath</key>
	<string>{log}</string>
</dict>
</plist>
"#
    )
}

fn unit_quote(s: &str) -> String {
    format!("\"{}\"", s.replace('\\', "\\\\").replace('"', "\\\""))
}

pub fn systemd_unit(exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path) -> String {
    format!(
        "# {MARKER}\n[Unit]\nDescription=agent-caller daemon (acd)\nAfter=network-online.target\n\n[Service]\nExecStart={} run\nEnvironment={}\nEnvironment=\"PATH={PATH_LINUX}\"\nRestart=on-failure\nRestartSec=2\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=default.target\n",
        unit_quote(&exe.to_string_lossy()),
        unit_quote(&format!("ACD_CONFIG_DIR={}", config_dir.display())),
    )
}

pub fn install(home: &Path, exe: &Path, config_dir: &Path, print: bool, force: bool, runner: &dyn Runner) -> Result<String> {
    if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        let plist = home.join("Library/LaunchAgents").join(format!("{LABEL}.plist"));
        let text = launchd_plist(exe, config_dir, &home.join("Library/Logs/acd.log"));
        if print {
            return Ok(text);
        }
        std::fs::create_dir_all(home.join("Library/Logs"))?;
        write_definition(&plist, &text, force)?;
        let uid = acd_securefs::euid();
        // Not loaded yet is fine.
        let _ = runner.run("launchctl", &["bootout".into(), format!("gui/{uid}/{LABEL}")]);
        runner.run("launchctl", &["bootstrap".into(), format!("gui/{uid}"), plist.display().to_string()])?;
        Ok(format!(
            "Installed and started {}. Stop it with: launchctl bootout gui/{uid}/{LABEL}",
            plist.display()
        ))
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        let unit = home.join(".config/systemd/user/acd.service");
        let text = systemd_unit(exe, config_dir);
        if print {
            return Ok(text);
        }
        write_definition(&unit, &text, force)?;
        // `restart` starts a stopped unit and replaces a running one, so a
        // reinstall always runs the new definition.
        for args in [&["daemon-reload"][..], &["enable", "acd.service"], &["restart", "acd.service"]] {
            let mut a = vec!["--user".to_string()];
            a.extend(args.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()));
            runner.run("systemctl", &a)?;
        }
        Ok(format!("Installed and started {}. Stop it with: systemctl --user disable --now acd.service", unit.display()))
    } else {
        bail!("acd service install supports macOS and Linux")
    }
}
```

```rust
mod service;

    /// Run acd under launchd (macOS) or a systemd user unit (Linux).
    Service {
        #[command(subcommand)]
        cmd: ServiceCmd,
    },

#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum ServiceCmd {
    Install {
        /// Print the service definition instead of installing it.
        #[arg(long)]
        print: bool,
        /// Replace a service file that acd did not write (a backup is kept).
        #[arg(long)]
        force: bool,
    },
}

        Cmd::Service { cmd: ServiceCmd::Install { print, force } } => {
            if !dir.is_dir() {
                bail!("{} not initialized; run `acd init`", dir.display());
            }
            preflight(&dir)?;
            let home = std::path::PathBuf::from(std::env::var_os("HOME").context("HOME is not set")?);
            let exe = std::fs::canonicalize(std::env::current_exe()?)?;
            let dir = std::fs::canonicalize(&dir)?;
            println!("{}", service::install(&home, &exe, &dir, print, force, service::runner().as_ref())?);
            Ok(())
        }
```

```rust
#[cfg(debug_assertions)] // ACD_TEST_STOP_SERVER is debug-only
#[test]
fn a_dead_server_task_exits_1_so_the_supervisor_restarts_acd() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[("ACD_TEST_STOP_SERVER", "local")]);
    let mut daemon = started.daemon;
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    let status = loop {
        if let Some(s) = daemon.0.try_wait().unwrap() { break s; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd kept running without its local socket server");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    };
    assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(1));
}

#[test]
fn sigterm_exits_cleanly_and_removes_the_socket() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[]);
    let pid = started.daemon.0.id().to_string();
    assert!(std::process::Command::new("kill").args(["-TERM", &pid]).status().unwrap().success());
    let mut daemon = started.daemon;
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    let status = loop {
        if let Some(s) = daemon.0.try_wait().unwrap() { break s; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd did not exit on SIGTERM");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
    };
    assert_eq!(status.code(), Some(0), "launchd and systemd must not restart a clean stop");
    assert!(!dir.join("acd.sock").exists());
}

        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd did not exit after a panic");
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`daemon.rs`:

```rust
/// A server task stopped: the supervisor restarts acd.
pub const EXIT_SERVER_STOPPED: i32 = 1;
/// Any panic, including a poisoned mutex (D14).
pub const EXIT_PANIC: i32 = 70;

fn exit_on_panic() {
    let default = std::panic::take_hook();
    std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
        default(info);
        eprintln!("acd: panic; exiting so the supervisor restarts acd");
        std::process::exit(EXIT_PANIC);
    }));
}
```

At the top of `run`: `exit_on_panic();`, then in debug builds:

```rust
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if let Some(ms) = std::env::var("ACD_TEST_PANIC_AFTER_MS").ok().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()) {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(ms)).await;
            panic!("test panic");
        });
    }
```

In debug builds, `ACD_TEST_STOP_SERVER=local` makes the local socket
task return its name at once instead of serving (it still removes the
socket file on exit).

Collect every server into one `tokio::task::JoinSet<String>` instead of
detached `tokio::spawn` calls. Each TCP listener task returns
`format!("listener {addr}")`, the Unix socket task `"local socket"`:

```rust
    let mut servers = tokio::task::JoinSet::new();
    // for each TCP listener:
    servers.spawn(async move {
        if let Err(e) = axum::serve(tls_listener, app.into_make_service_with_connect_info::<core_tls::Admit>()).await {
            tracing::error!(%addr, error = %e, "server stopped");
        }
        format!("listener {addr}")
    });
```

Replace the final `select!` with:

```rust
    let mut term = tokio::signal::unix::signal(tokio::signal::unix::SignalKind::terminate())?;
    let exit_code = tokio::select! {
        _ = tokio::signal::ctrl_c() => None,
        _ = term.recv() => None,
        _ = watch => Some(EXIT_ADDRESSES_CHANGED),
        Some(done) = servers.join_next() => {
            tracing::error!(server = %done.unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string()), "a server task stopped; exiting");
            Some(EXIT_SERVER_STOPPED)
        }
    };
```

The existing cleanup (abort the TLS watch, remove `acd.sock`) runs for
every branch.

### Task 30: Daemon wiring: config, hook listener, adapters

D13: the hook listener binds before `acd.sock` is bound, so a taken
hook port fails startup and leaves no socket file. The listener is capped (`endpoint::capped`,
Sol I8). Claude registry roots come from the install records plus the
default root (D24, Sol question 3), read again before every scan.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/config.rs` (`ignored_entrypoints`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/daemon.rs`
- Modify: `crates/acd/tests/common/mod.rs` (`post_hook`)
- Modify: `crates/acd/tests/cli.rs` (token logging test)
- Test: `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 29.
- Produces:
  - `Config::ignored_entrypoints: Vec<String>` (default `["sdk-ts"]`).
  - `common::post_hook(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String)`
    (blocking `std::net`, for binary tests).
  - `daemon::claude_roots(config_dir: &Path, home: &Path, claude_config_dir: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<PathBuf>`
    (recorded roots plus the default, deduplicated, in that order).
  - `daemon::hook_credentials(s: &hook_token::Store) -> (Credential, Option<Credential>)`.

Add to `crates/acd/tests/common/mod.rs`:

```rust
pub fn post_hook(port: u16, path: &str, token: &str, headers: &[(&str, &str)], body: &str) -> (u16, String) {
    use std::io::{Read, Write};
    let mut s = std::net::TcpStream::connect(("127.0.0.1", port)).unwrap();
    s.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
    let mut req = format!(
        "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\nX-Acd-Token: {token}\r\n",
        body.len()
    );
    for (k, v) in headers {
        req.push_str(&format!("{k}: {v}\r\n"));
    }
    req.push_str("\r\n");
    req.push_str(body);
    s.write_all(req.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    let mut out = String::new();
    s.read_to_string(&mut out).unwrap();
    let status = out.split_whitespace().nth(1).and_then(|c| c.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    (status, out.split_once("\r\n\r\n").map(|(_, b)| b.to_owned()).unwrap_or_default())
}

/// The current hook token from the store (Task 21 layout).
pub fn store_token(dir: &Path) -> String {
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("hook-tokens.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();
    v["current"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()
}
```

```rust
    fn claude_roots_are_the_recorded_roots_then_the_default() {

        std::fs::create_dir_all(&custom).unwrap();
        let mut rec = crate::hooks_install::Record::default();
        rec.files.insert(std::fs::canonicalize(&custom).unwrap().join("settings.json"), crate::hooks_install::FileRecord {
            target: crate::hooks_install::Target::Claude, created_file: true, created_hooks_key: true, created_events: vec![],
        });
        rec.save(d.path()).unwrap();
        let roots = claude_roots(d.path(), &home, None);
        assert_eq!(roots, vec![std::fs::canonicalize(&custom).unwrap().join("sessions"), home.join(".claude/sessions")]);
        let roots = claude_roots(d.path(), &home, Some(&std::fs::canonicalize(&custom).unwrap()));
        assert_eq!(roots.len(), 1, "deduplicated");
    }

    #[test]
    fn hook_credentials_follow_the_store_generations() {
        let d = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
        crate::hook_token::ensure(d.path(), false).unwrap();
        let held = crate::writer_lock::acquire(d.path()).unwrap();
        let s = crate::hook_token::rotate_store_locked(d.path(), &held, 1_000).unwrap();
        let (cur, prev) = hook_credentials(&s);
        assert_eq!((cur.generation, prev.as_ref().map(|p| p.generation)), (2, Some(1)));
        assert_eq!(cur.token, s.current);
    }
```

```rust
#[test]
fn the_daemon_serves_hooks_on_loopback_and_shows_the_session() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[]);
    let token = store_token(&dir);
    let body = claude_fixture("payload-UserPromptSubmit.json");
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", "wrong", &[], &body).0, 401);
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/UserPromptSubmit", &token, &[("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")], &body),
               (200, String::new()));
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    loop {
        let out = status_text(&dir);
        if out.contains("Working Claude") { break; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "{out}");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
    assert!(status_text(&dir).contains("hooks=Ok"));
}

#[test]
fn a_taken_hook_port_is_fatal_before_the_local_socket_is_served() {
    let (_home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let blocker = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").unwrap();
    let hook_port = blocker.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("config.toml"), format!("port = {}\nhook_port = {hook_port}\nlisten = [\"loopback\"]\n", free_port())).unwrap();
    let mut child = acd(&dir).arg("run").stderr(Stdio::piped()).spawn().unwrap();
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    let status = loop {
        if let Some(s) = child.try_wait().unwrap() { break s; }
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd kept running without its hook endpoint");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50));
    };
    let mut err = String::new();
    child.stderr.take().unwrap().read_to_string(&mut err).unwrap();
    assert!(!status.success());
    assert!(err.contains("hook endpoint"), "{err}");
    assert!(!dir.join("acd.sock").exists());
}

#[test]
fn the_daemon_and_init_migrate_a_legacy_dir_concurrently_without_hanging() {
    // Sol N7: both take hooks.lock once; neither reacquires it.
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    let token = store_token(&dir);
    std::fs::remove_file(dir.join("hook-tokens.json")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(dir.join("hook.token"), format!("{token}\n")).unwrap();
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::set_permissions(dir.join("hook.token"), std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
    let inits: Vec<_> = (0..3)
        .map(|_| acd_home(&dir, home.path()).arg("init").stdout(Stdio::null()).stderr(Stdio::null()).spawn().unwrap())
        .collect();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[]); // readiness bounds the daemon side (10 s)
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    for mut c in inits {
        loop {
            if let Some(st) = c.try_wait().unwrap() {
                assert!(st.success());
                break;
            }
            assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "acd init hung");
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20));
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(store_token(&dir), token, "one migration; everyone agrees on the token");
    assert!(!dir.join("hook.token").exists());
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &token, &[], "{}").0, 200);
}
```

```rust
use acd_adapters::hooks::endpoint::Credential;

pub fn claude_roots(config_dir: &Path, home: &Path, claude_config_dir: Option<&Path>) -> Vec<PathBuf> {
    let mut roots = crate::hooks_install::recorded_claude_roots(config_dir);
    let default = acd_adapters::hooks::claude_sessions::sessions_dir(home, claude_config_dir);
    let default = std::fs::canonicalize(default.parent().unwrap_or(&default))
        .map(|p| p.join("sessions"))
        .unwrap_or(default);
    if !roots.contains(&default) {
        roots.push(default);
    }
    roots
}

pub fn hook_credentials(s: &crate::hook_token::Store) -> (Credential, Option<Credential>) {
    (
        Credential { token: s.current.clone(), generation: s.generation },
        s.previous.as_ref().map(|p| Credential { token: p.token.clone(), generation: p.generation }),
    )
}
```

### Task 31: Hook token refresh and retirement in the daemon

Contract T4 and T5 in the running daemon (Sol I1, I2, minor 2). Every
2 s the daemon reloads the store into `HookAuth`, so a rotation takes
effect without a restart. Every 30 s it runs `retire_if_done` with the
last complete registry scan and the sessions seen on the current
generation, updates `HookAuth` at once after a retirement, and sets the
hooks source status. Retirement errors are logged and shown in status.

**Files:**
- Modify: `crates/acd/src/daemon.rs`
- Test: `crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 11 (`last_complete_scan`, `sessions_on_generation`),
  24, 26.
- Produces: debug-only `ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST`: 200 ms scans (Task 30),
  200 ms refresh and a 1 s retirement period.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`crates/acd/tests/hooks_cli.rs`:

```rust
const LIVE_SID: &str = "0b5e1c2a-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";

/// A registry entry for a real child process, so `SystemProc` sees a live
/// session that started before the rotation.
fn live_registry_session(home: &std::path::Path) -> std::process::Child {
    let child = std::process::Command::new("sleep").arg("120").spawn().unwrap();
    let pid = child.id();
    let out = std::process::Command::new("ps").env("TZ", "UTC")
        .args(["-o", "lstart=", "-p", &pid.to_string()]).output().unwrap();
    let lstart = String::from_utf8(out.stdout).unwrap().trim().to_owned();
    let sessions = home.join(".claude/sessions");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&sessions).unwrap();
    let entry = serde_json::json!({
        "pid": pid, "sessionId": LIVE_SID, "cwd": "/tmp", "procStart": lstart,
        "startedAt": chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() - 60_000,
        "entrypoint": "cli", "status": "idle", "kind": "interactive",
    });
    let p = sessions.join(format!("{pid}.json"));
    std::fs::write(&p, entry.to_string()).unwrap();
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::set_permissions(&p, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o600)).unwrap();
    child
}

fn until_true(what: &str, secs: u64, mut f: impl FnMut() -> bool) {
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(secs);
    while !f() {
        assert!(Instant::now() < deadline, "timed out waiting for {what}");
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)] // ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST is debug-only
#[test]
fn a_live_session_keeps_the_previous_token_until_it_is_seen_on_the_new_one() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let mut sleeper = live_registry_session(home.path());
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[("ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST", "1"), ("HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap())]);
    let old = store_token(&dir);
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(600)); // a complete scan sees the session
    assert!(acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).status().unwrap().success());
    let new = store_token(&dir);
    let mut body: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&claude_fixture("payload-Stop.json")).unwrap();
    body["session_id"] = LIVE_SID.into();
    let body = body.to_string();
    // Another session (the fixture's own id) proves the daemon reloaded the store.
    let other = claude_fixture("payload-Stop.json");
    until_true("the new token", 5, || post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &new, &[], &other).0 == 200);

    // Several retirement periods pass: the session started before the
    // headers changed and has not used the new token.
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3));
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &old, &[], &body).0, 200, "pending session keeps working");
    assert!(store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_object());
    until_true("pending status", 5, || status_text(&dir).contains("rotation pending: 1 Claude sessions"));

    // The same session authenticates with the new token (for example after /clear).
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &new, &[], &body).0, 200);
    // T4: the deciding scan starts at least REGISTRY_GRACE_MS (5 s) after the header update.
    until_true("retirement", 10, || store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_null());
    until_true("auth updated", 5, || post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &old, &[], &body).0 == 401);
    let _ = sleeper.kill();
}

#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
#[test]
fn an_exited_session_releases_the_previous_token() {
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let mut sleeper = live_registry_session(home.path());
    let _started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[("ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST", "1"), ("HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap())]);
    std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(600));
    assert!(acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).status().unwrap().success());
    // Sol R3-4: wait past the registry grace until an eligible retirement
    // attempt kept the token because of this session (the pending status
    // needs done headers and a fresh scan that started after the grace).
    until_true("an eligible attempt kept it", 15, || status_text(&dir).contains("rotation pending: 1 Claude sessions"));
    assert!(store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_object(), "kept while the session lives");
    sleeper.kill().unwrap();
    sleeper.wait().unwrap();
    until_true("retirement after exit", 10, || store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_null());
}

#[test]
fn without_a_registry_scan_the_previous_token_stays() {
    // An unreadable sessions root makes every scan incomplete (T4).
    if acd_securefs::euid() == 0 {
        return;
    }
    let (home, dir) = home_with_acd();
    assert!(install(&dir, home.path(), "claude", &[]).status.success());
    let sessions = home.path().join(".claude/sessions");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&sessions).unwrap();
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    std::fs::set_permissions(&sessions, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o000)).unwrap();
    let started = start_daemon(&dir, "", &[("ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST", "1"), ("HOME", home.path().to_str().unwrap())]);
    let old = store_token(&dir);
    assert!(acd_home(&dir, home.path()).args(["token", "rotate", "hook"]).status().unwrap().success());
    // Sol R3-4: wait until retirement would be eligible but for the scan
    // (past the 5 s grace, plus two retirement periods), and see why it kept the token.
    let updated = store_of(&dir)["headers"]["updated_at_ms"].as_i64().unwrap();
    while chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() < updated + 5_000 + 2_000 {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
    }
    until_true("kept for want of a scan", 5, || status_text(&dir).contains("waiting for a complete registry scan"));
    assert!(store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_object());
    assert_eq!(post_hook(started.hook_port, "/hook/claude/Stop", &old, &[], &claude_fixture("payload-Stop.json")).0, 200);
    std::fs::set_permissions(&sessions, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o700)).unwrap();
    // With the root readable again and no live session, it retires: the
    // earlier retention was the scan rule, not a test that cannot retire.
    until_true("retired once scans complete", 15, || store_of(&dir)["previous"].is_null());
}
```

`start_daemon` applies `env` after `acd_home`, so the `HOME` pair points
the daemon at the test home (the install record names the same root, so
the scan covers it either way). The `0o000` test returns early when
running as root (`acd_securefs::euid() == 0`), since root reads the
directory anyway.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`hook_token.rs`: remove the `#[allow(dead_code)]` lines Task 26 added.

`daemon.rs`, after Task 30's hook listener:

```rust
    #[allow(unused_mut)]
    let (mut refresh_every, mut retire_every) = (Duration::from_secs(2), Duration::from_secs(30));
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if std::env::var_os("ACD_TEST_HOOKS_FAST").is_some() {
        (refresh_every, retire_every) = (Duration::from_millis(200), Duration::from_secs(1));
    }
    let hook_dir = dir.to_path_buf();
    let (auth, hooks_r, core_r) = (hook_auth.clone(), hooks.clone(), core.clone());
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut last_retire = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        let started = tokio::time::Instant::now();
        loop {
            tokio::time::sleep(refresh_every).await;
            let store = match crate::hook_token::load(&hook_dir) {
                Ok(s) => s,
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::error!(error = %e, "hook token store unreadable; keeping the current credentials");
                    continue;
                }
            };
            let (cur, prev) = hook_credentials(&store);
            auth.set(cur, prev);
            if last_retire.elapsed() < retire_every {
                continue;
            }
            last_retire = tokio::time::Instant::now();
            let scan = hooks_r.last_complete_scan().map(|c| crate::hook_token::ScanView {
                started_at_ms: c.started_at_ms,
                finished_at_ms: c.finished_at_ms,
                live: c.live.into_iter()
                    .map(|e| crate::hook_token::LiveSession { session_id: e.session_id, started_at_ms: e.started_at_ms })
                    .collect(),
            });
            // T5: `on_current` belongs to this exact store; retirement does
            // nothing if the store on disk differs in any field.
            let on_current = hooks_r.sessions_on_generation(store.generation);
            let (d, expected) = (hook_dir.clone(), store.clone());
            // T6: `retire_if_done` takes the blocking writer lock and reads
            // the clock after holding it.
            let result = tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
                crate::hook_token::retire_if_done(
                    &d,
                    &expected,
                    scan.as_ref(),
                    scan_interval * 2,
                    &on_current,
                    &|| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis(),
                )
            })
            .await
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| Err(anyhow::anyhow!("retirement task failed: {e}")));
            let (kept, retire_error) = match result {
                Ok(crate::hook_token::Retire::Kept(k)) => (Some(k), None),
                Ok(crate::hook_token::Retire::Retired) => {
                    tracing::info!("previous hook token retired");
                    if let Ok(s) = crate::hook_token::load(&hook_dir) {
                        let (cur, prev) = hook_credentials(&s);
                        auth.set(cur, prev);
                    }
                    (None, None)
                }
                Ok(crate::hook_token::Retire::Nothing | crate::hook_token::Retire::Moved) => (None, None),
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::error!(error = %e, "could not retire the previous hook token");
                    (None, Some(e.to_string()))
                }
            };
            let window = Duration::from_secs(600).min(started.elapsed());
            let rejected = auth.rejected_since(tokio::time::Instant::now() - window);
            let store = crate::hook_token::load(&hook_dir).unwrap_or(store);
            let status = match crate::hook_token::status_message(&store, kept.as_ref(), rejected, retire_error.as_deref()) {
                Some((st, m)) => SourceStatus { state: st, message: Some(m) },
                None => SourceStatus { state: SourceState::Ok, message: None },
            };
            core_r.set_source_status(Source::Hooks, status);
        }
    });
```

```rust
mod common;
use common::*;

use std::time::Duration;

use acd_adapters::herdr::fake::fake_agent;
use acd_adapters::hooks::testing::read_response;
use acd_protocol::*;
use tokio::time::Instant;

fn herdr_screen(name: &str) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(format!("{}/../../tests/fixtures/herdr/{name}", env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"))).unwrap()
}

async fn answer(r: &Rig, id: &str, rid: &str, option: &str) -> CommandResult {
    r.core.handle("c", ClientMessage::RequestAnswer {
        id: id.into(), request_id: rid.into(), revision: None, option_id: Some(option.into()), text: None,
    }).await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn criterion_1_a_herdr_bound_claude_permission_is_answerable_within_2_s_and_yes_unblocks() {
    let r = rig(&[fake_agent("w1:p1", "claude", "blocked")], |_| {}).await;
    r.fake.as_ref().unwrap().set_screen("w1:p1", "detection", &herdr_screen("claude-permission-bash.txt"));
    let agent = r.herdr.as_ref().unwrap().panes().read().unwrap().keys().next().unwrap().clone();
    let sock = r.sock();
    let h = [("X-Herdr-Pane", "w1:p1"), ("X-Herdr-Socket", sock.as_str()), ("X-Claude-Entrypoint", "cli")];
    let (_, mut rx) = r.core.attach(None);
    let t = Instant::now();
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &h).await;
    let rid = loop {
        if let ServerMessage::RequestOpened { request, .. } = rx.recv().await.unwrap()
            && request.kind == RequestKind::Permission
        {
            break request.id;
        }
    };
    assert!(t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(2), "{:?}", t.elapsed());
    assert!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.is_ok());
    assert_eq!(read_response(held).await.1, acd_adapters::hooks::wire::reply_allow());
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a2", &rid, "deny").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestBusy),
        "the agent applies only the first answer");
    assert_eq!(r.core.request(&rid).unwrap().agent_id, agent);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn criterion_4_answered_in_the_terminal_first_gives_request_gone() {
    let r = rig(&[], |_| {}).await;
    let held = r.hold(&fx("claude/payload-PermissionRequest-Bash.json"), &CLI).await;
    let rid = until("request", || r.agent(&format!("claude:{SID}")).and_then(|a| a.pending).map(|p| p.id)).await;
    drop(held); // terminal deny
    let mut stop = fx("claude/payload-Stop-after-deny.json");
    stop["session_id"] = SID.into();
    r.hook("claude", "Stop", &stop, &CLI).await;
    until("resolved", || r.core.request(&rid).is_none().then_some(())).await;
    assert_eq!(answer(&r, "a1", &rid, "allow").await.error_code(), Some(ErrorCode::RequestGone));
}
```

`lifecycle_hooks_reply_at_once_without_a_consumer`; the install round
trip by Task 24.)

`docs/research/2026-10-09-p3-live.md`:

1. A Claude permission in a herdr pane appears within 2 s; "Yes" from
   the inbox runs the command; the card resolves `inbox`.
2. Terminal "Yes" while held: the card resolves `terminal` within about
   1 s of the tool finishing; the hold is released.
3. Terminal Esc while held: the request switches to keys while the
   dialog is up, or resolves `terminal`.
4. A single-select `AskUserQuestion` answered from the inbox.
5. A Codex approval in an embedded-mode Codex pane (one Codex pane in
   the cwd) answerable within 3 s; with two Codex panes in one cwd, both
   stay read-only.
6. **Tip lines (LIVE CHECK 10):** keep a Claude permission dialog up
   for 2 minutes and watch whether its `Tip:` line changes. If it does,
   report it: `screen::dialog_identity` must drop tip lines before keys
   requests are enabled.
7. **Codex hook trust (LIVE CHECK 11):** after `acd hooks install
   codex`, does Codex ask for a hook review for the user-level
   `hooks.json`?
8. `acd token rotate hook` while a Claude session runs: the session's
   hooks keep working (previous token); `acd status` shows the pending
   count; after the session exits, or after `/clear` in it, the
   previous token is retired within one retirement period.
9. `acd service install` on macOS: the agent starts, `kill -TERM` stops
   it without a restart, `kill -9` restarts it; a second install backs
   up the plist and reloads.
10. `acd hooks install` twice and `uninstall` on a scratch copy of a
    real settings file: semantically identical afterwards.
11. Codex runs the installed command when the `acd` binary path contains
    a space (the installer single-quotes it for `sh`; Codex's execution
    model is unverified).
12. **Command rendering (LIVE CHECK 12):** in a narrow pane, trigger a
    Claude and a Codex permission for a command longer than the pane
    width, and one with an embedded newline. Record the screen rows. The
    expected P3 behavior is no screen binding and no keys target (D26):
    the Claude one is answerable through its hold, the Codex one is
    read-only. Record what a future
    wrapping rule would need (pane width, wrap markers).
13. **Parallel tool calls (LIVE CHECK 13):** ask Claude for two Bash
    commands in one turn that both need permission. Record whether the
    second `PermissionRequest` arrives before the first dialog ends and
    how acd shows both (O1: one occurrence each, only the one whose
    dialog is on screen gets keys).
14. **Registry entry delay (LIVE CHECK 14, release gate):** start Claude
    sessions in a scratch config dir and record the time between the
    process start (`ps -o lstart=`) and the registry entry appearing in
    `<config>/sessions/`: idle machine, sessions started while
    `acd token rotate hook` runs, and sessions started under load (a
    parallel build plus several sessions starting at once).
    `REGISTRY_GRACE_MS` (5 s, T4) must exceed the worst delay with margin;
    if it does not, raise it before release. An underestimated grace can
    retire the previous token while a live session still uses it.

Questions need the complete question text and the full option list (M3). Wrapping narrowed, see below. |
| B2. Asynchronous reads can create or enable requests after their binding became invalid | Accept | Read tickets and generations G1 to G6 (pane incarnation, binding generation, occurrence generation, read sequence); revalidation inside the delivery lock; Task 5 (incarnation), Task 15 (tickets, out-of-order and replaced-pane tests), Task 17 (Codex watch tickets). |
| B3. T3 takeover clears only hooks-only requests | Accept | Ownership transitions table for every owner pair; Task 18 revokes requests, holds, targets and reads under the lock and never removes an unrelated herdr agent. |
| B4. The implemented Codex cwd rule is weaker than ruling Q3 | Accept | Task 10 `codex_bind`: exactly one pane reporting the session (duplicates rejected), else the only Codex pane in the cwd across all panes; Task 17: a competing pane showing the same command makes the request read-only. |
| B5. The delivery lock can make a fresh screen check stale | Accept | Task 4: the engine takes the per-agent lock before the final screen read and rechecks incarnation and lease right before the write; test `a_dialog_change_while_waiting_for_the_lock_sends_nothing`. Task 6 binds targets to the pane incarnation. |
| I1. The current-token session set spans every generation | Accept | T2: auth returns the matched generation; Task 8 tags events; Task 11 keeps `by_generation`; T4 excludes only sessions seen on the new generation (Task 26 `pending`, Task 31 live test). |
| I2. Rotation is neither transactional nor reliably capped | Accept | One atomic store `hook-tokens.json` (T1, Task 21); every store read-modify-write under the writer lock, and retirement skips a store that moved (Task 26); rotation refuses over an incomplete rotation, over a retained previous token without `--force`, and when a precheck fails, then commits the store before header rewrites, with idempotent repair through `acd hooks install claude` (T3, Task 27); retirement needs a fresh complete scan and always honors the 30-day cap; failures propagate and show in status (T4, T5, Tasks 26, 31); failure injection at `store-write`, `after-store`, `before-headers-state`, `record-write` (Tasks 21, 24, 27) and a read-only store directory (Task 26). |
| I3. Same-command retries can be discarded while the old request awaits evidence | Accept | Occurrence contract O1 to O6, defined before the hold path; Task 14 `end_session_occurrences` and tests for identical retries, duplicates, delayed events. |
| I4. Settings edits lack concurrency control and recoverable ownership updates | Accept | Task 24: `hooks.lock` writer lock, re-read and compare before the rename with bounded retry, a journal for settings and record updates with recovery, records keyed by canonical destination (D24). |
| I5. Marker matching can remove foreign hooks or send them the token | Accept | Task 23: exact marker parsing that validates entry type, destination and route (`Owned`, `Suspicious`, `Foreign`); suspicious entries are kept and reported; rotation refuses unexpected destinations before committing (Task 27 test). |
| I6. Git protection fails open and is bypassed during token rewrite | Accept | Task 24 `git::status` returns tracked, untracked or unknown; unknown refuses without `--allow-unknown-git`; the check runs before every token-bearing write, and rotation runs it over every destination before committing and again per file after (Task 27 test). |
| I7. The Bash `allow_always` ruling was not applied | Accept | Task 7: one rule-construction function (`always_rule`) used for display and delivery; "always" is offered only when `rule_displayable` holds (no truncation, no control or bidi characters); Task 14 test; Task 32 spec edit. |
| I8. Endpoint limits do not bound retained work; rejected headers leak into logs | Accept | Task 8: bounded mailbox events and bytes, capped connections (`CappedListener`), a control channel reserved for close and ownership events, at most 256 rejection entries, logs carry only `remote_id`; Task 11 loop priority; Task 15 read semaphore; Task 30 serves through `capped` and checks the logs. |
| Minor 1. A failed hold-end read is treated as terminal | Accept | Task 15: unavailable is distinct from absent; bounded retry, then `uncertain`. |
| Minor 2. Readiness from socket existence; rotation test without a live session | Accept | Task 1: ready means an authenticated `acd status` with both listeners bound; Task 31: rotation tests with a live registry session backed by a real process. |
| Minor 3. Service reinstall and failure tests are incomplete | Accept | Tasks 28 and 29: ownership marker, `--force` for foreign files, backups, Linux `daemon-reload`/`enable`/`restart`, runner-failure, reinstall and dead-server tests; Task 1 removes every debug override from inherited environments. |
| Question: what identifies a new occurrence? | Answered | Occurrence contract O1 to O6. |
| Question: malformed question arrays | Answered | D25: full structural validation; anything malformed is read-only (Task 7 `question_specs`). |
| Question: custom Claude config roots in the service | Answered | D24: the daemon reads roots from the install records plus the default root before every scan (Task 30 `claude_roots`); the service needs no `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`. |
| Question: D10 late-evidence monitoring | Decided | The promise is removed. A superseded request resolves `uncertain` at once with a debug log (D10); no diagnostic-only tracking is kept, since nothing would consume it. |

### Narrowed with evidence: wrapped commands (B1, M4)

The ruling asked for whitespace to be preserved except terminal line
wrapping, with wrapping defined precisely from `screen.rs` and the
fixtures. That definition cannot be made safe with what herdr provides:

- herdr reports no pane width. `pane.list` carries only
  `scroll.viewport_rows` (`tests/fixtures/herdr/api/pane.list.json`),
  and `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/screen.rs` has no width input.
- The screen text has no soft-wrap marks, so a row break cannot be told
  apart from a newline in the command, a space dropped at a word wrap,
  or a break inside a word.

Any join rule would therefore let two different commands render the same
rows, which is the B1 failure. The plan defines terminal line wrapping
as any rendering that spans more than one row and never matches it
(D26): such requests get no screen binding and no keys target. A held
Claude request stays answerable through its HTTP hold; an unheld or
Codex one is read-only and the user answers in the terminal. LIVE CHECK 12 (Task 32 Step 6) records real wrapping so a
later, separately reviewed change can add a width-aware rule.

### Sol round 2 (gpt-6.1-sol, on 984b99c)

Verdict: needs another round. Full review: `/tmp/acp3/review/sol-r2.md`.
Sol counted B5, I1, I5 to I7 and minors 1 to 3 as resolved, and B1 to
B4, I2 to I4 and I8 as partially resolved; the remaining gaps are its new
findings N1 to N9. It accepted the D26 narrowing with one wording fix.
The orchestrator accepted every finding, with the design choices below;
revision 3 resolves them as follows. Task numbers are revision 3's (two
tasks were added: 19 and 25; later tasks moved).

| Finding | Ruling | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| D26 wording | Accept | M4, D26, the spec edits, live check 12 and the round 1 narrowing note now say "no screen binding and no keys target"; a held Claude request stays answerable through its HTTP hold. |
| N1. Codex ambiguity protection incomplete and bypassed on enlargement | Accept: one validation path; unknown rival is read-only; revalidate inventory and binding at each enabling step; implement Codex `Bound(a)` transitions | Contract C1 to C5. Task 17: `spawn_codex_bind` is the only Codex read (first watch and every later enabling read), `pane_seen` maps failed or truncated reads to `Unknown`, `codex_decision` enables only when every rival is `NotShown`, the recorded rival inventory equals the current one and `codex_bind` still picks the same pane and incarnation. Tests: truncated rival, rival added during the read, enlarged dialog with a matching rival. Task 18: `codex_transitions` revokes on `Bound(a)` to `Bound(b)`, `Unbound` or `T3`, and rechecks a live target when the rival inventory changes. Tests: session moves, cwd becomes shared, new rival shows the command, new rival does not. |
| N2. Unbounded retained work and starvation | Accept: bound admitted work end to end; keep permits until consumed; cancel superseded tasks; bounded batches with guaranteed timer and ownership service | Contract A1 to A7. Task 8: hold permits travel inside `Inbound::Held` and are released only when the adapter is done with the hold, so the control channel is a bounded channel of `2 × max_holds`. Task 15: reads start only with a permit and never wait for one (wanted flags retried on the tick), the permit travels with the result, superseded reads are aborted. Task 19: track cap, batched turns that serve the tick and T3 changes first, `HookStats`, and the stalled-lock, hold-churn takeover, Codex flood and track-cap tests. |
| N3. A skipped presence read invalidates an outstanding result | Accept | G3 to G5 rewritten: per-purpose `read_seq`, bumped only after admission, at most one outstanding read per purpose (Task 15 `ReadSlots`). The sequence rule is pinned by unit tests (`a_read_that_is_not_admitted_does_not_bump_the_sequence`, `reads_of_different_kinds_do_not_invalidate_each_other`). The integration test pins admission end to end: a hold-end read that finds no slot is retried and still switches to keys. Sol's exact interleaving (a presence attempt while the same occurrence's capture is outstanding) cannot occur in the revised flows, because presence reads start only after a capture matched; the per-purpose sequences make it harmless if a later change allows it. |
| N4. Delayed evidence of an older identical occurrence | Accept: no source invocation id, so overlapping identical occurrences are evidence-ambiguous; resolve conservatively | O7. Tasks 12, 14 and 16: unconsumed identities per session; a shadowed occurrence ignores identity evidence in `apply_evidence` and the keys hub, and a delivered shadowed answer resolves `uncertain` at its turn end. Tests: the R1/R2 delayed-outcome case and the turn-end reset. |
| N5. Retirement can use a pre-update scan and the wrong boundary | Accept: completion time after all writes; a complete scan that started after it; freshness after the lock; compare the full store | T1, T4, T5. Task 27: `updated_at_ms` is read after the last header write (gated test with `ACD_TEST_PAUSE_AT`). Task 9: scans carry wall-clock start and end. Task 26: `retire_if_done` takes the expected `Store`, reads the clock after the lock, and needs a scan that started at least `REGISTRY_GRACE_MS` after the update. Tests: pre-update scan, grace, lock-wait staleness (gated thread), same-generation header change. |
| N6. Journal recovery discards ownership; the batch overwrites a failed journal | Accept: never delete an unresolved journal; stop the batch; recover from installed entries or an explicit conflict | J1 to J4. Task 24: failures before the rename remove the journal. New Task 25: `begin` never overwrites, recovery rebuilds the record from the acd entries the file holds, an unreadable file is a reported conflict (`acd hooks status`; installer commands and rotation refuse), and the token rewrite stops at the first record failure. Tests: record failure followed by a Claude-style atomic rewrite, a two-destination batch failing on the first, an unreadable file during recovery. |
| N7. Recursive `hooks.lock` acquisition | Accept: split read-only loading from migration; explicit locked helpers; no path reacquires the lock | T6. Task 21: `writer_lock` module, read-only `load`, `migrate`/`ensure`/`rotate_store` with `*_locked` forms. Task 24 re-exports the lock and migrates under the held lock. Task 27's `rotate` no longer calls the locking `rotate_store` (revision 2 did, which would have self-deadlocked). Tests: thread-level 5 s timeouts for `ensure` and concurrent `migrate`, a subprocess timeout for `acd init`, and concurrent daemon and `acd init` migration (Task 30). |
| N8. Contradictory symlink test; failure-injection false positives | Accept: prechecks use the write path's safe reader; precheck refusal leaves the store unchanged; injection tests prove the stage was reached | Task 27: `precheck_claude` reads with `read_settings`; the symlink test now expects refusal with an unchanged store; the post-commit failure test injects `settings-write`. Every `ACD_TEST_FAIL_AT` test asserts `injected failure at <stage>` in stderr and sets its prerequisites explicitly (`--allow-unknown-git`, or no recorded destinations). |
| N9. Structured metadata under a 4 KiB read limit | Accept | F1. Task 21: `read_structured`/`write_structured` with a 256 KiB bound checked before writing; the store, records and journal use them. Tests: over-bound refusal leaves the old file, a record with 40 long destinations, a long-path journal. |

Decisions taken within the rulings, for the next review to check:

- **A7 instead of a timeout on every lock wait.** The reconciler waits
  for a delivery lock without a timeout when it changes state, because
  abandoning an ending or a revocation halfway would break O4 and the
  transitions table, and making every handler re-runnable would be a
  larger redesign. Liveness rests on L1: every holder releases within two
  herdr call deadlines (`CALL_TIMEOUT` is 3 s in
  `crates/acd-adapters/src/herdr/client.rs`), per holder and per wait,
  not per reconciler turn (round 3). Read results, which can be
  dropped safely, wait at most `lock_wait`. Task 19's stalled-lock test
  holds the lock for a bounded time and checks the bounds while it does.
- **O7 is conservative in two places.** A delivered shadowed answer is
  `uncertain` even at a clean turn end, and keys requests resolved by the
  hub leave an unconsumed entry until the turn ends. Both can only turn a
  correct attribution into `uncertain`, never into a wrong one.
- **N6 reconstruction.** When an uninstall journal (`record_after` is
  `None`) finds acd entries still present, the record is rebuilt with
  `created_file` and `created_hooks_key` false and no created events, so
  a later uninstall removes only acd's entries and never the file.
- **`REGISTRY_GRACE_MS` (5 s) is an assumption.** Gate 5 showed that
  Claude writes its registry entry at startup, but not how long after
  the process starts. (Corrected in round 3: an underestimated grace is
  not safe. A session whose entry appears after the deciding scan
  started is missed, and the previous token can retire early.) LIVE
  CHECK 14 is a release gate, including starts during rotation and under
  load.

Rejected: none.

and the core lock. The orchestrator accepted R3-1 to R3-5 as specified.
No tasks were renumbered.

| Finding | Ruling | Resolution |
|---|---|---|
| R3-1 (blocking). A live Codex target stays answerable while its rival inventory is being rechecked | Accept as specified | New C6. Task 17: `KeysTargets` keeps a validated rival inventory beside the immutable target, and the keys lease (right before the write, under the delivery lock) refuses with `RequestNotReady` and zero keys when the current inventory differs. Task 18: `codex_transitions` marks the request not ready under the delivery lock before scheduling the recheck; only a C3 `Enable` stores the new inventory and restores readiness. Tests: blocked recheck, recheck starved of read slots, and the lease before the reconciler notices (ticks paused). |
| R3-2. O7 compares question expectations instead of evidence identities | Accept as specified | O7 text and Task 12 `same_identity` (labels excluded); `mark_shadow` uses it. `observe` returns question evidence only when the answers map has the expected question with a string answer.
